# Phases Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Let a creator with a deployed collection run its phases from Manage drop, Phases, on the contract's state: the server reads `collectionState()` on each page load and after every confirmed change, joins each group with what Cobweb keeps in `mint_groups` (name, description, visibility, order) and refreshes its copies of price, allocation and start from that read; status comes only from the contract's `active`. Every change on chain (Add phase, Open now, Close, Schedule, Clear schedule, Edit price, Edit max supply, Edit per-wallet limit, Close all) is built in one place, simulated and sent from the sign-in wallet by one manage machine, and confirmed by the server from the receipt, which reads the chain again; names, visibility and order need no transaction. The Overview shows the real minted total and live phases, and the deployed view keeps showing the launch phases as the constructor took them.

**Architecture:** `packages/shared` ships the collection's ABI alone as `@cobweb/shared/collection-abi` (generated by the existing copy script), so Manage drop's browser code never loads the 20 KB creation code. A migration gives `mint_groups` its off-chain columns (description, visibility, position) with their checks, narrows the owner's grant to those four columns, and adds three functions: `mint_group_record` (the service role writes a row only after a receipt or a chain read proves the group), `mint_groups_refresh` (the service role copies price, allocation, start and the public limit from a server read, never from an older block than the last) and `mint_group_move` (the owner swaps two positions). The web app gains pure modules for the calls (`phase-calls.ts`), the view (`phases-view.ts`), the receipt match (`phase-events.ts`) and the input shapes (`phase-schemas.ts`); the server cores (`phases-core.ts`: the chain read, the view, the confirm, the off-chain edits) behind `phasesWrite` and six server actions; `verify:phases` on a deploy fixture shared with `verify:deploy`; a pure manage machine (the deploy machine without prepare) with the collection's custom errors decoded into sentences; and the screens: one `TransactionDialog` on the machine for every tab, Phases and the Overview on the real view, Add phase's pending store with "Check again", and sample mode on the same code through `sampleManageActions` and `samplePhasesServer`.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 6 strict (ESM, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `verbatimModuleSyntax`) consumed as source; Node 26 locally; Next 16.3 (App Router, Turbopack) with React 19; wagmi 2.19 and viem 2.57 (RainbowKit for the wallet); zod 4.6; Vitest 5 (Node environment, no component tests); Supabase local (CLI 2.116) with Postgres; Anvil through `chain:local`; Prettier 3; `@cobweb/shared` consumed as workspace source and transpiled by Next.

**Spec:** [../specs/2026-10-15-phases.md](../specs/2026-10-15-phases.md)

## Global Constraints

- The spec is the binding authority. Where this plan decides a detail the spec leaves open, the line starts with "Decision:" and the decisions that shape more than one task are collected in the next section.
- Node 26 locally. ESM everywhere. Inside `packages/shared`, relative imports carry the `.ts` extension (`./chains.ts`). Inside `apps/web/src`, imports are extensionless and use the `@/` alias, as the existing files do. Inside `apps/web/scripts`, relative imports carry the `.ts` extension and the app's modules are imported as `../src/...` (the scripts run under `--import ./scripts/resolve-ts.mjs`, which maps `@/` and adds `.ts`; a script never imports a value from a `.tsx` module). Types are imported with `import type` or an inline `type` specifier.
- Vitest 5, Node environment. One file: `pnpm --filter <package> exec vitest run <file>`, for example `pnpm --filter @cobweb/web exec vitest run src/components/manage/manage-machine.test.ts`. No component test exists and none is added: pure modules are unit tested, components are checked by typecheck, lint, build and the browser walk.
- Test fixtures: annotate every fixture constant with its type (`const HASH: Hex = ...`, `const view: PhasesView = ...`, `const row: PhaseRowCopy = ...`), since an unannotated literal widens and fails `tsc`. Never await a sleeping sample action under fake timers without advancing them (`vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync`); attach a `rejects` expectation before advancing the timers that settle it.
- Prettier: no semicolons, single quotes, trailing commas, 110 columns. Before each commit run `pnpm exec prettier --write <the files you changed>` from the repo root. Never run `pnpm format` or Prettier on `.`. CI runs `pnpm format:check`, so every file you touch outside `docs/` must be formatted (`docs/`, `contracts/out/` and `packages/ui/src/components/` are in `.prettierignore`). The generated `packages/shared/src/collection.ts`, `collection-abi.ts` and `factory-abi.ts` are formatted by the copy script itself. The code blocks in this plan are already formatted; Prettier leaves them as they are.
- typescript-eslint's recommended rules and ESLint's `preserve-caught-error` apply. Do not write `throw new X(...)` directly inside a `catch` block. There is no react-hooks lint plugin; write hook dependencies correctly all the same.
- Copy: plain sentences. No em or en dashes anywhere, code, comments, commit messages and docs included. The product is Cobweb; never Kingdomly.
- CLAUDE.md rules 1 to 4 bind this sub-project. Liveness comes only from the contract's `active` field (rule 1): no browser clock, no event, no copy ever makes a phase live. The wallet signs exactly the request the simulation passed (rule 2): `simulate` returns the only way to send. Every server action checks the session and the ownership (rule 4) through `phasesWrite` (or `deployWrite`). The server writes the on-chain fields (`price_wei`, `max_supply`, `start_at`, `public_max_per_wallet`) only from its own chain read, never from a browser value, and records an added phase only after the receipt proves its registration.
- Security: no env name starts with `NEXT_PUBLIC_` except the ones that already do. No secret value is printed, logged or committed. An error is logged through `errorSummary` (name and one line) or as its code and message, never as the error object.
- Never deploy to any remote chain or service. Never pass `--prod` or `--target production` to any command. The only chain any step touches is the local Anvil on port 8545; every script refuses an endpoint that is not localhost.
- `docs/PLAN.md` must not change. `legacy/` is never read into, copied from, imported or committed.
- Docs keep their history: add dated notes, decisions and status sentences; never rewrite or delete earlier statements in `docs/MASTER_PLAN.md`, in specs or in `contracts/README.md`.
- Local services, for Tasks 2, 4, 6, 8 and 9: `supabase start` at the repo root (the migrations up to `20261014100000_deploy.sql` are applied; Task 2 applies the new one with `supabase migration up`, which keeps the local data), `supabase seed buckets` (creates the local `art` and `render` buckets; a restart or a reset alone does not), and `pnpm --filter @cobweb/web chain:local` running in the background on port 8545 with its two printed lines (`RPC_URL_31337` and `FACTORY_ADDRESS_31337`) in `apps/web/.env.local`. The local factory approved the shipped creation code at its deploy. Task 1 reads the Foundry build in `contracts/out` (present from earlier builds; if `contracts/out/CobwebCollection.sol/CobwebCollection.json` is missing, run `forge build` in `contracts/` first). Nothing else needs Foundry.
- `verify:local` totals drift as sections are added: this plan states a count as "the previous total plus N", N being the number of `check(` calls a step adds.
- Implementers never use `git commit --amend` or `git stash`. `git rm` and `git mv` are used only where a step says so. To fix a commit, make a new one.
- Before every commit, at the repo root: `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test`, after `pnpm exec prettier --write <the files you changed>`.
- Commit messages are one plain imperative sentence with no prefix, then a blank line, and end with exactly these two lines:

```
Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_012fcPR3AxkRXdB2iBkge8S6
```

## Decisions

1. Decision (spec section 5): `copy-abi.ts` writes `collection-abi.ts` (the ABI alone, with its own header) and `collection.ts` (the creation code, its hash and `export { collectionAbi } from './collection-abi.ts'`), so both entries export the same object; `factory-abi.ts` comes out unchanged. Every new web module imports `@cobweb/shared/collection-abi`; the deploy's imports stay as they are.
2. Decision (section 4.1): the position trigger is `public.mint_groups_set_position()` (the other trigger functions live in `public`), before insert, `coalesce(max(position) + 1, 0)`. The backfill sets `position = onchain_id` before the name and position indexes are created. The table comment names the columns each writer owns.
3. Decision (section 4.3): `mint_group_record` inserts with `on conflict (collection_id, onchain_id) do update` of the name, description and visibility, so a row already there (named before, or left by a reorg) keeps its position. `mint_groups_refresh` locks the `deployments` row after the collection, writes a copy only when it differs (`is distinct from`, so `updated_at` moves only on a real change), reads a `start` of 0 or none as null, and accepts the same block again (only an older block answers false). `mint_group_move` locks the row and its neighbour `for update` (the owner's column grant allows it) and is executable by `authenticated` only (revoked from `public`, `anon` and `service_role`, as `collection_placeholder_ready`). The migration was checked statement by statement in a transaction rolled back on the local database.
4. Decision (section 6): the shared types live in `lib/manage/phases-types.ts` (`ChainGroup`, `ChainState`, `StateReader`, `PhasesView`, `PhaseAdd`, `PhaseTxExpected`, `PhaseRowCopy`), so the browser imports them without `phases-core.ts`, which imports `@cobweb/shared/server`. `PhasesContext` stays in `phases-core.ts`.
5. Decision (section 6.2): `phases-view.ts` also holds `liveView` (the reachable view), `refreshPayload` (what a read copies, or null when an id, an allocation or the public limit does not fit an integer column: then that whole read is not copied, section 12), `moveGroup` (the screen's local reorder among named phases) and `INT_MAX`. A row parked at a null position sorts after the others.
6. Decision (sections 6.3 and 7.1): `ExpectedEvent` is `{ eventName, args }` with the ABI's argument names. `matchPhaseEvent` checks `to`, then `from`, then the logs the collection emitted: viem decodes any event of the ABI by its topic, so the decoded name is compared, then each wanted argument. Close all wants only its `status`.
7. Decision (section 6.4): the zod shapes live in `lib/manage/phase-schemas.ts`. `parseSentCall` answers UNSAVED for any malformed call (zod's own messages are never shown); each uint is one `refine` (zod 4 runs every check of a string, so a separate regex would not stop `BigInt('1.5')`); the name and the description use the screen's sentences, which `phase-rules.ts` now exports (`PHASE_NAME_EMPTY`, `PHASE_NAME_LONG`) beside `PHASE_NAME_TAKEN` from `result.ts`. Naming a group without a row reads the chain first; when the chain does not answer, the new sentence `CONTRACT_UNREADABLE` ('The contract could not be read. Try again in a minute.') is the answer.
8. Decision (section 6.4): every `42501` reads as NOT_FOUND; every Phases core starts from the deployment and refuses NOT_DEPLOYED without one; visibility refuses group 0 whatever the value; the actions call no `revalidatePath` (the Manage pages read on every load, and a refresh would render Phases again under its own state).
9. Decision (sections 6.4 and 7.4): the off-chain edits answer no view. The screen applies a rename and a visibility change after the server's answer, a move at once (moved back on a refusal), and reads again after naming a group that had no row (its position is new).
10. Decision (section 7.2): `ManageInput.call` and `ManageDeps.simulate` take `ManageCall | null`: null is a tab still on sample data, whose review the sample actions check, and "Check again", which never simulates. `ManageInput.addId` gives the PHASE_NAME_TAKEN sentence its group id. A failure before any step (no account, another account) reads at `preparing` in the deploy reducer, which the manage steps do not list, so only its alert shows. A terminal refusal reads "Cobweb could not confirm this change"; an unconfirmed one "Change not confirmed yet".
11. Decision (section 7.3): `TxRequest` moves to `manage-actions.ts`, its `check` kept for the tabs still on sample data; `ManageActions` gains the sample-only `review?(review)`. The context value is `{ actions, chainId, wallet }` (`manage-context.tsx`); without a provider, `useManageActions` answers one shared sample target (Base, the sample creator), so the Whitelist, Airdrop, Settings and Overview dialogs need no provider. With sample actions the dialog treats the sign-in wallet as the connected account on the collection's chain. "Try again" after a change sent but not confirmed checks its hash again ("Check again"), never sends again.
12. Decision (section 7.4): the read line ("Read from the chain <relative>" with "Read again") sits under the page heading, above the supply strip; when the chain cannot be read, the alert replaces it. Unreachable rows read "Not read" for status and minted; the supply strip's edit, every on-chain button and menu item, Add phase and Close all are off with the reason "The contract could not be read, so changes on chain wait until it can be."; naming, hiding and moving stay on.
13. Decision (section 7.4): Add phase's request replaces the plain confirm with `server.record(hash, add)` and carries `pending`; the screen keeps the pending add in localStorage and in state. The pending panel shows while an add is remembered and the Add dialog is not open; a terminal refusal there (which forgets the add and closes the panel) goes to a toast. Edit details for a group without a row is titled "Name group <id>" and starts with an empty name.
14. Decision (section 7.5): `launchPhasesOf(args, rows)` lives in `deploy-args.ts`; `readDeployment` reads `args` and no longer `public_max_per_wallet` (now the copy). The Overview's deployment is `DEMO_DEPLOYMENT` with the record's chain, contract, transaction, deployer and deploy time (the pointer, fees, royalty and payout stay sample). A refused read on either page shows `ManageUnavailable` with "Try again" (`router.refresh()`).
15. Decision (section 8): one `sent` map is shared by `sampleManageActions` and `samplePhasesServer`, so a sample confirm applies exactly the call the simulated wallet sent; every sample server call takes 900 ms; a sample tab's failing check throws "The contract would reject this with <name>."; `?pending=add` shows an add named "Holders" waiting at the next id.
16. Decision (section 10): `verify:phases` has twenty checks (Task 6). `deploy-fixture.ts` exports the settings, the clients, `check` and `runChecks`, `signIn`, `seededCollection`, the two contexts, `pay`, `finishRender`, `deploy`, `collectionRow` (verify-deploy's `collectionState`, renamed so it is not mistaken for the contract's view), `renderCollection`, `deployedCollection` and `removeFixtures`; `verify:deploy` keeps its fifteen checks under the same names.
17. Decision (section 10): `verify:local` section 9k (`artPhases`, after `artDeploy`, fixture tag 151, record salt 11) adds sixteen checks; the owner-create check becomes a refusal with the service role seeding groups A and B, the deployed collection's `info` line becomes one check, and 9j's replacement check seeds its stale row with the service role. The total grows by seventeen.
18. Decision (section 4): `apps/web/src/lib/database.types.ts` is edited by hand as the generator writes it (alphabetical keys, numeric as `number`, `SetofOptions` for `mint_group_record`), in the same commit as the migration.

## Review Focus

Inputs and failure modes the spec implies but its own test list would not exercise, most likely to bite a creator first. Each is pinned to a test in the task that owns the code.

1. A price far above 2^53 wei (one million MON on Monad is 10^24 wei, which the price input allows) must reach the confirm's expected event unrounded: a rounded price would refuse the creator's own change as NOT_THIS_CHANGE and the machine would forget it. Pinned by "keeps every wei of a price above 2^53 through sentOf, callOf and the expected event (Review Focus 1)" (Task 3, `phase-calls.test.ts`).
2. The session wallet is stored lowercase while receipts carry checksummed addresses: a comparison that minds the case would refuse every confirm as NOT_FROM_WALLET (or NOT_THIS_CONTRACT). Pinned by "matches the contract and the sign-in wallet whatever their case (Review Focus 2)" (Task 3, `phase-events.test.ts`).
3. Close all's `AllMintGroupsStatusChanged` carries the number of groups it closed, which grows when a group is registered outside Cobweb: an expected event that named the count would refuse a successful Close all. Pinned by "matches Close all whatever number of groups it closed (Review Focus 3)" (Task 3, `phase-events.test.ts`).
4. A name typed with outer spaces: the database's check (`name = btrim(name)`) would refuse it as UNSAVED unless the server trims before writing, and 40 characters inside spaces must still pass. Pinned by "trims a name with outer spaces before its checks, so 40 characters inside spaces pass and the database gets the trimmed name (Review Focus 4)" (Task 3, `phase-schemas.test.ts`).
5. An id or a limit above 2,147,483,647 set outside Cobweb: a refresh would fail on the integer columns at every read; the view must still render from the chain while the copy keeps its last read. Pinned by "copies nothing when an id, an allocation or the public limit is above 2,147,483,647 (Review Focus 5)" (Task 3, `phases-view.test.ts`).

### Task 1: The ABI-only collection entry in the shared package

**Files:**

- Modify: `packages/shared/scripts/copy-abi.ts` (whole file)
- Modify: `packages/shared/package.json` (`exports`: one line)
- Create (generated by the script): `packages/shared/src/collection-abi.ts`
- Modify (generated by the script): `packages/shared/src/collection.ts` (the ABI moves out; a re-export takes its place)
- Modify: `packages/shared/src/collection.test.ts` (whole file)
- Modify: `contracts/README.md` (one paragraph added after the paragraph that starts "**The artifact the web app ships.**")
- Test: `packages/shared/src/collection.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: the Foundry artifacts `contracts/out/CobwebCollection.sol/CobwebCollection.json` and `contracts/out/CobwebFactory.sol/CobwebFactory.json`.
- Produces:
  - `@cobweb/shared/collection-abi`: `export const collectionAbi = [...] as const` (the collection's whole ABI, 144 entries).
  - `@cobweb/shared/collection`: `COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE: Hex`, `COLLECTION_CODE_HASH: Hex` and `collectionAbi`, re-exported from `./collection-abi.ts` (the same object).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Replace the whole content of `packages/shared/src/collection.test.ts` with:

```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'
import { getAbiItem, keccak256, toEventSelector, toFunctionSelector } from 'viem'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { collectionAbi as abiOnly } from './collection-abi.ts'
import { COLLECTION_CODE_HASH, COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE, collectionAbi } from './collection.ts'

const artifact = resolve(
  dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
  '../../../contracts/out/CobwebCollection.sol/CobwebCollection.json',
)
/** The limit CI has watched since Phase 1 (docs/MASTER_PLAN.md, Phase 1: creation code under 24 KB). */
const MAX_CREATION_CODE_BYTES = 24_576

describe('the shipped collection artifact', () => {
  it('ships a hash that is the keccak256 of the shipped creation code', () => {
    expect(COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE).toMatch(/^0x[0-9a-f]+$/)
    expect(COLLECTION_CODE_HASH).toBe(keccak256(COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE))
  })

  it('ships creation code under 24,576 bytes', () => {
    const bytes = (COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE.length - 2) / 2
    expect(Number.isInteger(bytes)).toBe(true)
    expect(bytes).toBeGreaterThan(10_000)
    expect(bytes).toBeLessThan(MAX_CREATION_CODE_BYTES)
  })

  it('carries the constructor and the views the deploy checks read', () => {
    const constructor = collectionAbi.find((entry) => entry.type === 'constructor')
    expect(
      constructor && 'inputs' in constructor ? constructor.inputs.map((input) => input.type) : [],
    ).toEqual(['tuple', 'uint256[]', 'uint256[]', 'uint256', 'uint96', 'address', 'address'])
    for (const [name, signature] of [
      ['name', 'name()'],
      ['symbol', 'symbol()'],
      ['maxSupply', 'maxSupply()'],
      ['baseURI', 'baseURI()'],
      ['owner', 'owner()'],
      ['royaltyInfo', 'royaltyInfo(uint256,uint256)'],
      ['collectionState', 'collectionState()'],
      ['publicMaxMintPerWallet', 'publicMaxMintPerWallet()'],
      ['ownerPayoutAddress', 'ownerPayoutAddress()'],
      ['fees', 'fees()'],
    ] as const) {
      expect(toFunctionSelector(getAbiItem({ abi: collectionAbi, name }))).toBe(toFunctionSelector(signature))
    }
  })

  if (existsSync(artifact)) {
    it('ships the bytecode of the Foundry build in contracts/out', () => {
      const built = JSON.parse(readFileSync(artifact, 'utf8')) as { bytecode: { object: string } }
      expect(COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE).toBe(built.bytecode.object)
      expect(COLLECTION_CODE_HASH).toBe(keccak256(built.bytecode.object as `0x${string}`))
    })
  } else {
    it.skip('ships the bytecode of the Foundry build in contracts/out (run forge build in contracts/ to check it)', () => {})
  }
})

/** `name(type,type)`, from an ABI item. */
const signatureOf = (item: { name: string; inputs: readonly { type: string }[] }) =>
  `${item.name}(${item.inputs.map((input) => input.type).join(',')})`
/** The argument names of an ABI item, in order. */
const namesOf = (item: { inputs: readonly { name?: string }[] }) => item.inputs.map((input) => input.name)

describe('the ABI-only entry and what Phases relies on (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 5)', () => {
  it('exports the same ABI from both entries', () => {
    expect(abiOnly).toBe(collectionAbi)
    expect(abiOnly.length).toBeGreaterThan(100)
  })

  it('carries the calls Manage drop sends, with their selectors and argument names (the price first in changeSalePrice)', () => {
    for (const [name, signature, inputs] of [
      [
        'registerMintGroup',
        'registerMintGroup(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256)',
        ['mintGroup', 'price', 'maxSupply_', 'startTimestamp'],
      ],
      ['setMintGroupStatus', 'setMintGroupStatus(uint256,bool)', ['mintGroup', 'status']],
      ['scheduleMintGroupStart', 'scheduleMintGroupStart(uint256,uint256)', ['mintGroup', 'startTimestamp']],
      ['changeSalePrice', 'changeSalePrice(uint256,uint256)', ['newMintPrice', 'mintGroup']],
      ['setMintGroupMaxSupply', 'setMintGroupMaxSupply(uint256,uint256)', ['mintGroup', 'newMaxSupply']],
      ['setPublicMaxMintPerWallet', 'setPublicMaxMintPerWallet(uint256)', ['newPublicMaxMintPerWallet']],
      ['stopOrStartAllMintGroups', 'stopOrStartAllMintGroups(bool)', ['status']],
      ['mintGroups', 'mintGroups()', []],
    ] as const) {
      const item = getAbiItem({ abi: abiOnly, name })
      expect(toFunctionSelector(item)).toBe(toFunctionSelector(signature))
      expect(namesOf(item)).toEqual(inputs)
    }
  })

  it('carries the events the confirm matches', () => {
    for (const [name, signature] of [
      ['MintGroupRegistered', 'MintGroupRegistered(uint256)'],
      ['MintGroupStatusChanged', 'MintGroupStatusChanged(uint256,bool)'],
      ['AllMintGroupsStatusChanged', 'AllMintGroupsStatusChanged(bool,uint256)'],
      ['MintGroupStartTimestampChanged', 'MintGroupStartTimestampChanged(uint256,uint256)'],
      ['SalePriceChanged', 'SalePriceChanged(uint256,uint256)'],
      ['MintGroupMaxSupplyUpdated', 'MintGroupMaxSupplyUpdated(uint256,uint256)'],
      ['PublicMaxMintPerWalletChanged', 'PublicMaxMintPerWalletChanged(uint256)'],
    ] as const) {
      expect(toEventSelector(getAbiItem({ abi: abiOnly, name }))).toBe(toEventSelector(signature))
    }
  })

  it('carries the errors the manage sentences decode', () => {
    for (const [name, signature] of [
      ['BelowMinted', 'BelowMinted(uint256)'],
      ['AllocationsExceedMaxSupply', 'AllocationsExceedMaxSupply()'],
      ['MintGroupAlreadyExists', 'MintGroupAlreadyExists(uint256)'],
      ['MintGroupDoesNotExist', 'MintGroupDoesNotExist(uint256)'],
      ['AddressZero', 'AddressZero()'],
      ['Unauthorized', 'Unauthorized(address)'],
      ['OwnableUnauthorizedAccount', 'OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address)'],
    ] as const) {
      expect(signatureOf(getAbiItem({ abi: abiOnly, name }))).toBe(signature)
    }
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The copy script and the export**

Replace the whole content of `packages/shared/scripts/copy-abi.ts` with:

```ts
// Copies the factory ABI entries the app uses from the Foundry build into src/factory-abi.ts, the
// collection's ABI alone into src/collection-abi.ts, and the collection's creation code and its keccak256
// hash into src/collection.ts (which re-exports the ABI), so the app's ABIs and the bytes it deploys always
// come from contracts/out (never from legacy/). Run `forge build` in contracts/ first.
//
//   pnpm --filter @cobweb/shared abi
//
// The hash printed at the end is the one the factory admin approves with approveCreationCode; the
// local factory (chain:local) approves keccak256(type(CobwebCollection).creationCode) of the same
// build, which is the same bytes (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 5). Manage drop imports
// the ABI-only entry, so the dashboard never loads the 20 KB creation code
// (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 5).

import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process'
import { existsSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'
import { keccak256, type Hex } from 'viem'

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url))
const repoRoot = resolve(here, '../../..')
const factoryArtifact = resolve(repoRoot, 'contracts/out/CobwebFactory.sol/CobwebFactory.json')
const collectionArtifact = resolve(repoRoot, 'contracts/out/CobwebCollection.sol/CobwebCollection.json')
const factoryOut = resolve(here, '../src/factory-abi.ts')
const collectionAbiOut = resolve(here, '../src/collection-abi.ts')
const collectionOut = resolve(here, '../src/collection.ts')
// The five entries the pay flow uses, then the five the deploy flow uses.
const WANTED = [
  'payForArt',
  'artFeePerToken',
  'jobPayment',
  'treasury',
  'ArtPaid',
  'deployCollection',
  'predictCollectionAddress',
  'collectionFees',
  'isCollection',
  'CollectionDeployed',
]

type AbiEntry = { type: string; name?: string }
type Artifact = { abi: AbiEntry[]; bytecode: { object: string } }

function readArtifact(path: string): Artifact {
  if (!existsSync(path)) throw new Error(`${path} is missing. Run forge build in contracts/ first.`)
  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, 'utf8')) as Artifact
}

const HEADER = (artifact: string) => [
  `// Generated by \`pnpm --filter @cobweb/shared abi\` from contracts/out/${artifact}.`,
  '// Do not edit by hand: change the contract, run forge build, then run the script again.',
]

const factory = readArtifact(factoryArtifact)
const entries = WANTED.map((name) => {
  const found = factory.abi.find(
    (entry) => entry.name === name && (entry.type === 'function' || entry.type === 'event'),
  )
  if (!found) throw new Error(`${name} is not in the factory's ABI. Run forge build in contracts/ first.`)
  return found
})
writeFileSync(
  factoryOut,
  [
    ...HEADER('CobwebFactory.sol/CobwebFactory.json'),
    `export const factoryAbi = ${JSON.stringify(entries, null, 2)} as const`,
    '',
  ].join('\n'),
)

const collection = readArtifact(collectionArtifact)
const code = collection.bytecode.object
if (!/^0x[0-9a-f]+$/.test(code)) throw new Error('The collection bytecode in the artifact is not hex.')
const hash = keccak256(code as Hex)
writeFileSync(
  collectionAbiOut,
  [
    ...HEADER('CobwebCollection.sol/CobwebCollection.json'),
    '',
    '/**',
    " * The collection's ABI alone, without the creation code: the entry Manage drop's browser code imports",
    ' * (`@cobweb/shared/collection-abi`), so the dashboard never loads the 20 KB of bytecode.',
    ' */',
    `export const collectionAbi = ${JSON.stringify(collection.abi, null, 2)} as const`,
    '',
  ].join('\n'),
)
writeFileSync(
  collectionOut,
  [
    ...HEADER('CobwebCollection.sol/CobwebCollection.json'),
    "import type { Hex } from 'viem'",
    '',
    '/**',
    " * The collection's creation code without constructor arguments: the bytes `deployCollection` takes,",
    ' * whose hash the factory admin approves. About 20 KB, so only the deploy screen and the confirming',
    ' * core import this entry.',
    ' */',
    `export const COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE: Hex = '${code}'`,
    '',
    '/** keccak256 of the creation code: what the factory checks against its approved hashes. */',
    `export const COLLECTION_CODE_HASH: Hex = '${hash}'`,
    '',
    "/** The ABI, from the ABI-only entry, so the deploy's imports stay as they were. */",
    "export { collectionAbi } from './collection-abi.ts'",
    '',
  ].join('\n'),
)
execFileSync('pnpm', ['exec', 'prettier', '--write', factoryOut, collectionAbiOut, collectionOut], {
  cwd: repoRoot,
  stdio: 'ignore',
})
console.log(`Wrote ${entries.length} entries to packages/shared/src/factory-abi.ts`)
console.log(`Wrote packages/shared/src/collection-abi.ts: ${collection.abi.length} ABI entries`)
console.log(
  `Wrote packages/shared/src/collection.ts: ${(code.length - 2) / 2} bytes of creation code and the ABI re-exported`,
)
console.log(`Collection creation code hash: ${hash}`)
```

```json
  },
```

with

```json
  },
```

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `private.lock_owned_collection(p_collection_id uuid, p_owner uuid)` (`20261009020000_lock_and_pay.sql`), `public.collection_record_deploy` and `public.deployments` (`20261014100000_deploy.sql`); in verify-local: `renderedFixture(A, payer, tag)`, `recordArgs(owner, cid, payer, n, overrides)`, `callArt`, `expectError`, `expectSentence`, `expectNothing`, `countRows`, `insertCollection`, `describe`, `admin`, `anon`, `CHECK`, `UNIQUE`, `PERMISSION` (all already in the file).

6. Immediately before the separator line `// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------` that precedes `// 10. Cleanup`, insert this block, followed by one blank line:

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 9k. Phases: the off-chain columns of mint_groups and their checks, the owner's column grant, the
// positions, mint_group_move, mint_group_record and mint_groups_refresh
// (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 4)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
type PhaseCheckRow = {
  onchain_id: number
  name: string
  description: string
  visible: boolean
  position: number | null
  price_wei: string
  max_supply: number
  start_at: string | null
}

async function phaseRows(cid: string): Promise<PhaseCheckRow[]> {
  const { data, error } = await admin
    .from('mint_groups')
    .select('onchain_id, name, description, visible, position, price_wei::text, max_supply, start_at')
    .eq('collection_id', cid)
    .order('onchain_id')
  if (error) throw new Error(`phases: ${describe(error as DbError)}`)
  return data as unknown as PhaseCheckRow[]
}

type CopyState = { public_max_per_wallet: number; state_block: number | null; state_read_at: string | null }

async function copyState(cid: string): Promise<CopyState> {
  const { data, error } = await admin
    .from('deployments')
    .select('public_max_per_wallet, state_block, state_read_at')
    .eq('collection_id', cid)
    .single()
  if (error) throw new Error(`copy state: ${describe(error as DbError)}`)
  return data as CopyState
}

const NOT_DEPLOYED_SENTENCE = 'The contract is not deployed yet.'
/** 1 November 2026 00:00 UTC plus the block number in seconds: each test block has its own time. */
const blockTime = (block: number) => new Date((1_793_491_200 + block) * 1000).toISOString()

const phaseRecordArgs = (
  owner: string,
  cid: string,
  id: number,
  name: string,
  description = '',
  visible = true,
) => ({
  p_collection_id: cid,
  p_owner: owner,
  p_onchain_id: id,
  p_name: name,
  p_description: description,
  p_visible: visible,
})

const refreshArgs = (
  owner: string,
  cid: string,
  block: number,
  overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {},
): Record<string, unknown> => ({
  p_collection_id: cid,
  p_owner: owner,
  p_block: block,
  p_block_time: blockTime(block),
  p_public_max_per_wallet: 3,
  p_groups: [
    { onchain_id: 0, price_wei: '0', max_supply: 2, start: 0 },
    { onchain_id: 1, price_wei: '1000000000000000', max_supply: 1, start: 0 },
  ],
  ...overrides,
})

async function artPhases(A: Creator, B: Creator): Promise<void> {
  const payer = A.account.address.toLowerCase()
  // Rendered and recorded as deployed with Public (0) and Holders (1), as collection_record_deploy writes them.
  const { cid } = await renderedFixture(A, payer, 151)
  const recorded = await callArt(admin, 'collection_record_deploy', recordArgs(A.userId, cid, payer, 11))
  if (recorded.error) throw new Error(`setup: record: ${describe(recorded.error)}`)
  // A draft without a deployment: the record functions refuse it.
  const draft = await insertCollection(A.client)
  if (!draft.row) throw new Error(`setup: ${describe(draft.error)}`)
  const draftId = draft.row.id
  const update = (onchainId: number, patch: Record<string, unknown>) =>
    A.client.from('mint_groups').update(patch).eq('collection_id', cid).eq('onchain_id', onchainId).select()
  const move = (client: SupabaseClient, onchainId: number, up: boolean) =>
    callArt(client, 'mint_group_move', { p_collection_id: cid, p_onchain_id: onchainId, p_up: up })
  const positions = async () =>
    (await phaseRows(cid)).map((row) => `${row.onchain_id}:${row.position}`).join(' ')

  await check(
    'mint_groups: the launch phases read positions 0 and 1, an empty description and shown',
    async () => {
      const rows = await phaseRows(cid)
      const got = rows.map((row) => [row.onchain_id, row.position, row.description, row.visible])
      return JSON.stringify(got) ===
        JSON.stringify([
          [0, 0, '', true],
          [1, 1, '', true],
        ])
        ? null
        : JSON.stringify(rows)
    },
  )

  await check(
    "mint_groups: a name of 41 characters, a name with outer spaces and another phase's name in other case are refused",
    async () => {
      const problems: string[] = []
      for (const [label, name, codes] of [
        ['41 characters', 'x'.repeat(41), CHECK],
        ['outer spaces', ' Holders ', CHECK],
        ["another phase's name in other case", 'PUBLIC', UNIQUE],
      ] as const) {
        const { data, error } = await update(1, { name })
        const problem = expectError(error as DbError, codes, data)
        if (problem) problems.push(`${label}: ${problem}`)
      }
      if (problems.length) return problems.join('; ')
      return (await phaseRows(cid))[1]?.name === 'Holders' ? null : 'the name changed'
    },
  )

  await check('mint_groups: a description of 501 characters is refused; 500 is kept', async () => {
    const long = await update(1, { description: 'd'.repeat(501) })
    const refused = expectError(long.error as DbError, CHECK, long.data)
    if (refused) return `501: ${refused}`
    const fits = await update(1, { description: 'd'.repeat(500) })
    if (fits.error) return `500: ${describe(fits.error as DbError)}`
    const kept = (await phaseRows(cid))[1]?.description.length
    const reset = await update(1, { description: '' })
    if (reset.error) return `reset: ${describe(reset.error as DbError)}`
    return kept === 500 ? null : `kept ${kept} characters`
  })

  await check('mint_groups: the public sale cannot be hidden; another phase can', async () => {
    const publicSale = await update(0, { visible: false })
    const refused = expectSentence(publicSale.error as DbError, CHECK, 'mint_groups_public_shown')
    if (refused) return `public sale: ${refused}`
    const other = await update(1, { visible: false })
    if (other.error) return `holders: ${describe(other.error as DbError)}`
    const rows = await phaseRows(cid)
    const back = await update(1, { visible: true })
    if (back.error) return `back: ${describe(back.error as DbError)}`
    return rows[0]?.visible === true && rows[1]?.visible === false ? null : JSON.stringify(rows)
  })

  await check('mint_groups: the owner updates the name, description, visibility and position', async () => {
    const changed = await update(1, {
      name: 'Holders club',
      description: 'For holders.',
      visible: false,
      position: 5,
    })
    if (changed.error) return describe(changed.error as DbError)
    const row = (await phaseRows(cid))[1]
    const back = await update(1, { name: 'Holders', description: '', visible: true, position: 1 })
    if (back.error) return `back: ${describe(back.error as DbError)}`
    return row?.name === 'Holders club' &&
      row.description === 'For holders.' &&
      row.visible === false &&
      row.position === 5
      ? null
      : JSON.stringify(row)
  })

  await check(
    'mint_groups: the owner is refused the copies, onchain_id, an insert and a delete',
    async () => {
      const problems: string[] = []
      for (const patch of [
        { price_wei: '1' },
        { max_supply: 0 },
        { start_at: new Date().toISOString() },
        { onchain_id: 5 },
      ]) {
        const { data, error } = await update(1, patch)
        const problem = expectError(error as DbError, PERMISSION, data)
        if (problem) problems.push(`${Object.keys(patch)[0]}: ${problem}`)
      }
      const insert = await A.client
        .from('mint_groups')
        .insert({ collection_id: cid, onchain_id: 5, name: 'Sneaky' })
        .select()
      const inserted = expectError(insert.error as DbError, PERMISSION, insert.data)
      if (inserted) problems.push(`insert: ${inserted}`)
      const removed = await A.client.from('mint_groups').delete().eq('collection_id', cid).select()
      const deleted = expectError(removed.error as DbError, PERMISSION, removed.data)
      if (deleted) problems.push(`delete: ${deleted}`)
      if (problems.length) return problems.join('; ')
      const rows = await phaseRows(cid)
      return rows.length === 2 && rows[1]?.price_wei === '1000000000000000' && rows[1]?.max_supply === 1
        ? null
        : JSON.stringify(rows)
    },
  )

  await check("mint_groups: B cannot update A's phases", async () => {
    const { data, error } = await B.client
      .from('mint_groups')
      .update({ name: 'Hijacked' })
      .eq('collection_id', cid)
      .select()
    const problem = expectNothing(error as DbError, data)
    if (problem) return problem
    return (await phaseRows(cid)).every((row) => row.name !== 'Hijacked') ? null : 'a name changed'
  })

  await check(
    'mint_group_move: moves a phase up and down by swapping positions; an end row stays',
    async () => {
      const up = await move(A.client, 1, true)
      if (up.error) return `up: ${describe(up.error)}`
      const afterUp = await positions()
      const down = await move(A.client, 1, false)
      if (down.error) return `down: ${describe(down.error)}`
      const afterDown = await positions()
      const top = await move(A.client, 0, true)
      const bottom = await move(A.client, 1, false)
      if (top.error || bottom.error) return `end rows: ${describe(top.error ?? bottom.error)}`
      const afterEnds = await positions()
      return afterUp === '0:1 1:0' && afterDown === '0:0 1:1' && afterEnds === '0:0 1:1'
        ? null
        : `${afterUp} | ${afterDown} | ${afterEnds}`
    },
  )

  await check(
    "mint_group_move: B's session on A's phase is refused as not found; a signed-out caller cannot execute it",
    async () => {
      const stranger = await move(B.client, 1, true)
      const notFound = expectSentence(stranger.error, PERMISSION, 'phase not found')
      if (notFound) return `B: ${notFound}`
      const signedOut = await move(anon, 1, true)
      const denied = expectSentence(signedOut.error, PERMISSION, 'permission denied')
      if (denied) return `signed out: ${denied}`
      return (await positions()) === '0:0 1:1' ? null : 'the positions changed'
    },
  )

  await check(
    "mint_group_record and mint_groups_refresh: the owner's session and a signed-out caller cannot execute them",
    async () => {
      const problems: string[] = []
      for (const [who, client] of [
        ['owner session', A.client],
        ['signed out', anon],
      ] as const) {
        const record = await callArt(
          client,
          'mint_group_record',
          phaseRecordArgs(A.userId, cid, 2, 'Allowlist'),
        )
        const recordProblem = expectSentence(record.error, PERMISSION, 'permission denied')
        if (recordProblem) problems.push(`${who} record: ${recordProblem}`)
        const refresh = await callArt(client, 'mint_groups_refresh', refreshArgs(A.userId, cid, 1))
        const refreshProblem = expectSentence(refresh.error, PERMISSION, 'permission denied')
        if (refreshProblem) problems.push(`${who} refresh: ${refreshProblem}`)
      }
      return problems.length ? problems.join('; ') : null
    },
  )

  await check(
    'mint_group_record and mint_groups_refresh: a draft is refused as not deployed, another owner as not found',
    async () => {
      const problems: string[] = []
      const draftRecord = await callArt(
        admin,
        'mint_group_record',
        phaseRecordArgs(A.userId, draftId, 1, 'Early'),
      )
      const recordDraft = expectSentence(draftRecord.error, CHECK, NOT_DEPLOYED_SENTENCE)
      if (recordDraft) problems.push(`record a draft: ${recordDraft}`)
      const draftRefresh = await callArt(admin, 'mint_groups_refresh', refreshArgs(A.userId, draftId, 1))
      const refreshDraft = expectSentence(draftRefresh.error, CHECK, NOT_DEPLOYED_SENTENCE)
      if (refreshDraft) problems.push(`refresh a draft: ${refreshDraft}`)
      const otherRecord = await callArt(
        admin,
        'mint_group_record',
        phaseRecordArgs(B.userId, cid, 2, 'Allowlist'),
      )
      const recordOther = expectSentence(otherRecord.error, PERMISSION, 'collection not found')
      if (recordOther) problems.push(`record for another owner: ${recordOther}`)
      const otherRefresh = await callArt(admin, 'mint_groups_refresh', refreshArgs(B.userId, cid, 1))
      const refreshOther = expectSentence(otherRefresh.error, PERMISSION, 'collection not found')
      if (refreshOther) problems.push(`refresh for another owner: ${refreshOther}`)
      if (problems.length) return problems.join('; ')
      return (await countRows('mint_groups', 'collection_id', draftId)) === 0
        ? null
        : 'a row was written for the draft'
    },
  )

  await check(
    'mint_group_record: records a new phase at the next position with its name, description and visibility',
    async () => {
      const { data, error } = await callArt(
        admin,
        'mint_group_record',
        phaseRecordArgs(A.userId, cid, 2, 'Allowlist', 'Friends of the project.', false),
      )
      if (error) return describe(error)
      const returned = data as { onchain_id?: number; position?: number } | null
      if (returned?.onchain_id !== 2 || returned.position !== 2) return `returned ${JSON.stringify(returned)}`
      const added = (await phaseRows(cid))[2]
      return added?.name === 'Allowlist' &&
        added.description === 'Friends of the project.' &&
        added.visible === false &&
        added.price_wei === '0' &&
        added.max_supply === 0 &&
        added.start_at === null
        ? null
        : JSON.stringify(added)
    },
  )

  await check(
    'mint_group_record: on a phase that has a row it updates the name, description and visibility and keeps the position',
    async () => {
      const moved = await move(A.client, 2, true)
      if (moved.error) return `setup move: ${describe(moved.error)}`
      const { error } = await callArt(
        admin,
        'mint_group_record',
        phaseRecordArgs(A.userId, cid, 2, 'Allowlist two', 'Renamed.', true),
      )
      if (error) return describe(error)
      const rows = await phaseRows(cid)
      const back = await move(A.client, 2, false)
      if (back.error) return `move back: ${describe(back.error)}`
      const two = rows[2]
      return two?.name === 'Allowlist two' &&
        two.description === 'Renamed.' &&
        two.visible === true &&
        two.position === 1 &&
        rows[1]?.position === 2
        ? null
        : JSON.stringify(rows)
    },
  )

  await check(
    'mint_group_record: a taken name and a hidden public sale are refused as the index and the check raise them',
    async () => {
      const taken = await callArt(admin, 'mint_group_record', phaseRecordArgs(A.userId, cid, 3, 'holders'))
      const nameProblem = expectSentence(taken.error, UNIQUE, 'mint_groups_name_key')
      if (nameProblem) return `taken name: ${nameProblem}`
      const hidden = await callArt(
        admin,
        'mint_group_record',
        phaseRecordArgs(A.userId, cid, 0, 'Public', '', false),
      )
      const hiddenProblem = expectSentence(hidden.error, CHECK, 'mint_groups_public_shown')
      if (hiddenProblem) return `hidden public sale: ${hiddenProblem}`
      const rows = await phaseRows(cid)
      return rows.length === 3 && rows[0]?.visible === true ? null : JSON.stringify(rows)
    },
  )

  await check(
    'mint_groups_refresh: writes the copies that differ, the cap, state_block and state_read_at, and answers true',
    async () => {
      const { data, error } = await callArt(
        admin,
        'mint_groups_refresh',
        refreshArgs(A.userId, cid, 100, {
          p_public_max_per_wallet: 5,
          p_groups: [
            { onchain_id: 0, price_wei: '2000', max_supply: 2, start: 1_793_491_260 },
            { onchain_id: 1, price_wei: '1000000000000000', max_supply: 1, start: 0 },
            { onchain_id: 2, price_wei: '5', max_supply: 0, start: 0 },
            { onchain_id: 9, price_wei: '1', max_supply: 1, start: 0 },
          ],
        }),
      )
      if (error) return describe(error)
      if (data !== true) return `answered ${JSON.stringify(data)}`
      const rows = await phaseRows(cid)
      const state = await copyState(cid)
      const publicSale = rows[0]
      const problems = [
        publicSale?.price_wei === '2000' &&
        publicSale.start_at !== null &&
        Date.parse(publicSale.start_at) === 1_793_491_260_000
          ? null
          : `public sale ${JSON.stringify(publicSale)}`,
        rows[2]?.price_wei === '5' ? null : `allowlist ${JSON.stringify(rows[2])}`,
        rows.length === 3 ? null : `${rows.length} rows: the refresh inserted one`,
        state.public_max_per_wallet === 5 ? null : `cap ${state.public_max_per_wallet}`,
        state.state_block === 100 ? null : `state_block ${state.state_block}`,
        state.state_read_at !== null && Date.parse(state.state_read_at) === Date.parse(blockTime(100))
          ? null
          : `state_read_at ${state.state_read_at}`,
      ].filter(Boolean)
      return problems.length ? problems.join('; ') : null
    },
  )

  await check(
    'mint_groups_refresh: an older block writes nothing and answers false; the same block writes again, a cap of 0 included',
    async () => {
      const older = await callArt(
        admin,
        'mint_groups_refresh',
        refreshArgs(A.userId, cid, 99, { p_public_max_per_wallet: 7 }),
      )
      if (older.error) return `older: ${describe(older.error)}`
      if (older.data !== false) return `the older block answered ${JSON.stringify(older.data)}`
      const unchanged = await copyState(cid)
      if (unchanged.public_max_per_wallet !== 5 || unchanged.state_block !== 100)
        return `the older block wrote ${JSON.stringify(unchanged)}`
      if ((await phaseRows(cid))[0]?.price_wei !== '2000') return 'the older block wrote a copy'
      const same = await callArt(
        admin,
        'mint_groups_refresh',
        refreshArgs(A.userId, cid, 100, { p_public_max_per_wallet: 0 }),
      )
      if (same.error) return `same block: ${describe(same.error)}`
      const after = await copyState(cid)
      const publicSale = (await phaseRows(cid))[0]
      return same.data === true &&
        after.public_max_per_wallet === 0 &&
        publicSale?.price_wei === '0' &&
        publicSale.start_at === null
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify(after)} ${JSON.stringify(publicSale)}`
    },
  )
}
```

Section 9k adds sixteen `check(` calls; with the `info` line turned into a check, the total grows by seventeen (the owner-create check is replaced one for one).

Expected: the run ends `<passed> passed, <failed> failed` with at least fifteen failures, among them `FAIL mint_groups: A cannot insert a group; rows come only from the record functions: accepted (1 row(s) written)` and `FAIL mint_groups: the launch phases read positions 0 and 1, an empty description and shown: threw phases: 42703 column mint_groups.description does not exist` (the columns and functions do not exist yet). - [ ] **Step 3: The migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261015000000_phases.sql`:

```sql
-- Phases (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 4): mint_groups gains what Cobweb keeps off chain
-- (a description, the mint page visibility and the creator's order), names of 1 to 40 characters without
-- outer spaces and unique in the collection ignoring case, and the public sale always shown. The owner's
-- client changes only those four columns; rows come only from collection_record_deploy and
-- mint_group_record, and the copies of price, allocation and start only from mint_groups_refresh, which
-- writes what a server read of collectionState() returned. deployments keeps the public limit as a copy
-- of the contract's, with the block and the time of the read the copies came from. mint_group_move swaps
-- two positions for the owner.

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- mint_groups: the off-chain columns and their checks
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
alter table public.mint_groups
  add column description text not null default '' check (char_length(description) <= 500),
  add column visible boolean not null default true,
  add column position integer check (position >= 0),
  add constraint mint_groups_public_shown check (onchain_id <> 0 or visible);

alter table public.mint_groups drop constraint mint_groups_name_check;
alter table public.mint_groups add constraint mint_groups_name_check
  check (char_length(name) between 1 and 40 and name = btrim(name));

-- Existing rows came through the deploy form, so their names already hold the new check and index; their
-- order is the order the constructor took them in.
update public.mint_groups set position = onchain_id;

create unique index mint_groups_name_key on public.mint_groups (collection_id, lower(name));
create unique index mint_groups_position_key on public.mint_groups (collection_id, position)
  where position is not null;

comment on table public.mint_groups is
  'One row per mint group Cobweb knows on a deployed collection. Off chain, changed by the owner: name, description, visible and position (null only inside mint_group_move). A copy of the chain, written only by mint_groups_refresh from a server read of collectionState(): price_wei, max_supply and start_at. Rows come only from collection_record_deploy and mint_group_record.';

-- A row inserted without a position takes the next one in its collection. Both writers lock the
-- collection first (private.lock_owned_collection), so two inserts never pick the same position.
create function public.mint_groups_set_position()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
security invoker
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if new.position is null then
    select coalesce(max(g.position) + 1, 0) into new.position
    from public.mint_groups g
    where g.collection_id = new.collection_id;
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$$;

create trigger mint_groups_set_position
before insert on public.mint_groups
for each row execute function public.mint_groups_set_position();

-- The owner reads every column and changes only the four off-chain ones. The policy
-- "mint_groups: owner all" stays: it scopes what the grants allow to the owner's rows.
revoke insert, update, delete on public.mint_groups from authenticated;
grant update (name, description, visible, position) on public.mint_groups to authenticated;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- deployments: the public limit becomes the copy of the contract's, with the read it came from
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
alter table public.deployments drop constraint deployments_public_max_per_wallet_check;
alter table public.deployments
  add constraint deployments_public_max_per_wallet_check check (public_max_per_wallet >= 0),
  add column state_block bigint check (state_block >= 0),
  add column state_read_at timestamptz;

comment on column public.deployments.public_max_per_wallet is
  'A copy of the contract''s publicMaxMintPerWallet(), refreshed by mint_groups_refresh. The value at deploy stays in args.publicMaxPerWallet.';
comment on column public.deployments.state_block is
  'The block of the server read the copies (mint_groups price_wei, max_supply, start_at and public_max_per_wallet) were last refreshed from.';
comment on column public.deployments.state_read_at is 'The time of that block.';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- mint_group_record: the row of a group the chain proved, written after its receipt
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Service role only: the id comes from a receipt's MintGroupRegistered log or from a server read of the
-- chain, never from the browser alone. p_owner must equal collections.owner_id.
create function public.mint_group_record(
  p_collection_id uuid,
  p_owner uuid,
  p_onchain_id integer,
  p_name text,
  p_description text,
  p_visible boolean
)
returns public.mint_groups
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_collection public.collections;
  v_row public.mint_groups;
begin
  v_collection := private.lock_owned_collection(p_collection_id, p_owner);
  if not exists (select 1 from public.deployments d where d.collection_id = v_collection.id) then
    raise exception 'The contract is not deployed yet.' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  -- A row already there (named before, or left behind by a reorg) is taken over and keeps its position.
  -- The name index and the checks raise as they are.
  insert into public.mint_groups (collection_id, onchain_id, name, description, visible)
  values (v_collection.id, p_onchain_id, p_name, p_description, p_visible)
  on conflict (collection_id, onchain_id) do update
    set name = excluded.name, description = excluded.description, visible = excluded.visible
  returning * into v_row;
  return v_row;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.mint_group_record(uuid, uuid, integer, text, text, boolean)
  from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.mint_group_record(uuid, uuid, integer, text, text, boolean) to service_role;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- mint_groups_refresh: the copies from a server read of collectionState()
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Service role only. p_groups is [{ onchain_id, price_wei, max_supply, start }], start in Unix seconds and
-- 0 for none. A read older than the last one writes nothing and answers false; rows are never inserted or
-- deleted here.
create function public.mint_groups_refresh(
  p_collection_id uuid,
  p_owner uuid,
  p_block bigint,
  p_block_time timestamptz,
  p_public_max_per_wallet integer,
  p_groups jsonb
)
returns boolean
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_collection public.collections;
  v_deployment public.deployments;
  v_group jsonb;
  v_id integer;
  v_price numeric;
  v_supply integer;
  v_start timestamptz;
begin
  v_collection := private.lock_owned_collection(p_collection_id, p_owner);
  select d.* into v_deployment
  from public.deployments d
  where d.collection_id = v_collection.id
  for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'The contract is not deployed yet.' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  -- A slower read never overwrites a newer one.
  if v_deployment.state_block is not null and p_block < v_deployment.state_block then
    return false;
  end if;
  for v_group in select e.value from jsonb_array_elements(p_groups) as e(value)
  loop
    v_id := (v_group->>'onchain_id')::integer;
    v_price := (v_group->>'price_wei')::numeric;
    v_supply := (v_group->>'max_supply')::integer;
    v_start := case
      when coalesce((v_group->>'start')::bigint, 0) = 0 then null
      else to_timestamp((v_group->>'start')::bigint)
    end;
    update public.mint_groups g
    set price_wei = v_price, max_supply = v_supply, start_at = v_start
    where g.collection_id = v_collection.id
      and g.onchain_id = v_id
      and (g.price_wei is distinct from v_price
        or g.max_supply is distinct from v_supply
        or g.start_at is distinct from v_start);
  end loop;
  update public.deployments d
  set public_max_per_wallet = p_public_max_per_wallet,
      state_block = p_block,
      state_read_at = p_block_time
  where d.collection_id = v_collection.id;
  return true;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.mint_groups_refresh(uuid, uuid, bigint, timestamptz, integer, jsonb)
  from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.mint_groups_refresh(uuid, uuid, bigint, timestamptz, integer, jsonb)
  to service_role;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- mint_group_move: the owner's Move up and Move down
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Security invoker: it runs under the owner's policy and column grant, so another creator's phase is
-- not found. The row parks at null, the neighbour takes its position, the row takes the neighbour's;
-- an end row stays where it is.
create function public.mint_group_move(p_collection_id uuid, p_onchain_id integer, p_up boolean)
returns void
language plpgsql
security invoker
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_row public.mint_groups;
  v_neighbour public.mint_groups;
begin
  select g.* into v_row
  from public.mint_groups g
  where g.collection_id = p_collection_id and g.onchain_id = p_onchain_id
  for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'phase not found' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_up then
    select g.* into v_neighbour
    from public.mint_groups g
    where g.collection_id = p_collection_id and g.position < v_row.position
    order by g.position desc
    limit 1
    for update;
  else
    select g.* into v_neighbour
    from public.mint_groups g
    where g.collection_id = p_collection_id and g.position > v_row.position
    order by g.position asc
    limit 1
    for update;
  end if;
  if not found then
    return;
  end if;
  update public.mint_groups g set position = null where g.id = v_row.id;
  update public.mint_groups g set position = v_row.position where g.id = v_neighbour.id;
  update public.mint_groups g set position = v_neighbour.position where g.id = v_row.id;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.mint_group_move(uuid, integer, boolean) from public, anon, service_role;
grant execute on function public.mint_group_move(uuid, integer, boolean) to authenticated;
```

Run: `supabase migration up`
Expected: `Applying migration 20261015000000_phases.sql...` then `Local database is up to date.` If it stops on a constraint because local rows from an earlier manual walk break the new name rules, run `supabase db reset && supabase seed buckets` (local only; it wipes the local data) and continue.

4. Replace the `mint_groups` Row, Insert and Update (from `      mint_groups: {` through the `Update` block's closing `        }`, just before `        Relationships: [`):

```ts
      mint_groups: {
        Row: {
          collection_id: string
          created_at: string
          id: string
          max_supply: number
          name: string
          onchain_id: number
          price_wei: number
          start_at: string | null

        }
        Insert: {
```
