Create `apps/web/src/lib/manage/phase-calls.test.ts`:

```ts
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import { concat, encodeAbiParameters, encodeFunctionData, toFunctionSelector, type Hex } from 'viem'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { SupportedChain } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import { callOf, expectedEvent, phaseCalls, reviewOf, sentOf, type ManageCall } from './phase-calls'

const BASE: SupportedChain = {
  id: 8453,
  name: 'Base',
  nativeSymbol: 'ETH',
  explorerUrl: 'https://basescan.org',
  transferLock: true,
}
const UINT = { type: 'uint256' } as const
const BOOL = { type: 'bool' } as const

/** The calldata a hand-written signature gives for the same values. */
function handEncoded(signature: string, types: readonly { type: string }[], values: readonly unknown[]): Hex {
  return concat([toFunctionSelector(signature), encodeAbiParameters(types, values)])
}

const encoded = (call: ManageCall) =>
  encodeFunctionData({ abi: collectionAbi, functionName: call.functionName, args: call.args })

describe('phaseCalls', () => {
  it('encodes each call as the hand-written signature does, the price first in changeSalePrice', () => {
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.register(2, '1500000000000000', 100, 1_791_039_600))).toBe(
      handEncoded(
        'registerMintGroup(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256)',
        [UINT, UINT, UINT, UINT],
        [2n, 1_500_000_000_000_000n, 100n, 1_791_039_600n],
      ),
    )
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.open(1))).toBe(
      handEncoded('setMintGroupStatus(uint256,bool)', [UINT, BOOL], [1n, true]),
    )
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.close(1))).toBe(
      handEncoded('setMintGroupStatus(uint256,bool)', [UINT, BOOL], [1n, false]),
    )
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.closeAll())).toBe(
      handEncoded('stopOrStartAllMintGroups(bool)', [BOOL], [false]),
    )
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.publicCap(5))).toBe(
      handEncoded('setPublicMaxMintPerWallet(uint256)', [UINT], [5n]),
    )
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.schedule(1, 1_791_039_600))).toBe(
      handEncoded('scheduleMintGroupStart(uint256,uint256)', [UINT, UINT], [1n, 1_791_039_600n]),
    )
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.clearSchedule(1))).toBe(
      handEncoded('scheduleMintGroupStart(uint256,uint256)', [UINT, UINT], [1n, 0n]),
    )
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.price(1, '2000000000000000'))).toBe(
      handEncoded('changeSalePrice(uint256,uint256)', [UINT, UINT], [2_000_000_000_000_000n, 1n]),
    )
    expect(encoded(phaseCalls.allocation(1, 0))).toBe(
      handEncoded('setMintGroupMaxSupply(uint256,uint256)', [UINT, UINT], [1n, 0n]),
    )
  })
})

describe('expectedEvent', () => {
  it('names the event each call leaves, with its arguments', () => {
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.register(2, '0', 1, 0))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'MintGroupRegistered',
      args: { mintGroup: 2n },
    })
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.open(1))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'MintGroupStatusChanged',
      args: { mintGroup: 1n, status: true },
    })
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.close(1))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'MintGroupStatusChanged',
      args: { mintGroup: 1n, status: false },
    })
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.closeAll())).toEqual({
      eventName: 'AllMintGroupsStatusChanged',
      args: { status: false },
    })
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.schedule(1, 1_791_039_600))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'MintGroupStartTimestampChanged',
      args: { mintGroup: 1n, timestamp: 1_791_039_600n },
    })
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.clearSchedule(1))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'MintGroupStartTimestampChanged',
      args: { mintGroup: 1n, timestamp: 0n },
    })
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.price(1, '2000'))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'SalePriceChanged',
      args: { mintGroup: 1n, newPrice: 2000n },
    })
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.allocation(1, 0))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'MintGroupMaxSupplyUpdated',
      args: { mintGroup: 1n, newMaxSupply: 0n },
    })
    expect(expectedEvent(phaseCalls.publicCap(5))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'PublicMaxMintPerWalletChanged',
      args: { newMaxMintPerWallet: 5n },
    })
  })
})

describe('reviewOf', () => {
  it("shows the ABI's argument names with readable values", () => {
    expect(reviewOf(phaseCalls.price(1, '1500000000000000'), BASE)).toEqual([
      { name: 'newMintPrice', value: '0.0015 ETH (1500000000000000 wei)' },
      { name: 'mintGroup', value: '1' },
    ])
    expect(reviewOf(phaseCalls.register(2, '0', 100, 1_791_039_600), BASE)).toEqual([
      { name: 'mintGroup', value: '2' },
      { name: 'price', value: '0 ETH (0 wei)' },
      { name: 'maxSupply', value: '100' },
      { name: 'startTimestamp', value: '2026-10-03 15:00 UTC (1791039600)' },
    ])
    expect(reviewOf(phaseCalls.clearSchedule(1), BASE)).toEqual([
      { name: 'mintGroup', value: '1' },
      { name: 'startTimestamp', value: '0 (no scheduled start)' },
    ])
    expect(reviewOf(phaseCalls.open(1), BASE)).toEqual([
      { name: 'mintGroup', value: '1' },
      { name: 'status', value: 'true' },
    ])
    expect(reviewOf(phaseCalls.closeAll(), BASE)).toEqual([{ name: 'status', value: 'false' }])
    expect(reviewOf(phaseCalls.publicCap(5), BASE)).toEqual([
      { name: 'newPublicMaxMintPerWallet', value: '5' },
    ])
  })
})

describe('sentOf and callOf', () => {
  it('round trip every call through decimal strings', () => {
    const calls: ManageCall[] = [
      phaseCalls.register(2, '1500000000000000', 100, 1_791_039_600),
      phaseCalls.open(1),
      phaseCalls.close(0),
      phaseCalls.closeAll(),
      phaseCalls.publicCap(5),
      phaseCalls.schedule(1, 1_791_039_600),
      phaseCalls.clearSchedule(1),
      phaseCalls.price(1, '2000'),
      phaseCalls.allocation(1, 0),
    ]
    for (const call of calls) expect(callOf(sentOf(call))).toEqual(call)
    expect(sentOf(phaseCalls.price(1, '2000'))).toEqual({
      functionName: 'changeSalePrice',
      args: ['2000', '1'],
    })
    expect(sentOf(phaseCalls.open(1))).toEqual({ functionName: 'setMintGroupStatus', args: ['1', true] })
  })

  it('keeps every wei of a price above 2^53 through sentOf, callOf and the expected event (Review Focus 1)', () => {
    // 1,000,000 MON is 10^24 wei: far above what a JavaScript number holds exactly.
    const priceWei = '1000000000000000000000001'
    const sent = sentOf(phaseCalls.price(3, priceWei))
    expect(sent.args[0]).toBe(priceWei)
    expect(expectedEvent(callOf(sent))).toEqual({
      eventName: 'SalePriceChanged',
      args: { mintGroup: 3n, newPrice: 1_000_000_000_000_000_000_000_001n },
    })
  })
})
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/manage/phases-view.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { Address } from 'viem'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { MintGroup } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import type { ChainGroup, ChainState, PhaseRowCopy } from './phases-types'
import { copyView, joinPhases, liveView, moveGroup, refreshPayload, type ViewBase } from './phases-view'

const CONTRACT: Address = '0x0409454C375c8a6aB05383bFB6D9FcDe9ebB8F85'
const START = 1_791_039_600

const chainGroup = (id: number, patch: Partial<ChainGroup> = {}): ChainGroup => ({
  id,
  priceWei: '0',
  maxSupply: 1,
  minted: 0,
  startTimestamp: 0,
  flag: false,
  active: false,
  ...patch,
})

const row = (id: number, position: number | null, patch: Partial<PhaseRowCopy> = {}): PhaseRowCopy => ({
  onchain_id: id,
  name: id === 0 ? 'Public' : `Phase ${id}`,
  description: '',
  visible: true,
  position,
  price_wei: '0',
  max_supply: 1,
  start_at: null,
  ...patch,
})

const state = (groups: ChainGroup[], patch: Partial<ChainState> = {}): ChainState => ({
  block: 120n,
  blockTimestamp: START + 60,
  maxSupply: 3,
  totalMinted: 1,
  publicMaxMintPerWallet: 2,
  mintOnBehalfActive: false,
  groups,
  ...patch,
})

const base: ViewBase = { chainId: 31337, contract: CONTRACT, rows: [] }

describe('joinPhases', () => {
  it("orders the groups by the rows' positions, then the groups without a row by id as Group N", () => {
    const groups = joinPhases(state([chainGroup(0), chainGroup(1), chainGroup(7), chainGroup(3)]), [
      row(0, 1),
      row(1, 0),
    ])
    expect(groups.map((group) => [group.id, group.name, group.named])).toEqual([
      [1, 'Phase 1', true],
      [0, 'Public', true],
      [3, 'Group 3', false],
      [7, 'Group 7', false],
    ])
    expect(groups.find((group) => group.id === 3)).toMatchObject({
      description: '',
      visible: true,
      kind: 'whitelist',
    })
    expect(groups.find((group) => group.id === 0)?.kind).toBe('public')
  })

  it('takes price, allocation, minted, the flag, the start and live from the chain, and the off-chain fields from the row', () => {
    const [group] = joinPhases(
      state([
        chainGroup(1, {
          priceWei: '2000',
          maxSupply: 5,
          minted: 4,
          startTimestamp: START,
          flag: false,
          active: true,
        }),
      ]),
      [row(1, 0, { price_wei: '1', max_supply: 9, description: 'For holders.', visible: false })],
    )
    expect(group).toEqual<MintGroup>({
      id: 1,
      name: 'Phase 1',
      kind: 'whitelist',
      priceWei: '2000',
      maxSupply: 5,
      minted: 4,
      flag: false,
      scheduledStart: START,
      active: true,
      description: 'For holders.',
      visible: false,
      named: true,
    })
  })

  it('leaves out a row the chain does not list, and sorts a row parked at a null position last', () => {
    const groups = joinPhases(state([chainGroup(0), chainGroup(1), chainGroup(2)]), [
      row(2, null),
      row(0, 0),
      row(5, 1),
      row(1, 2),
    ])
    expect(groups.map((group) => group.id)).toEqual([0, 1, 2])
  })
})

describe('liveView and copyView', () => {
  it('answers the totals, the limit and the time of a read that reached the chain', () => {
    const view = liveView({ ...base, rows: [row(0, 0)] }, state([chainGroup(0)]))
    expect(view).toMatchObject({
      chainId: 31337,
      contract: CONTRACT,
      publicMaxPerWallet: 2,
      totalMinted: 1,
      maxSupply: 3,
      blockTimestamp: START + 60,
      readAt: START + 60,
      chainReachable: true,
    })
    expect(view.groups.map((group) => group.name)).toEqual(['Public'])
  })

  it('answers the copy in order, never minted or live, the start from start_at and the read time as the clock', () => {
    const view = copyView({
      ...base,
      rows: [
        row(1, 1, { price_wei: '1000000000000000', start_at: new Date((START - 600) * 1000).toISOString() }),
        row(0, 0),
      ],
      publicMaxPerWallet: 5,
      maxSupply: 3,
      readAt: START,
    })
    expect(view.chainReachable).toBe(false)
    expect(view.blockTimestamp).toBe(START)
    expect(view.readAt).toBe(START)
    expect(view.totalMinted).toBe(0)
    expect(view.publicMaxPerWallet).toBe(5)
    expect(view.groups.map((group) => [group.id, group.minted, group.flag, group.active])).toEqual([
      [0, 0, false, false],
      [1, 0, false, false],
    ])
    // A start that has passed is shown as a time, never as live.
    expect(view.groups[1]).toMatchObject({ scheduledStart: START - 600, priceWei: '1000000000000000' })
  })
})

describe('refreshPayload', () => {
  it('copies each group as mint_groups_refresh takes it', () => {
    expect(refreshPayload(state([chainGroup(0, { priceWei: '2000', startTimestamp: START })]))).toEqual({
      publicMaxPerWallet: 2,
      groups: [{ onchain_id: 0, price_wei: '2000', max_supply: 1, start: START }],
    })
  })

  it('copies nothing when an id, an allocation or the public limit is above 2,147,483,647 (Review Focus 5)', () => {
    expect(refreshPayload(state([chainGroup(0), chainGroup(2_147_483_648)]))).toBeNull()
    expect(refreshPayload(state([chainGroup(1, { maxSupply: 2_147_483_648 })]))).toBeNull()
    expect(refreshPayload(state([chainGroup(0)], { publicMaxMintPerWallet: 2 ** 60 }))).toBeNull()
    expect(refreshPayload(state([chainGroup(2_147_483_647)]))).not.toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('moveGroup', () => {
  const groups: MintGroup[] = joinPhases(
    state([chainGroup(0), chainGroup(1), chainGroup(2), chainGroup(9)]),
    [row(0, 0), row(1, 1), row(2, 2)],
  )

  it('swaps a named phase with its named neighbour and keeps the unnamed last', () => {
    expect(moveGroup(groups, 2, 'up').map((group) => group.id)).toEqual([0, 2, 1, 9])
    expect(moveGroup(groups, 0, 'down').map((group) => group.id)).toEqual([1, 0, 2, 9])
  })

  it('changes nothing past either end, for a group without a row, or for an unknown id', () => {
    expect(moveGroup(groups, 0, 'up')).toBe(groups)
    expect(moveGroup(groups, 2, 'down')).toBe(groups)
    expect(moveGroup(groups, 9, 'up')).toBe(groups)
    expect(moveGroup(groups, 4, 'up')).toBe(groups)
  })
})
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/chain/phase-events.test.ts`:

```ts
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import {
  encodeAbiParameters,
  encodeEventTopics,
  getAbiItem,
  type AbiEvent,
  type Address,
  type Hex,
} from 'viem'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { NOT_FROM_WALLET, NOT_THIS_CHANGE, NOT_THIS_CONTRACT } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { expectedEvent, phaseCalls, type ManageCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import { matchPhaseEvent, type PhaseReceipt } from './phase-events'

const CONTRACT: Address = '0x0409454C375c8a6aB05383bFB6D9FcDe9ebB8F85'
const ELSEWHERE: Address = '0x2073D2716161E2Fa4eBcB3BA42348dE8f98A3dDd'
const WALLET: Address = '0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8'
const STRANGER: Address = '0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC'

type ReceiptLog = PhaseReceipt['logs'][number]
type EventName = Extract<(typeof collectionAbi)[number], { type: 'event' }>['name']

/** A log of a collection event, encoded as the contract emits it: indexed arguments in topics, the rest in data. */
function logOf(address: Address, eventName: EventName, args: Record<string, bigint | boolean>): ReceiptLog {
  const item = getAbiItem({ abi: collectionAbi, name: eventName }) as AbiEvent
  const indexed = Object.fromEntries(
    item.inputs.filter((input) => input.indexed).map((input) => [input.name ?? '', args[input.name ?? '']]),
  )
  const data = item.inputs.filter((input) => !input.indexed)
  return {
    address,
    topics: encodeEventTopics({
      abi: [item],
      eventName: item.name,
      args: indexed,
    } as never) as ReceiptLog['topics'],
    data: encodeAbiParameters(data, data.map((input) => args[input.name ?? '']) as never) as Hex,
  }
}

const receipt = (logs: ReceiptLog[], patch: Partial<PhaseReceipt> = {}): PhaseReceipt => ({
  status: 'success',
  from: WALLET,
  to: CONTRACT,
  logs,
  ...patch,
})

const expect_ = (call: ManageCall) => ({
  contract: CONTRACT,
  from: WALLET.toLowerCase(),
  event: expectedEvent(call),
})

describe('matchPhaseEvent', () => {
  it.each<[string, ManageCall, EventName, Record<string, bigint | boolean>]>([
    ['Add phase', phaseCalls.register(2, '0', 1, 0), 'MintGroupRegistered', { mintGroup: 2n }],
    ['Open now', phaseCalls.open(1), 'MintGroupStatusChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, status: true }],
    ['Close', phaseCalls.close(1), 'MintGroupStatusChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, status: false }],
    ['Close all', phaseCalls.closeAll(), 'AllMintGroupsStatusChanged', { status: false, groupsAffected: 3n }],
    [
      'Schedule',
      phaseCalls.schedule(1, 1_791_039_600),
      'MintGroupStartTimestampChanged',
      { mintGroup: 1n, timestamp: 1_791_039_600n },
    ],
    [
      'Clear schedule',
      phaseCalls.clearSchedule(1),
      'MintGroupStartTimestampChanged',
      { mintGroup: 1n, timestamp: 0n },
    ],
    ['Edit price', phaseCalls.price(1, '2000'), 'SalePriceChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, newPrice: 2000n }],
    [
      'Edit max supply',
      phaseCalls.allocation(1, 0),
      'MintGroupMaxSupplyUpdated',
      { mintGroup: 1n, newMaxSupply: 0n },
    ],
    [
      'Edit per-wallet limit',
      phaseCalls.publicCap(5),
      'PublicMaxMintPerWalletChanged',
      { newMaxMintPerWallet: 5n },
    ],
  ])('matches %s by its event and arguments', (_label, call, eventName, args) => {
    expect(matchPhaseEvent(receipt([logOf(CONTRACT, eventName, args)]), expect_(call))).toEqual({ ok: true })
  })

  it('refuses a transaction sent to another contract, and a contract creation', () => {
    const log = logOf(CONTRACT, 'MintGroupStatusChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, status: true })
    expect(matchPhaseEvent(receipt([log], { to: ELSEWHERE }), expect_(phaseCalls.open(1)))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: NOT_THIS_CONTRACT,
    })
    expect(matchPhaseEvent(receipt([log], { to: null }), expect_(phaseCalls.open(1)))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: NOT_THIS_CONTRACT,
    })
  })

  it('refuses a transaction sent from another wallet', () => {
    const log = logOf(CONTRACT, 'MintGroupStatusChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, status: true })
    expect(matchPhaseEvent(receipt([log], { from: STRANGER }), expect_(phaseCalls.open(1)))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: NOT_FROM_WALLET,
    })
  })

  it('refuses a receipt without the event, with other arguments, or with another event of the collection', () => {
    const refused = { ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_CHANGE }
    expect(matchPhaseEvent(receipt([]), expect_(phaseCalls.open(1)))).toEqual(refused)
    expect(
      matchPhaseEvent(
        receipt([logOf(CONTRACT, 'MintGroupStatusChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, status: false })]),
        expect_(phaseCalls.open(1)),
      ),
    ).toEqual(refused)
    expect(
      matchPhaseEvent(
        receipt([logOf(CONTRACT, 'SalePriceChanged', { mintGroup: 2n, newPrice: 2000n })]),
        expect_(phaseCalls.price(1, '2000')),
      ),
    ).toEqual(refused)
    expect(
      matchPhaseEvent(
        receipt([logOf(CONTRACT, 'SalePriceChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, newPrice: 2000n })]),
        expect_(phaseCalls.allocation(1, 2000)),
      ),
    ).toEqual(refused)
    const unrelated: ReceiptLog = { address: CONTRACT, topics: [`0x${'12'.repeat(32)}`], data: '0x' }
    expect(matchPhaseEvent(receipt([unrelated]), expect_(phaseCalls.open(1)))).toEqual(refused)
  })

  it('ignores the expected event when another contract logs it in the same receipt', () => {
    expect(
      matchPhaseEvent(
        receipt([logOf(ELSEWHERE, 'MintGroupStatusChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, status: true })]),
        expect_(phaseCalls.open(1)),
      ),
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_CHANGE })
  })

  it('never matches a reverted receipt', () => {
    expect(
      matchPhaseEvent(
        receipt([logOf(CONTRACT, 'MintGroupStatusChanged', { mintGroup: 1n, status: true })], {
          status: 'reverted',
        }),
        expect_(phaseCalls.open(1)),
      ),
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_CHANGE })
  })

  it('matches the contract and the sign-in wallet whatever their case (Review Focus 2)', () => {
    const log = logOf(CONTRACT.toLowerCase() as Address, 'MintGroupStatusChanged', {
      mintGroup: 1n,
      status: true,
    })
    // The session wallet is stored lowercase; the receipt carries it checksummed, the contract either way.
    expect(
      matchPhaseEvent(receipt([log], { from: WALLET, to: CONTRACT.toLowerCase() as Address }), {
        contract: CONTRACT,
        from: WALLET.toLowerCase(),
        event: expectedEvent(phaseCalls.open(1)),
      }),
    ).toEqual({ ok: true })
  })

  it('matches Close all whatever number of groups it closed (Review Focus 3)', () => {
    for (const groupsAffected of [1n, 3n, 10n])
      expect(
        matchPhaseEvent(
          receipt([
            logOf(CONTRACT, 'MintGroupStatusChanged', { mintGroup: 0n, status: false }),
            logOf(CONTRACT, 'AllMintGroupsStatusChanged', { status: false, groupsAffected }),
          ]),
          expect_(phaseCalls.closeAll()),
        ),
      ).toEqual({ ok: true })
  })
})
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/manage/phase-schemas.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { PHASE_NAME_EMPTY, PHASE_NAME_LONG } from '@/components/manage/phase-rules'
import { DESCRIPTION_TOO_LONG, UNSAVED } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { parse } from '@/lib/art/schemas'
import { callOf, phaseCalls, sentOf, type ManageCall } from './phase-calls'
import {
  directionShape,
  onchainIdShape,
  parseSentCall,
  phaseAddShape,
  phaseDetailsShape,
} from './phase-schemas'

describe('parseSentCall', () => {
  it('accepts every call the dialogs build, and answers it unchanged', () => {
    const calls: ManageCall[] = [
      phaseCalls.register(2, '1500000000000000', 100, 1_791_039_600),
      phaseCalls.open(1),
      phaseCalls.close(0),
      phaseCalls.closeAll(),
      phaseCalls.publicCap(5),
      phaseCalls.schedule(1, 1_791_039_600),
      phaseCalls.clearSchedule(1),
      phaseCalls.price(2_147_483_647, '1000000000000000000000001'),
      phaseCalls.allocation(1, 0),
    ]
    for (const call of calls) {
      const parsed = parseSentCall(sentOf(call))
      expect(parsed.ok).toBe(true)
      if (parsed.ok) expect(callOf(parsed.data)).toEqual(call)
    }
  })

  it.each<[string, unknown]>([
    ['an unknown function', { functionName: 'withdrawMintFunds', args: [] }],
    ['a missing argument', { functionName: 'changeSalePrice', args: ['1000'] }],
    ['an extra argument', { functionName: 'setPublicMaxMintPerWallet', args: ['5', '6'] }],
    ['an id above 2,147,483,647', { functionName: 'setMintGroupStatus', args: ['2147483648', true] }],
    ['a negative value', { functionName: 'changeSalePrice', args: ['-1', '1'] }],
    ['a fraction', { functionName: 'setMintGroupMaxSupply', args: ['1', '1.5'] }],
    ['a value above 2^256 - 1', { functionName: 'changeSalePrice', args: ['9'.repeat(78), '1'] }],
    ['a boolean as text', { functionName: 'stopOrStartAllMintGroups', args: ['false'] }],
    ['nothing at all', null],
  ])('refuses %s as UNSAVED', (_label, input) => {
    expect(parseSentCall(input)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: UNSAVED })
  })
})

describe('phaseAddShape and phaseDetailsShape', () => {
  it("accepts an add and trims the name and the description; refuses with the screen's sentences", () => {
    expect(
      parse(phaseAddShape, {
        onchainId: 2,
        name: ' Holders ',
        description: ' For holders. ',
        visible: false,
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      data: { onchainId: 2, name: 'Holders', description: 'For holders.', visible: false },
    })
    expect(parse(phaseAddShape, { onchainId: 0, name: 'Public', description: '', visible: true })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: UNSAVED,
    })
    expect(parse(phaseAddShape, { onchainId: 2, name: '   ', description: '', visible: true })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: PHASE_NAME_EMPTY,
    })
    expect(
      parse(phaseAddShape, { onchainId: 2, name: 'x'.repeat(41), description: '', visible: true }),
    ).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: PHASE_NAME_LONG,
    })
    expect(parse(phaseDetailsShape, { name: 'Holders', description: 'd'.repeat(501) })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: DESCRIPTION_TOO_LONG,
    })
    expect(parse(phaseAddShape, { onchainId: 2, name: 'Holders', description: '', visible: 'yes' })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: UNSAVED,
    })
  })

  it('trims a name with outer spaces before its checks, so 40 characters inside spaces pass and the database gets the trimmed name (Review Focus 4)', () => {
    const name = 'x'.repeat(40)
    expect(parse(phaseDetailsShape, { name: `  ${name}  `, description: '' })).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      data: { name, description: '' },
    })
  })

  it('checks a group id and a direction', () => {
    expect(parse(onchainIdShape, 0)).toEqual({ ok: true, data: 0 })
    expect(parse(onchainIdShape, 2_147_483_648)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: UNSAVED })
    expect(parse(onchainIdShape, 1.5)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: UNSAVED })
    expect(parse(directionShape, 'up')).toEqual({ ok: true, data: 'up' })
    expect(parse(directionShape, 'left')).toEqual({ ok: false, error: UNSAVED })
  })
})
```

```ts
describe('phasesWriteError', () => {
  it('reads every refusal of the owner check as not found', () => {
    expect(phasesWriteError({ code: '42501', message: 'collection not found' })).toBe(NOT_FOUND)
    expect(phasesWriteError({ code: '42501', message: 'phase not found' })).toBe(NOT_FOUND)
    expect(phasesWriteError({ code: '42501', message: 'permission denied for table mint_groups' })).toBe(
      NOT_FOUND,
    )
  })

  it('maps the name index and the public sale check, and passes the not-deployed sentence through', () => {
    expect(
      phasesWriteError({
        code: '23505',
        message: 'duplicate key value violates unique constraint "mint_groups_name_key"',
      }),
    ).toBe(PHASE_NAME_TAKEN)
    expect(
      phasesWriteError({
        code: '23514',
        message: 'new row for relation "mint_groups" violates check constraint "mint_groups_public_shown"',
      }),
    ).toBe(PUBLIC_SHOWN)
    expect(phasesWriteError({ code: '23514', message: NOT_DEPLOYED })).toBe(NOT_DEPLOYED)
  })

  it('says "reload" for anything else', () => {
    expect(
      phasesWriteError({
        code: '23505',
        message: 'duplicate key value violates unique constraint "mint_groups_position_key"',
      }),
    ).toBe(UNSAVED)
    expect(
      phasesWriteError({
        code: '23514',
        message: 'new row for relation "mint_groups" violates check constraint "mint_groups_name_check"',
      }),
    ).toBe(UNSAVED)
    expect(phasesWriteError(null)).toBe(UNSAVED)
  })
})
```

```ts
// Phases (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 6.5). The database raises NOT_DEPLOYED as it is.
export const NOT_DEPLOYED = 'The contract is not deployed yet.'
// Said by the server or the screen (the database does not raise these).
export const PHASE_NAME_TAKEN = 'Another phase already has this name.'
export const PUBLIC_SHOWN = 'The public sale is always shown on the mint page.'
export const PHASE_NOT_CONFIRMED = 'The change has not been confirmed on chain yet.'
export const PHASE_REVERTED = 'The change reverted on chain.'
export const NOT_THIS_CONTRACT = "The transaction was not sent to this collection's contract."
export const NOT_FROM_WALLET = 'The transaction was not sent from your sign-in wallet.'
```

In `apps/web/src/lib/art/db-error.ts`, in the import list from `./result` (the first statement of the file): add `NOT_DEPLOYED,` after `NOT_DEFERRED,`, add `PHASE_NAME_TAKEN,` after `PAYER_NOT_YOU,`, and add `PUBLIC_SHOWN,` after `PLACEHOLDER_LOCKED,`. (Change only the import list; `PAYER_NOT_YOU` and `PLACEHOLDER_LOCKED` also appear in the `LOCK_SENTENCES` set below, which stays as it is.) Then append at the end of the file:

```ts

/** Sentences the phases functions raise (supabase/migrations/20261015000000_phases.sql). */
const PHASES_SENTENCES = new Set([NOT_DEPLOYED])

/**
 * A failed phases write, by SQLSTATE, in the creator's words (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section
 * 6.4): the owner lock's 'collection not found', mint_group_move's 'phase not found' and a refused grant
 * all read as not found; the name index as a taken name; the public sale's check as always shown.
 */
export function phasesWriteError(error: DbError): string {
  if (!error) return UNSAVED
  if (error.code === '42501') return NOT_FOUND
  if (error.code === '23505' && error.message.includes('mint_groups_name_key')) return PHASE_NAME_TAKEN
  if (error.code === '23514') {
    if (error.message.includes('mint_groups_public_shown')) return PUBLIC_SHOWN
    if (PHASES_SENTENCES.has(error.message)) return error.message
  }
  return UNSAVED
}
```

```ts
/** Why a phase name cannot be used, or null. Names are compared without case or outer spaces. */
export function phaseNameError(name: string, others: string[]): string | null {
  const trimmed = name.trim()
  if (!trimmed) return 'Name the phase, for example "Holders".'
  if (trimmed.length > PHASE_NAME_MAX) return `Use at most ${PHASE_NAME_MAX} characters.`
  if (others.some((other) => other.trim().toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase()))
    return 'Another phase already has this name.'
  return null
}
```

```ts
/** The name sentences, shared with the server's checks (lib/manage/phase-schemas.ts). */
export const PHASE_NAME_EMPTY = 'Name the phase, for example "Holders".'
export const PHASE_NAME_LONG = `Use at most ${PHASE_NAME_MAX} characters.`

/** Why a phase name cannot be used, or null. Names are compared without case or outer spaces. */
export function phaseNameError(name: string, others: string[]): string | null {
  const trimmed = name.trim()
  if (!trimmed) return PHASE_NAME_EMPTY
  if (trimmed.length > PHASE_NAME_MAX) return PHASE_NAME_LONG
  if (others.some((other) => other.trim().toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase())) return PHASE_NAME_TAKEN
  return null
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The calls, the types, the view, the receipt match and the shapes**

Create `apps/web/src/lib/manage/phase-calls.ts`:

```ts
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import { getAbiItem, type AbiFunction, type ContractFunctionArgs } from 'viem'
import { formatNative, formatUtc } from '@/lib/demo/helpers'
import type { SupportedChain } from '@/lib/demo/types'

/**
 * The collection calls Manage drop sends (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.1), built in one
 * place for the browser, the server and verify:phases. Pure. The next two sub-projects add their
 * functions to `ManageFunction`.
 */
export type ManageFunction =
  | 'registerMintGroup'
  | 'setMintGroupStatus'
  | 'scheduleMintGroupStart'
  | 'changeSalePrice'
  | 'setMintGroupMaxSupply'
  | 'setPublicMaxMintPerWallet'
  | 'stopOrStartAllMintGroups'

/** A call as viem takes it: the function name and its arguments typed from the collection's ABI. */
export type ManageCall = {
  [F in ManageFunction]: {
    functionName: F
    args: ContractFunctionArgs<typeof collectionAbi, 'nonpayable', F>
  }
}[ManageFunction]

/** A call as it crosses the server action boundary: every uint256 as a decimal string, booleans as they are. */
export type SentCall = { functionName: ManageFunction; args: (string | boolean)[] }

/**
 * The collection event a call must leave in its receipt, with viem's argument names (section 6.3). Close
 * all names only its status: any number of groups may have changed.
 */
export type ExpectedEvent =
  | { eventName: 'MintGroupRegistered'; args: { mintGroup: bigint } }
  | { eventName: 'MintGroupStatusChanged'; args: { mintGroup: bigint; status: boolean } }
  | { eventName: 'AllMintGroupsStatusChanged'; args: { status: boolean } }
  | { eventName: 'MintGroupStartTimestampChanged'; args: { mintGroup: bigint; timestamp: bigint } }
  | { eventName: 'SalePriceChanged'; args: { mintGroup: bigint; newPrice: bigint } }
  | { eventName: 'MintGroupMaxSupplyUpdated'; args: { mintGroup: bigint; newMaxSupply: bigint } }
  | { eventName: 'PublicMaxMintPerWalletChanged'; args: { newMaxMintPerWallet: bigint } }

/** The builders the Phases dialogs use. Prices are wei as decimal strings, so no wei is lost. */
export const phaseCalls: {
  register(id: number, priceWei: string, maxSupply: number, startUnix: number): ManageCall
  open(id: number): ManageCall
  close(id: number): ManageCall
  closeAll(): ManageCall
  publicCap(cap: number): ManageCall
  schedule(id: number, startUnix: number): ManageCall
  clearSchedule(id: number): ManageCall
  price(id: number, priceWei: string): ManageCall
  allocation(id: number, maxSupply: number): ManageCall
} = {
  register: (id, priceWei, maxSupply, startUnix) => ({
    functionName: 'registerMintGroup',
    args: [BigInt(id), BigInt(priceWei), BigInt(maxSupply), BigInt(startUnix)],
  }),
  open: (id) => ({ functionName: 'setMintGroupStatus', args: [BigInt(id), true] }),
  close: (id) => ({ functionName: 'setMintGroupStatus', args: [BigInt(id), false] }),
  closeAll: () => ({ functionName: 'stopOrStartAllMintGroups', args: [false] }),
  publicCap: (cap) => ({ functionName: 'setPublicMaxMintPerWallet', args: [BigInt(cap)] }),
  schedule: (id, startUnix) => ({
    functionName: 'scheduleMintGroupStart',
    args: [BigInt(id), BigInt(startUnix)],
  }),
  clearSchedule: (id) => ({ functionName: 'scheduleMintGroupStart', args: [BigInt(id), 0n] }),
  // The contract takes the price first: changeSalePrice(newMintPrice, mintGroup).
  price: (id, priceWei) => ({ functionName: 'changeSalePrice', args: [BigInt(priceWei), BigInt(id)] }),
  allocation: (id, maxSupply) => ({
    functionName: 'setMintGroupMaxSupply',
    args: [BigInt(id), BigInt(maxSupply)],
  }),
}

/** The event the confirm looks for in the receipt (section 6.3). */
export function expectedEvent(call: ManageCall): ExpectedEvent {
  switch (call.functionName) {
    case 'registerMintGroup':
      return { eventName: 'MintGroupRegistered', args: { mintGroup: call.args[0] } }
    case 'setMintGroupStatus':
      return { eventName: 'MintGroupStatusChanged', args: { mintGroup: call.args[0], status: call.args[1] } }
    case 'stopOrStartAllMintGroups':
      return { eventName: 'AllMintGroupsStatusChanged', args: { status: call.args[0] } }
    case 'scheduleMintGroupStart':
      return {
        eventName: 'MintGroupStartTimestampChanged',
        args: { mintGroup: call.args[0], timestamp: call.args[1] },
      }
    case 'changeSalePrice':
      return { eventName: 'SalePriceChanged', args: { mintGroup: call.args[1], newPrice: call.args[0] } }
    case 'setMintGroupMaxSupply':
      return {
        eventName: 'MintGroupMaxSupplyUpdated',
        args: { mintGroup: call.args[0], newMaxSupply: call.args[1] },
      }
    case 'setPublicMaxMintPerWallet':
      return { eventName: 'PublicMaxMintPerWalletChanged', args: { newMaxMintPerWallet: call.args[0] } }
  }
}

/** A call as the browser sends it to the confirm action. */
export function sentOf(call: ManageCall): SentCall {
  return {
    functionName: call.functionName,
    args: call.args.map((arg) => (typeof arg === 'boolean' ? arg : arg.toString())),
  }
}

/** A sent call back as viem takes it. The shape is checked first (`parseSentCall`), so every value converts. */
export function callOf(sent: SentCall): ManageCall {
  const uint = (index: number) => BigInt(sent.args[index] as string)
  const bool = (index: number) => sent.args[index] === true
  switch (sent.functionName) {
    case 'registerMintGroup':
      return { functionName: 'registerMintGroup', args: [uint(0), uint(1), uint(2), uint(3)] }
    case 'setMintGroupStatus':
      return { functionName: 'setMintGroupStatus', args: [uint(0), bool(1)] }
    case 'scheduleMintGroupStart':
      return { functionName: 'scheduleMintGroupStart', args: [uint(0), uint(1)] }
    case 'changeSalePrice':
      return { functionName: 'changeSalePrice', args: [uint(0), uint(1)] }
    case 'setMintGroupMaxSupply':
      return { functionName: 'setMintGroupMaxSupply', args: [uint(0), uint(1)] }
    case 'setPublicMaxMintPerWallet':
      return { functionName: 'setPublicMaxMintPerWallet', args: [uint(0)] }
    case 'stopOrStartAllMintGroups':
      return { functionName: 'stopOrStartAllMintGroups', args: [bool(0)] }
  }
}

const PRICE_INPUTS = new Set(['price', 'newMintPrice'])

/**
 * The rows the dialog shows before signing: the ABI's argument names (a trailing underscore dropped) with
 * readable values, a price as the native amount and its wei, a start as UTC and its Unix value.
 */
export function reviewOf(call: ManageCall, chain: SupportedChain): { name: string; value: string }[] {
  const item = getAbiItem({ abi: collectionAbi, name: call.functionName }) as AbiFunction
  return call.args.map((arg, index) => {
    const name = (item.inputs[index]?.name ?? `argument ${index + 1}`).replace(/_$/, '')
    if (typeof arg === 'boolean') return { name, value: String(arg) }
    if (PRICE_INPUTS.has(name))
      return { name, value: `${formatNative(arg, chain.nativeSymbol, 18)} (${arg} wei)` }
    if (name === 'startTimestamp')
      return { name, value: arg === 0n ? '0 (no scheduled start)' : `${formatUtc(Number(arg))} (${arg})` }
    return { name, value: arg.toString() }
  })
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/manage/phases-types.ts`:

```ts
import type { Address } from 'viem'
import type { MintGroup } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import type { ExpectedEvent } from './phase-calls'

/**
 * The shapes Phases moves between the chain, the database, the server and the screens
 * (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 6). Types only, so the browser imports them without the
 * server module (phases-core.ts).
 */

/** One group of `collectionState()`, as the screens read it. */
export type ChainGroup = {
  id: number
  priceWei: string
  maxSupply: number
  minted: number
  startTimestamp: number
  /** The raw open flag. */
  flag: boolean
  /** The contract's `mintGroupActive`: the only source of "live". */
  active: boolean
}

/** `collectionState()` as the screens read it: counts as numbers, prices as decimal strings. */
export type ChainState = {
  /** The block the state was read at. */
  block: bigint
  /** The chain's time at that block (Unix seconds). */
  blockTimestamp: number
  maxSupply: number
  totalMinted: number
  publicMaxMintPerWallet: number
  mintOnBehalfActive: boolean
  groups: ChainGroup[]
}

/** At the latest block, or at `atLeast` when the node is behind it; null when the chain, its RPC or the contract does not answer. */
export type StateReader = (chainId: number, address: Address, atLeast?: bigint) => Promise<ChainState | null>

/** What Phases and the Overview show: the contract's groups joined with what Cobweb keeps (section 6.2). */
export type PhasesView = {
  chainId: number
  contract: Address
  /** In the creator's order; groups the chain lists without a row follow by id, as "Group N". */
  groups: MintGroup[]
  publicMaxPerWallet: number
  totalMinted: number
  maxSupply: number
  /** The chain's time at the read, for relative labels only (never liveness). */
  blockTimestamp: number
  /** The read's block time, or the copy's `state_read_at` when the chain did not answer. */
  readAt: number
  /** False: the groups are the copy, and minted counts and status are unknown. */
  chainReachable: boolean
}

/** What Cobweb records for a phase Add phase registers, once its receipt proves the registration. */
export type PhaseAdd = { onchainId: number; name: string; description: string; visible: boolean }

/** What a confirm must find in the receipt, and the add's row to write when it does. */
export type PhaseTxExpected = { event: ExpectedEvent; add?: PhaseAdd }

/** A `mint_groups` row as the view reads it: numeric columns as text, so no wei is lost. */
export type PhaseRowCopy = {
  onchain_id: number
  name: string
  description: string
  visible: boolean
  /** Null only inside a move. */
  position: number | null
  price_wei: string
  max_supply: number
  start_at: string | null
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/manage/phases-view.ts`:

```ts
import type { Address } from 'viem'
import type { MintGroup } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import type { ChainGroup, ChainState, PhaseRowCopy, PhasesView } from './phases-types'

/**
 * The Phases view, pure (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 6.2): the contract's groups joined with
 * the `mint_groups` rows, the copy when the chain does not answer, what a read copies back, and the
 * screen's local reorder. Status comes only from the chain's `active`.
 */

/** The largest value the copy's integer columns hold (`onchain_id`, `max_supply`, `public_max_per_wallet`). */
export const INT_MAX = 2_147_483_647

const kindOf = (id: number): MintGroup['kind'] => (id === 0 ? 'public' : 'whitelist')

/** Rows in the creator's order: by position, then by id; a row parked inside a move sorts last. */
function byPosition(a: PhaseRowCopy, b: PhaseRowCopy): number {
  const left = a.position ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  const right = b.position ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  return left - right || a.onchain_id - b.onchain_id
}

/** What the chain says about a group: everything but the off-chain fields. */
function fromChain(group: ChainGroup): Omit<MintGroup, 'name' | 'description' | 'visible' | 'named'> {
  return {
    id: group.id,
    kind: kindOf(group.id),
    priceWei: group.priceWei,
    maxSupply: group.maxSupply,
    minted: group.minted,
    flag: group.flag,
    scheduledStart: group.startTimestamp,
    active: group.active,
  }
}

/**
 * The chain's groups with their rows: rows by position, then id, each group taking its row's name,
 * description and visibility; the groups without a row follow by id as "Group N" (`named: false`); a row
 * the chain does not list is left out.
 */
export function joinPhases(state: Pick<ChainState, 'groups'>, rows: PhaseRowCopy[]): MintGroup[] {
  const onChain = new Map(state.groups.map((group) => [group.id, group]))
  const named = [...rows].sort(byPosition).flatMap((row): MintGroup[] => {
    const group = onChain.get(row.onchain_id)
    if (!group) return []
    return [
      {
        ...fromChain(group),
        name: row.name,
        description: row.description,
        visible: row.visible,
        named: true,
      },
    ]
  })
  const rowIds = new Set(rows.map((row) => row.onchain_id))
  const unnamed = state.groups
    .filter((group) => !rowIds.has(group.id))
    .sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id)
    .map((group): MintGroup => ({
      ...fromChain(group),
      name: `Group ${group.id}`,
      description: '',
      visible: true,
      named: false,
    }))
  return [...named, ...unnamed]
}

/** Where the view's groups come from: the deployment's chain and contract, and the rows. */
export type ViewBase = { chainId: number; contract: Address; rows: PhaseRowCopy[] }

/** The view from a read that reached the chain. */
export function liveView(base: ViewBase, state: ChainState): PhasesView {
  return {
    chainId: base.chainId,
    contract: base.contract,
    groups: joinPhases(state, base.rows),
    publicMaxPerWallet: state.publicMaxMintPerWallet,
    totalMinted: state.totalMinted,
    maxSupply: state.maxSupply,
    blockTimestamp: state.blockTimestamp,
    readAt: state.blockTimestamp,
    chainReachable: true,
  }
}

/**
 * The view when the chain does not answer: the rows' copies of price, allocation and start, minted 0 and
 * never live (the screen says status and minted counts are not read), the collection's supply, and the
 * time of the read the copies came from as both `readAt` and `blockTimestamp`.
 */
export function copyView(
  base: ViewBase & { publicMaxPerWallet: number; maxSupply: number; readAt: number },
): PhasesView {
  const groups = [...base.rows].sort(byPosition).map((row): MintGroup => ({
    id: row.onchain_id,
    name: row.name,
    kind: kindOf(row.onchain_id),
    priceWei: row.price_wei,
    maxSupply: row.max_supply,
    minted: 0,
    flag: false,
    scheduledStart: row.start_at === null ? 0 : Math.floor(Date.parse(row.start_at) / 1000),
    active: false,
    description: row.description,
    visible: row.visible,
    named: true,
  }))
  return {
    chainId: base.chainId,
    contract: base.contract,
    groups,
    publicMaxPerWallet: base.publicMaxPerWallet,
    totalMinted: 0,
    maxSupply: base.maxSupply,
    blockTimestamp: base.readAt,
    readAt: base.readAt,
    chainReachable: false,
  }
}

/** What `mint_groups_refresh` copies from a read: `start` in Unix seconds, 0 for none. */
export type RefreshPayload = {
  publicMaxPerWallet: number
  groups: { onchain_id: number; price_wei: string; max_supply: number; start: number }[]
}

/**
 * The copies a read refreshes, or null when an id, an allocation or the public limit set outside Cobweb
 * does not fit the integer columns: then that read is not copied at all (section 12).
 */
export function refreshPayload(state: ChainState): RefreshPayload | null {
  const fits = (value: number) => Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value <= INT_MAX
  if (
    !fits(state.publicMaxMintPerWallet) ||
    state.groups.some((group) => !fits(group.id) || !fits(group.maxSupply))
  )
    return null
  return {
    publicMaxPerWallet: state.publicMaxMintPerWallet,
    groups: state.groups.map((group) => ({
      onchain_id: group.id,
      price_wei: group.priceWei,
      max_supply: group.maxSupply,
      start: group.startTimestamp,
    })),
  }
}

/**
 * Move up and Move down as the screen shows them before the server answers (section 7.4): a named phase
 * swaps with its named neighbour, as `mint_group_move` swaps their positions; phases without a row stay
 * last; past either end nothing moves.
 */
export function moveGroup(groups: MintGroup[], groupId: number, direction: 'up' | 'down'): MintGroup[] {
  const named = groups.filter((group) => group.named !== false)
  const unnamed = groups.filter((group) => group.named === false)
  const from = named.findIndex((group) => group.id === groupId)
  const to = from + (direction === 'up' ? -1 : 1)
  if (from < 0 || to < 0 || to >= named.length) return groups
  const moved = [...named]
  const [entry] = moved.splice(from, 1)
  if (!entry) return groups
  moved.splice(to, 0, entry)
  return [...moved, ...unnamed]
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/chain/phase-events.ts`:

```ts
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import { decodeEventLog, type Address } from 'viem'
import { NOT_FROM_WALLET, NOT_THIS_CHANGE, NOT_THIS_CONTRACT } from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { ExpectedEvent } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import type { ReceiptLike } from './art-paid'

/** What `matchPhaseEvent` reads of a transaction receipt. viem's `TransactionReceipt` fits it. */
export type PhaseReceipt = ReceiptLike & { from: Address; to: Address | null }

export type PhaseEventMatch = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }

/** Whether every wanted argument equals the decoded one (Close all names only its status). */
function sameArgs(decoded: Record<string, unknown>, wanted: Record<string, bigint | boolean>): boolean {
  return Object.entries(wanted).every(([name, value]) => decoded[name] === value)
}

/**
 * Checks that a receipt made the change the creator asked for (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section
 * 6.3): sent to the collection (`NOT_THIS_CONTRACT`), from the sign-in wallet (`NOT_FROM_WALLET`), and
 * carrying, among the logs the collection emitted, the expected event with the expected arguments
 * (`NOT_THIS_CHANGE`). Addresses compare without case. The confirm checks a reverted receipt first.
 */
export function matchPhaseEvent(
  receipt: PhaseReceipt,
  expected: { contract: Address; from: string; event: ExpectedEvent },
): PhaseEventMatch {
  const contract = expected.contract.toLowerCase()
  if (receipt.to?.toLowerCase() !== contract) return { ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_CONTRACT }
  if (receipt.from.toLowerCase() !== expected.from.toLowerCase()) return { ok: false, error: NOT_FROM_WALLET }
  if (receipt.status !== 'success') return { ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_CHANGE }
  for (const log of receipt.logs) {
    if (log.address.toLowerCase() !== contract) continue
    try {
      const event = decodeEventLog({ abi: collectionAbi, data: log.data, topics: log.topics })
      // viem decodes any event of the ABI by its topic, so the name is compared here.
      if (event.eventName !== expected.event.eventName) continue
      if (sameArgs(event.args as Record<string, unknown>, expected.event.args)) return { ok: true }
    } catch {
      // A log the collection's ABI does not decode.
    }
  }
  return { ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_CHANGE }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/manage/phase-schemas.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod'
import {
  PHASE_DESCRIPTION_MAX,
  PHASE_NAME_EMPTY,
  PHASE_NAME_LONG,
  PHASE_NAME_MAX,
} from '@/components/manage/phase-rules'
import { DESCRIPTION_TOO_LONG, UNSAVED } from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { SentCall } from './phase-calls'
import { INT_MAX } from './phases-view'

/**
 * What the Phases actions accept from the browser (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 6.4), checked
 * before the session and owner read. A malformed value means the page is out of date (UNSAVED); a name or
 * a description reads as the screen's own sentences.
 */

const UINT256_MAX = 2n ** 256n - 1n

/** A group id as a number: the copy's integer column holds 0 to 2,147,483,647. */
export const onchainIdShape = z.number({ error: UNSAVED }).int(UNSAVED).min(0, UNSAVED).max(INT_MAX, UNSAVED)
export const directionShape = z.enum(['up', 'down'], { error: UNSAVED })
export const visibleShape = z.boolean({ error: UNSAVED })

const nameShape = z
  .string({ error: PHASE_NAME_EMPTY })
  .trim()
  .min(1, PHASE_NAME_EMPTY)
  .max(PHASE_NAME_MAX, PHASE_NAME_LONG)
const descriptionShape = z.string({ error: UNSAVED }).trim().max(PHASE_DESCRIPTION_MAX, DESCRIPTION_TOO_LONG)

/** Edit details: the name trimmed with the screen's sentences, the description at most 500 characters. */
export const phaseDetailsShape = z.object(
  { name: nameShape, description: descriptionShape },
  { error: UNSAVED },
)

/** Add phase's off-chain fields: the id is 1 or more, since 0 is always the public sale. */
export const phaseAddShape = z.object(
  {
    onchainId: onchainIdShape.min(1, UNSAVED),
    name: nameShape,
    description: descriptionShape,
    visible: visibleShape,
  },
  { error: UNSAVED },
)

// One refine each: zod runs every check of a string, so a separate regex would not stop BigInt('1.5').
const idText = z.string().refine((value) => /^\d{1,10}$/.test(value) && Number(value) <= INT_MAX)
const uintText = z.string().refine((value) => /^\d{1,78}$/.test(value) && BigInt(value) <= UINT256_MAX)

/** A sent call: ids 0 to 2,147,483,647, every uint256 a decimal string, in each function's argument order. */
export const sentCallShape = z.discriminatedUnion('functionName', [
  z.object({
    functionName: z.literal('registerMintGroup'),
    args: z.tuple([idText, uintText, uintText, uintText]),
  }),
  z.object({ functionName: z.literal('setMintGroupStatus'), args: z.tuple([idText, z.boolean()]) }),
  z.object({ functionName: z.literal('scheduleMintGroupStart'), args: z.tuple([idText, uintText]) }),
  z.object({ functionName: z.literal('changeSalePrice'), args: z.tuple([uintText, idText]) }),
  z.object({ functionName: z.literal('setMintGroupMaxSupply'), args: z.tuple([idText, uintText]) }),
  z.object({ functionName: z.literal('setPublicMaxMintPerWallet'), args: z.tuple([uintText]) }),
  z.object({ functionName: z.literal('stopOrStartAllMintGroups'), args: z.tuple([z.boolean()]) }),
])

/** A sent call from the browser, or UNSAVED whatever is wrong with it. */
export function parseSentCall(input: unknown): { ok: true; data: SentCall } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  const result = sentCallShape.safeParse(input)
  return result.success ? { ok: true, data: result.data } : { ok: false, error: UNSAVED }
}
```

```ts
/**
 * The launch phases as the constructor took them (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.5): prices and
 * allocations from `deployments.args`, each name from its `mint_groups` row by `onchain_id`, "Group N"
 * without one. Phases changes the live values and the copies; the deployed view keeps showing what landed.
 */
export function launchPhasesOf(
  args: Pick<PreparedArgs, 'groupPrices' | 'groupSupplies'>,
  rows: { onchain_id: number; name: string }[],
): DeployRecordGroup[] {

    name: rows.find((row) => row.onchain_id === index)?.name ?? `Group ${index}`,
    priceWei,
    maxSupply: Number(args.groupSupplies[index] ?? '0'),
  }))
}
```

```ts
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import { publicClientFor } from '@cobweb/shared/server'
import { getAddress, TransactionReceiptNotFoundError, type Address, type Hex, type PublicClient } from 'viem'
import { errorSummary } from '@/lib/art/lock-core'
import {
  CHAIN_NOT_READY,
  CONTRACT_UNREADABLE,
  NAME_FIRST,
  NOT_DEPLOYED,
  PHASE_NOT_CONFIRMED,
  PHASE_NOT_ON_CHAIN,
  PHASE_REVERTED,
  PUBLIC_SHOWN,
} from '@/lib/art/result'
import { matchPhaseEvent } from '@/lib/chain/phase-events'
import type { Json } from '@/lib/database.types'
import type { DeployContext, DeployOutcome } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-core'
import type { ChainState, PhaseRowCopy, PhasesView, PhaseTxExpected, StateReader } from './phases-types'
import { copyView, liveView, refreshPayload } from './phases-view'

/**
 * Phases on the server (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 6): the chain read, the view, the confirm
 * of an on-chain change and the off-chain edits, on a Supabase client acting as the signed-in creator. The
 * actions (app/actions/phases.ts) call these after the session and ownership check; verify:phases calls
 * them with a signed-in script client. No `server-only` marker, so the script can import it; the browser
 * never does. The server writes the chain's copies only from its own read, never from browser values.
 */
export type PhasesContext = DeployContext

/**
 * `collectionState()` at the latest block, or at `atLeast` when the node is behind it, through the
 * server's client for the chain. A failure is logged and answers null, as `readCollectionFees` does.
 */
export const readCollectionState: StateReader = async (chainId, address, atLeast) => {
  try {
    const client = publicClientFor(chainId)
    // cacheTime 0: viem reuses a block number for the poll interval, which can read older than a receipt.
    const latest = await client.getBlockNumber({ cacheTime: 0 })
    const block = atLeast !== undefined && atLeast > latest ? atLeast : latest
    const state = await client.readContract({
      address,
      abi: collectionAbi,
      functionName: 'collectionState',
      blockNumber: block,
    })
    return {
      block,
      blockTimestamp: Number(state.blockTimestamp),
      maxSupply: Number(state.maxSupply),
      totalMinted: Number(state.totalMinted),
      publicMaxMintPerWallet: Number(state.publicMaxMintPerWallet),
      mintOnBehalfActive: state.mintOnBehalfActive,
      groups: state.groups.map((group) => ({
        id: Number(group.id),
        priceWei: group.price.toString(),
        maxSupply: Number(group.maxSupply),
        minted: Number(group.minted),
        startTimestamp: Number(group.startTimestamp),
        flag: group.activeFlag,
        active: group.active,
      })),
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`The collection state could not be read on chain ${chainId}: ${errorSummary(error)}`)
    return null
  }
}

/** The mint_groups columns the view reads, the numeric one as text. */
const PHASE_ROW_COLUMNS =
  'onchain_id, name, description, visible, position, price_wei::text, max_supply, start_at'

/** What every Phases step starts from: the deployment and the rows, under the creator's client. */
type PhaseBase = {
  chainId: number
  contract: Address
  /** The copy of the public limit. */
  publicMaxPerWallet: number
  /** The copies' read time, or the deploy time before any refresh (Unix seconds). */
  readAt: number
  rows: PhaseRowCopy[]
}

/** The collection's deployment and its rows, or null before deploy (and for a stranger, under the row policies). */
async function phaseBase(ctx: PhasesContext): Promise<PhaseBase | null> {
  const { data, error } = await ctx.supabase
    .from('deployments')
    .select('chain_id, contract_address, public_max_per_wallet, deployed_at, state_read_at')
    .eq('collection_id', ctx.collection.id)
    .maybeSingle()
  if (error) throw new Error(`The deployment could not be read: ${error.message}`)
  if (!data) return null
  const rows = await ctx.supabase
    .from('mint_groups')
    .select(PHASE_ROW_COLUMNS)
    .eq('collection_id', ctx.collection.id)
  if (rows.error) throw new Error(`The phases could not be read: ${rows.error.message}`)
  return {
    chainId: data.chain_id,
    contract: getAddress(data.contract_address),
    publicMaxPerWallet: data.public_max_per_wallet,
    readAt: Math.floor(Date.parse(data.state_read_at ?? data.deployed_at) / 1000),
    rows: (rows.data ?? []) as unknown as PhaseRowCopy[],
  }
}

/**
 * Writes the copies from a read through `mint_groups_refresh` with the read's block, so a slower read
 * never overwrites a newer one. A value too large for its column, or a refresh that fails, is logged
 * and never fails the view.
 */
async function refreshCopies(ctx: PhasesContext, state: ChainState): Promise<void> {
  const payload = refreshPayload(state)
  if (!payload) {
    console.error(
      `The phases of ${ctx.collection.id} hold a value too large to copy; the copy keeps its last read.`,
    )
    return
  }
  try {
    const { error } = await ctx.service().rpc('mint_groups_refresh', {
      p_collection_id: ctx.collection.id,
      p_owner: ctx.owner,
      p_block: Number(state.block),
      p_block_time: new Date(state.blockTimestamp * 1000).toISOString(),
      p_public_max_per_wallet: payload.publicMaxPerWallet,
      p_groups: payload.groups as unknown as Json,
    })
    if (error)
      console.error(
        `The phase copies could not be refreshed for ${ctx.collection.id}: ${error.code} ${error.message}`,
      )
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`The phase copies could not be refreshed for ${ctx.collection.id}: ${errorSummary(error)}`)
  }
}

/** The view from a read (the copies refreshed from it), or the copy when the chain did not answer. */
async function viewOf(ctx: PhasesContext, base: PhaseBase, state: ChainState | null): Promise<PhasesView> {
  if (!state)
    return copyView({
      chainId: base.chainId,
      contract: base.contract,
      rows: base.rows,
      publicMaxPerWallet: base.publicMaxPerWallet,
      maxSupply: ctx.collection.max_supply ?? 0,
      readAt: base.readAt,
    })
  await refreshCopies(ctx, state)
  return liveView({ chainId: base.chainId, contract: base.contract, rows: base.rows }, state)
}

/** What Phases and the Overview show (section 6.2): the chain's groups joined with their rows. */
export async function phasesViewCore(
  ctx: PhasesContext,
  read: StateReader = readCollectionState,
): Promise<DeployOutcome<PhasesView>> {
  const base = await phaseBase(ctx)
  if (!base) return { refused: NOT_DEPLOYED }
  return { data: await viewOf(ctx, base, await read(base.chainId, base.contract)) }
}

/**
 * Confirms an on-chain change from its receipt (section 6.3), as `confirmDeployCore` without the depth: the
 * receipt must have landed, succeeded, been sent from the sign-in wallet to the collection and carry the
 * expected event. An add's row is written now and only now. Then the chain is read again at the receipt's
 * block or later, the copies refreshed, and the fresh view answered. A second confirm of the same hash
 * answers the view again.
 */
export async function confirmPhaseTxCore(
  ctx: PhasesContext,
  txHash: Hex,
  expected: PhaseTxExpected,
  read: StateReader = readCollectionState,
): Promise<DeployOutcome<PhasesView>> {
  const base = await phaseBase(ctx)
  if (!base) return { refused: NOT_DEPLOYED }
  const hash = txHash.toLowerCase() as Hex
  let client: PublicClient
  try {
    client = publicClientFor(base.chainId)
  } catch {
    return { refused: CHAIN_NOT_READY }
  }
  let receipt: Awaited<ReturnType<PublicClient['getTransactionReceipt']>>
  try {
    receipt = await client.getTransactionReceipt({ hash })
  } catch (error) {
    // Not mined yet, or not visible to this node yet: the machine asks again in 3 seconds.
    if (!(error instanceof TransactionReceiptNotFoundError))
      console.error(`The receipt could not be read: ${errorSummary(error)}`)
    return { refused: PHASE_NOT_CONFIRMED }
  }
  // sic: viem types the receipt as always present; a node can still answer with nothing.
  if (!receipt) return { refused: PHASE_NOT_CONFIRMED }
  if (receipt.status === 'reverted') return { refused: PHASE_REVERTED }
  const match = matchPhaseEvent(receipt, { contract: base.contract, from: ctx.wallet, event: expected.event })
  if (!match.ok) return { refused: match.error }
  let current = base
  if (expected.add) {
    const recorded = await ctx.service().rpc('mint_group_record', {
      p_collection_id: ctx.collection.id,
      p_owner: ctx.owner,
      p_onchain_id: expected.add.onchainId,
      p_name: expected.add.name,
      p_description: expected.add.description,
      p_visible: expected.add.visible,
    })
    if (recorded.error) return { error: recorded.error }
    current = (await phaseBase(ctx)) ?? base
  }
  return { data: await viewOf(ctx, current, await read(base.chainId, base.contract, receipt.blockNumber)) }
}

/**
 * Edit details (section 6.4): the row's name and description under the creator's client, or, for a group
 * the chain lists without a row (registered outside Cobweb), a row recorded through `mint_group_record`.
 */
export async function renamePhaseCore(
  ctx: PhasesContext,
  onchainId: number,
  details: { name: string; description: string },
  read: StateReader = readCollectionState,
): Promise<DeployOutcome<undefined>> {
  const base = await phaseBase(ctx)
  if (!base) return { refused: NOT_DEPLOYED }
  if (base.rows.some((row) => row.onchain_id === onchainId)) {
    const { error } = await ctx.supabase
      .from('mint_groups')
      .update({ name: details.name, description: details.description })
      .eq('collection_id', ctx.collection.id)
      .eq('onchain_id', onchainId)
    return error ? { error } : { data: undefined }
  }
  const state = await read(base.chainId, base.contract)
  if (!state) return { refused: CONTRACT_UNREADABLE }
  if (!state.groups.some((group) => group.id === onchainId)) return { refused: PHASE_NOT_ON_CHAIN }
  const recorded = await ctx.service().rpc('mint_group_record', {
    p_collection_id: ctx.collection.id,
    p_owner: ctx.owner,
    p_onchain_id: onchainId,
    p_name: details.name,
    p_description: details.description,
    p_visible: true,
  })
  return recorded.error ? { error: recorded.error } : { data: undefined }
}

/** Hide from or show on the mint page (section 6.4). The public sale is always shown; a group needs its row. */
export async function setPhaseVisibilityCore(
  ctx: PhasesContext,
  onchainId: number,
  visible: boolean,
): Promise<DeployOutcome<undefined>> {
  if (onchainId === 0) return { refused: PUBLIC_SHOWN }
  const base = await phaseBase(ctx)
  if (!base) return { refused: NOT_DEPLOYED }
  if (!base.rows.some((row) => row.onchain_id === onchainId)) return { refused: NAME_FIRST }
  const { error } = await ctx.supabase
    .from('mint_groups')
    .update({ visible })
    .eq('collection_id', ctx.collection.id)
    .eq('onchain_id', onchainId)
  return error ? { error } : { data: undefined }
}

/** Move up or Move down (section 6.4): `mint_group_move` under the creator's client; past either end nothing changes. */
export async function movePhaseCore(
  ctx: PhasesContext,
  onchainId: number,
  direction: 'up' | 'down',
): Promise<DeployOutcome<undefined>> {
  const base = await phaseBase(ctx)
  if (!base) return { refused: NOT_DEPLOYED }
  if (!base.rows.some((row) => row.onchain_id === onchainId)) return { refused: NAME_FIRST }
  const { error } = await ctx.supabase.rpc('mint_group_move', {
    p_collection_id: ctx.collection.id,
    p_onchain_id: onchainId,
    p_up: direction === 'up',
  })
  return error ? { error } : { data: undefined }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The write helper**

Create `apps/web/src/lib/manage/phases-write.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import { phasesWriteError } from '@/lib/art/db-error'
import { errorSummary } from '@/lib/art/lock-core'
import { ownedCollection } from '@/lib/art/owned'
import { fail, THROWN, type ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { DeployOutcome } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-core'
import { createServiceClient } from '@/lib/supabase/service'
import type { PhasesContext } from './phases-core'

/**
 * Runs a Phases step for the signed-in owner of a collection (deployWrite's counterpart for Phases;
 * docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 6.4). Two clients: the creator's RLS client for the session
 * check, the owner read, every read and the off-chain edits, and the service client for
 * `mint_group_record` and `mint_groups_refresh`, whose arguments come from a verified receipt or the
 * server's own chain read, called with the verified user's id as `p_owner`. The service client is built
 * on first use, after the ownership check. A refusal is returned as its sentence; a database error becomes
 * the creator's sentence (`phasesWriteError`); anything thrown is logged and reads "Try again".
 */
export async function phasesWrite<T>(
  collectionId: string,
  run: (ctx: PhasesContext) => Promise<DeployOutcome<T>>,
): Promise<ActionResult<T>> {
  try {
    const owned = await ownedCollection(collectionId)
    if (owned.error !== undefined) return fail(owned.error)
    const outcome = await run({
      supabase: owned.supabase,
      service: createServiceClient,
      owner: owned.user.id,
      wallet: owned.user.walletAddress,
      collection: owned.collection,
    })
    if ('refused' in outcome) return fail(outcome.refused)
    if ('error' in outcome) return fail(phasesWriteError(outcome.error))
    return { ok: true, data: outcome.data }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`A phases step threw. ${errorSummary(error)}`)
    return fail(THROWN)
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The actions**

Create `apps/web/src/app/actions/phases.ts`:

```ts
'use server'

import { cache } from 'react'
import { txHash as txHashSchema } from '@/lib/art/lock-schemas'
import type { ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { parse } from '@/lib/art/schemas'
import { callOf, expectedEvent, type SentCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import {
  directionShape,
  onchainIdShape,
  parseSentCall,
  phaseAddShape,
  phaseDetailsShape,
  visibleShape,
} from '@/lib/manage/phase-schemas'
import {
  confirmPhaseTxCore,
  movePhaseCore,
  phasesViewCore,
  readCollectionState,
  renamePhaseCore,
  setPhaseVisibilityCore,
} from '@/lib/manage/phases-core'
import type { PhaseAdd, PhasesView } from '@/lib/manage/phases-types'
import { phasesWrite } from '@/lib/manage/phases-write'

/**
 * Phases' server actions (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 6.4). Each checks its input first, then
 * runs through `phasesWrite`, which checks the session and the ownership (CLAUDE.md rule 4). The browser
 * never writes an on-chain value: a confirm answers the view the server read from the chain.
 */

/** One chain read per request, however many times a page asks (React's cache). */
const readOnce = cache(readCollectionState)

/** The contract's groups joined with their rows, read on the server. */
export async function getPhases(collectionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<PhasesView>> {
  return phasesWrite(collectionId, (ctx) => phasesViewCore(ctx, readOnce))
}

/** Confirms an on-chain change from its receipt, then answers the fresh view. */
export async function confirmPhaseTx(
  collectionId: string,
  txHash: string,
  sent: SentCall,
): Promise<ActionResult<PhasesView>> {
  const hash = parse(txHashSchema, txHash)
  if (!hash.ok) return hash
  const call = parseSentCall(sent)
  if (!call.ok) return call
  return phasesWrite(collectionId, (ctx) =>
    confirmPhaseTxCore(ctx, hash.data, { event: expectedEvent(callOf(call.data)) }),
  )
}

/** Confirms Add phase from its receipt's MintGroupRegistered log, records the phase's row, then answers the view. */
export async function recordPhase(
  collectionId: string,
  txHash: string,
  add: PhaseAdd,
): Promise<ActionResult<PhasesView>> {
  const hash = parse(txHashSchema, txHash)
  if (!hash.ok) return hash
  const input = parse(phaseAddShape, add)
  if (!input.ok) return input
  return phasesWrite(collectionId, (ctx) =>
    confirmPhaseTxCore(ctx, hash.data, {
      event: { eventName: 'MintGroupRegistered', args: { mintGroup: BigInt(input.data.onchainId) } },
      add: input.data,
    }),
  )
}

/** Edit details: the name and the description, or the first name of a group registered outside Cobweb. */
export async function renamePhase(
  collectionId: string,
  onchainId: number,
  details: { name: string; description: string },
): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const id = parse(onchainIdShape, onchainId)
  if (!id.ok) return id
  const input = parse(phaseDetailsShape, details)
  if (!input.ok) return input
  return phasesWrite(collectionId, (ctx) => renamePhaseCore(ctx, id.data, input.data, readOnce))
}

/** Hide from or show on the mint page. */
export async function setPhaseVisibility(
  collectionId: string,
  onchainId: number,
  visible: boolean,
): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const id = parse(onchainIdShape, onchainId)
  if (!id.ok) return id
  const shown = parse(visibleShape, visible)
  if (!shown.ok) return shown
  return phasesWrite(collectionId, (ctx) => setPhaseVisibilityCore(ctx, id.data, shown.data))
}

/** Move up or Move down in the creator's order, which the mint page follows. */
export async function movePhase(
  collectionId: string,
  onchainId: number,
  direction: 'up' | 'down',
): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const id = parse(onchainIdShape, onchainId)
  if (!id.ok) return id
  const towards = parse(directionShape, direction)
  if (!towards.ok) return towards
  return phasesWrite(collectionId, (ctx) => movePhaseCore(ctx, id.data, towards.data))
}
```

```ts
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import {
  ContractFunctionExecutionError,
  ContractFunctionRevertedError,
  encodeErrorResult,
  type Address,
} from 'viem'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { classifyError } from './tx-errors'

describe('classifyError', () => {
  it('answers a failure for a rejection that is not an error, instead of throwing inside a catch', () => {
    const failure = classifyError(undefined, 'wallet', 'deploy')
    expect(failure).toMatchObject({ kind: 'rejected', title: 'The wallet did not send the deploy' })
    expect(failure.message).toBe('Unknown error. No gas was spent.')
    expect(classifyError(null, 'switching', 'deploy')).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'switch',
      title: 'The network switch failed',
    })
    expect(classifyError(null, 'confirming', 'deploy').message).toBe(
      "Unknown error. Check your wallet's activity before you deploy again.",
    )
  })

  it('reads a message from a plain object as well as from an Error', () => {
    expect(classifyError({ message: 'Timed out.' }, 'confirming', 'deploy').message).toBe(
      "Timed out. Check your wallet's activity before you deploy again.",
    )
    expect(classifyError(new Error('Timed out.'), 'wallet', 'payment').message).toBe(
      'Timed out. Nothing was charged.',
    )
  })
})

const CONTRACT: Address = '0x0409454C375c8a6aB05383bFB6D9FcDe9ebB8F85'
const STRANGER: Address = '0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC'

/** A simulation revert as wagmi throws it: the decoded custom error inside the call's execution error. */
function simulationRevert(errorName: string, args: readonly unknown[]): ContractFunctionExecutionError {
  const data = encodeErrorResult({ abi: collectionAbi, errorName, args } as never)
  const reverted = new ContractFunctionRevertedError({
    abi: collectionAbi,
    data,
    functionName: 'setMintGroupMaxSupply',
  })
  return new ContractFunctionExecutionError(reverted, {
    abi: collectionAbi,
    functionName: 'setMintGroupMaxSupply',
    args: [0n, 0n],
    contractAddress: CONTRACT,
  })
}

describe('classifyError for a change in Manage drop (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.2)', () => {
  it.each<[string, readonly unknown[], string]>([
    ['BelowMinted', [1n], 'It cannot go below 1, the tokens this phase has already minted.'],
    ['BelowMinted', [1234n], 'It cannot go below 1,234, the tokens this phase has already minted.'],
    [
      'AllocationsExceedMaxSupply',
      [],
      "The phases' allocations together would be more than the collection's supply. Lower another phase first.",
    ],
    [
      'MintGroupAlreadyExists',
      [2n],
      'Phase 2 is already on the contract. Read the contract again, then add the phase.',
    ],
    ['MintGroupDoesNotExist', [9n], 'Phase 9 is not on the contract. Read the contract again.'],
    ['AddressZero', [], 'The address cannot be the zero address.'],
    [
      'Unauthorized',
      [STRANGER],
      `Only the contract's owner can make this change, and ${STRANGER} is not the owner.`,
    ],
    [
      'OwnableUnauthorizedAccount',
      [STRANGER],
      `Only the contract's owner can make this change, and ${STRANGER} is not the owner.`,
    ],
  ])('words %s (%s) from the decoded revert, ending "Nothing was sent."', (errorName, args, sentence) => {
    expect(classifyError(simulationRevert(errorName, args), 'simulating', 'manage')).toEqual({
      kind: 'reverted',
      title: 'The change would fail',
      message: `${sentence} Nothing was sent.`,
    })
  })

  it('falls back to the short message for any other revert, and keeps the factory wording for payment and deploy', () => {
    expect(
      classifyError(simulationRevert('ExceedsMaxSupply', [5n, 1n]), 'simulating', 'manage').message,
    ).toBe(
      'The check against the contract failed: The contract function "setMintGroupMaxSupply" reverted. Nothing was sent.',
    )
    expect(classifyError(new Error('fetch failed'), 'simulating', 'manage').message).toBe(
      'The check against the contract failed: fetch failed Nothing was sent.',
    )
    expect(classifyError(simulationRevert('BelowMinted', [1n]), 'simulating', 'deploy').message).toMatch(
      /^The check against the factory failed: /,
    )
  })

  it('words the wallet steps for a change', () => {
    const rejection = Object.assign(new Error('User rejected the request.'), { code: 4001 })
    expect(classifyError(rejection, 'wallet', 'manage')).toEqual({
      kind: 'rejected',
      title: 'Cancelled in your wallet',
      message: 'Nothing was sent and no gas was spent. Try again when you are ready.',
    })
    expect(classifyError(rejection, 'switching', 'manage')).toEqual({
      kind: 'switch',
      title: 'Network switch cancelled',
      message:
        'Your wallet stayed on its current network, so nothing was sent. Try again and approve the switch.',
    })
    expect(
      classifyError(new Error('insufficient funds for gas * price + value'), 'simulating', 'manage'),
    ).toMatchObject({ kind: 'balance', title: 'Not enough funds for gas' })
    expect(classifyError(new Error('Timed out.'), 'wallet', 'manage')).toEqual({
      kind: 'rejected',
      title: 'The wallet did not send the change',
      message: 'Timed out. No gas was spent.',
    })
    expect(classifyError(new Error('Timed out.'), 'confirming', 'manage')).toEqual({
      kind: 'reverted',
      title: 'The change could not be confirmed',
      message: "Timed out. Check your wallet's activity before you send it again.",
    })
  })
})
```
