Create `apps/web/src/components/manage/manage-machine.test.ts`:

```ts
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import { ContractFunctionRevertedError, encodeErrorResult, type Address, type Hex } from 'viem'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { deployReducer, retryHash, type DeployEvent, type KeyStore } from '@/components/deploy/deploy-machine'
import type { TxPhase } from '@/components/deploy/tx-progress'
import {
  CHAIN_NOT_READY,
  NOT_FOUND,
  NOT_FROM_WALLET,
  NOT_THIS_CHANGE,
  NOT_THIS_CONTRACT,
  PHASE_NAME_TAKEN,
  PHASE_NOT_CONFIRMED,
  PHASE_REVERTED,
  PHASE_TIMED_OUT,
  SIGNED_OUT,
  THROWN,
  UNSAVED,
  type ActionResult,
} from '@/lib/art/result'
import { phaseCalls, type ManageCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import {
  forgetAdd,
  pendingAdd,
  rememberAdd,
  runManage,
  type ManageDeps,
  type ManageInput,
  type PendingAdd,
} from './manage-machine'

const WALLET: Address = '0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8'
const STRANGER: Address = '0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC'
const HASH: Hex = `0x${'ab'.repeat(32)}`
const VIEW = 'the fresh view'
const NOT_YET: ActionResult<string> = { ok: false, error: PHASE_NOT_CONFIRMED }

const input: ManageInput = {
  call: phaseCalls.open(1),
  account: WALLET.toLowerCase(),
  wallet: WALLET,
  chainId: 31337,
  connectedChainId: 31337,
}

function fakeDeps(confirms: ActionResult<string>[], overrides: Partial<ManageDeps<string>> = {}) {
  let clock = 0
  const calls = {
    switchChain: [] as number[],
    simulated: [] as (ManageCall | null)[],
    send: 0,
    confirm: 0,
    remember: [] as Hex[],
    forget: 0,
    sleeps: [] as number[],
  }
  const deps: ManageDeps<string> = {
    switchChain: async (chainId) => {
      calls.switchChain.push(chainId)
    },
    simulate: async (call) => {
      calls.simulated.push(call)
      return async () => {
        calls.send++
        return HASH
      }
    },
    wait: async () => 'success',
    confirm: async () => {
      calls.confirm++
      return confirms.shift() ?? NOT_YET
    },
    remember: (hash) => {
      calls.remember.push(hash)
    },
    forget: () => {
      calls.forget++
    },
    sleep: async (ms) => {
      calls.sleeps.push(ms)
      clock += ms
    },
    now: () => clock,
    ...overrides,
  }
  return { deps, calls }
}

async function run(deps: ManageDeps<string>, manageInput: ManageInput = input) {
  const events: DeployEvent[] = []
  let phase: TxPhase = { step: 'idle' }
  const result = await runManage(manageInput, deps, (event) => {
    events.push(event)
    phase = deployReducer(phase, event)
  })
  return { result, events, last: events[events.length - 1], phase }
}

describe('runManage', () => {
  it('simulates, sends, waits, then polls the server every 3 seconds until it confirms, and answers the view', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([NOT_YET, NOT_YET, { ok: true, data: VIEW }])
    const { result, events } = await run(deps)
    expect(events.map((event) => event.type)).toEqual([
      'simulating',
      'wallet',
      'confirming',
      'verifying',
      'success',
    ])
    expect(calls.simulated).toEqual([phaseCalls.open(1)])
    expect(calls.switchChain).toEqual([])
    expect(calls.sleeps).toEqual([3_000, 3_000])
    expect(result).toBe(VIEW)
  })

  it('switches the wallet first when it is on another chain', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: true, data: VIEW }])
    const { events } = await run(deps, { ...input, connectedChainId: 1 })
    expect(events.map((event) => event.type)).toEqual([
      'switching',
      'simulating',
      'wallet',
      'confirming',
      'verifying',
      'success',
    ])
    expect(calls.switchChain).toEqual([31337])
  })

  it('never opens the wallet without an account, or for an account that is not the sign-in wallet', async () => {
    const noWallet = fakeDeps([])
    const first = await run(noWallet.deps, { ...input, account: undefined })
    expect(noWallet.calls.simulated).toEqual([])
    expect(first.last).toEqual({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: {
        kind: 'refused',
        title: 'Connect your wallet',
        message: 'Connect your wallet to make this change. Nothing was sent.',
      },
    })

    const other = fakeDeps([])
    const second = await run(other.deps, { ...input, account: STRANGER })
    expect(other.calls.simulated).toEqual([])
    expect(other.calls.switchChain).toEqual([])
    expect(second.last).toEqual({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: {
        kind: 'refused',
        title: 'Use your sign-in wallet',
        message: `Cobweb checks changes sent from ${WALLET}, your sign-in wallet. Switch to that account in your wallet, then try again.`,
      },
    })
  })

  it('a rejected switch simulates nothing and sends nothing', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([], {
      switchChain: async () => {
        throw Object.assign(new Error('User rejected the request.'), { code: 4001 })
      },
    })
    const { last, phase } = await run(deps, { ...input, connectedChainId: 1 })
    expect(calls.simulated).toEqual([])
    expect(calls.send).toBe(0)
    expect(last).toMatchObject({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: { kind: 'switch', title: 'Network switch cancelled' },
    })
    expect(phase).toMatchObject({ step: 'failed', at: 'switching' })
  })

  it("a simulation revert sends nothing and reads as the contract's sentence", async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([], {
      simulate: async () => {
        throw new ContractFunctionRevertedError({
          abi: collectionAbi,
          data: encodeErrorResult({ abi: collectionAbi, errorName: 'BelowMinted', args: [1n] }),
          functionName: 'setMintGroupMaxSupply',
        })
      },
    })
    const { last, phase } = await run(deps, { ...input, call: phaseCalls.allocation(0, 0) })
    expect(calls.send).toBe(0)
    expect(calls.remember).toEqual([])
    expect(last).toEqual({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: {
        kind: 'reverted',
        title: 'The change would fail',
        message: 'It cannot go below 1, the tokens this phase has already minted. Nothing was sent.',
      },
    })
    expect(phase).toMatchObject({ step: 'failed', at: 'simulating' })
  })

  it('a rejection in the wallet reads as cancelled and remembers nothing', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([], {
      simulate: async () => async () => {
        throw Object.assign(new Error('User rejected the request.'), { code: 4001 })
      },
    })
    const { last } = await run(deps)
    expect(last).toMatchObject({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: { kind: 'rejected', title: 'Cancelled in your wallet' },
    })
    expect(calls.remember).toEqual([])
  })

  it('reports a change that reverted on chain and forgets a remembered add', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([], { wait: async () => 'reverted' })
    const { last, result } = await run(deps, { ...input, call: phaseCalls.register(2, '0', 1, 0), addId: 2 })
    expect(result).toBeNull()
    expect(calls.remember).toEqual([HASH])
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(1)
    expect(last).toEqual({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: {
        kind: 'reverted',
        title: 'The change failed on chain',
        message:
          'The transaction was included but reverted, so nothing changed. You paid gas only. Try again.',
      },
    })
  })

  it('a wait that throws keeps the sent hash to check again', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([], {
      wait: async () => {
        throw new Error('timeout')
      },
    })
    const { last, phase } = await run(deps)
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed' } })
    expect(calls.confirm).toBe(0)
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(0)
    expect(retryHash(phase)).toBe(HASH)
  })

  it('stops after 2 minutes of "not confirmed yet" and keeps the hash to check again', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([])
    const { last, phase } = await run(deps)
    expect(calls.confirm).toBe(41)
    expect(last).toEqual({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', title: 'Change not confirmed yet', message: PHASE_TIMED_OUT },
    })
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(0)
    expect(retryHash(phase)).toBe(HASH)
  })

  it.each([PHASE_REVERTED, NOT_THIS_CONTRACT, NOT_FROM_WALLET, NOT_THIS_CHANGE])(
    'a terminal answer (%s) is shown as it is and forgets a remembered add',
    async (error) => {
      const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: false, error }])
      const { last, phase } = await run(deps)
      expect(last).toEqual({
        type: 'failed',
        failure: { kind: 'refused', title: 'Cobweb could not confirm this change', message: error },
      })
      expect(calls.forget).toBe(1)
      expect(retryHash(phase)).toBeUndefined()
    },
  )

  it('a taken name after an add says where the phase is, and forgets the add', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: false, error: PHASE_NAME_TAKEN }])
    const { last } = await run(deps, { ...input, call: phaseCalls.register(2, '0', 1, 0), addId: 2 })
    expect(last).toMatchObject({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: {
        kind: 'refused',
        message: 'The phase is on the contract as Group 2, but its name is taken. Name it from its menu.',
      },
    })
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(1)
  })

  it.each([SIGNED_OUT, THROWN, UNSAVED, CHAIN_NOT_READY, NOT_FOUND])(
    'a transient answer (%s) keeps the hash to check again',
    async (error) => {
      const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: false, error }])
      const { phase } = await run(deps)
      expect(phase).toMatchObject({ step: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', message: error } })
      expect(retryHash(phase)).toBe(HASH)
      expect(calls.forget).toBe(0)
    },
  )

  it('a confirm that throws is transient', async () => {
    const { deps } = fakeDeps([], {
      confirm: async () => {
        throw new Error('fetch failed')
      },
    })
    const { last } = await run(deps)
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', message: THROWN } })
  })

  it('checks a remembered add instead of sending it again, with no wallet or chain needed', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: true, data: VIEW }])
    const { events, result } = await run(deps, {
      ...input,
      call: null,
      account: undefined,
      connectedChainId: undefined,
      sentHash: HASH,
      addId: 2,
    })
    expect(events.map((event) => event.type)).toEqual(['verifying', 'success'])
    expect(calls.simulated).toEqual([])
    expect(calls.switchChain).toEqual([])
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(1)
    expect(result).toBe(VIEW)
  })

  it("remembers an add's hash when the wallet sends it and forgets it once confirmed", async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: true, data: VIEW }])
    await run(deps, { ...input, call: phaseCalls.register(2, '0', 1, 0), addId: 2 })
    expect(calls.remember).toEqual([HASH])
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(1)
  })

  it('passes a null call to the simulation (a tab still on sample data)', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: true, data: VIEW }])
    await run(deps, { ...input, call: null })
    expect(calls.simulated).toEqual([null])
  })
})

describe('the pending add kept in the browser', () => {
  function memoryStore(): KeyStore {
    const map = new Map<string, string>()
    return {
      getItem: (key) => map.get(key) ?? null,
      setItem: (key, value) => {
        map.set(key, value)
      },
      removeItem: (key) => {
        map.delete(key)
      },
    }
  }
  const blocked: KeyStore = {
    getItem: () => {
      throw new Error('blocked')
    },
    setItem: () => {
      throw new Error('blocked')
    },
    removeItem: () => {
      throw new Error('blocked')
    },
  }
  const pending: PendingAdd = {
    hash: HASH,
    form: { onchainId: 2, name: 'Allowlist', description: 'Friends of the project.', visible: false },
  }

  it('remembers the sent hash and the form per collection until they are forgotten', () => {
    const store = memoryStore()
    rememberAdd(store, 'col-1', pending)
    expect(pendingAdd(store, 'col-1')).toEqual(pending)
    expect(pendingAdd(store, 'col-2')).toBeNull()
    expect(store.getItem('cobweb-phase:col-1')).toContain(HASH)
    forgetAdd(store, 'col-1')
    expect(pendingAdd(store, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('ignores a stored value that is not a pending add, and a store that throws', () => {
    const store = memoryStore()
    for (const value of [
      'nonsense',
      JSON.stringify({ hash: 'not-a-hash', form: pending.form }),
      JSON.stringify({ hash: HASH }),
      JSON.stringify({ hash: HASH, form: { ...pending.form, onchainId: 0 } }),
      JSON.stringify({ hash: HASH, form: { ...pending.form, name: '' } }),
      JSON.stringify({ hash: HASH, form: { ...pending.form, visible: 'no' } }),
    ]) {
      store.setItem('cobweb-phase:col-1', value)
      expect(pendingAdd(store, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
    }
    expect(() => rememberAdd(blocked, 'col-1', pending)).not.toThrow()
    expect(pendingAdd(blocked, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
    expect(pendingAdd(null, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
    expect(() => forgetAdd(null, 'col-1')).not.toThrow()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The classifier**

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/components/deploy/tx-errors.ts` with:

```ts
import type { TxFailure } from './tx-progress'

/**
 * A wallet or chain error as the creator reads it, shared by the pay machine, the deploy machine and the
 * manage machine (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 7.1; docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section
 * 7.2). The wording follows the subject: the payment sentences are the ones the pay step has shown since
 * Phase 3, and a change in Manage drop names the collection's own custom errors.
 */
export type TxSubject = 'payment' | 'deploy' | 'manage'
export type TxErrorStep = 'switching' | 'simulating' | 'wallet' | 'confirming'

type Words = {
  rejected: TxFailure
  balance: TxFailure
  switchRejected: string
  simulateTitle: string
  walletTitle: string
  walletTail: string
  confirmTitle: string
  confirmTail: string
}

const WORDS: Record<TxSubject, Words> = {
  payment: {
    rejected: {
      kind: 'rejected',
      title: 'Payment cancelled in your wallet',
      message: 'Nothing was sent and nothing was charged. Try again when you are ready.',
    },
    balance: {
      kind: 'balance',
      title: 'Not enough funds in this wallet',
      message:
        'The fee and network gas are more than this wallet holds on the network. Add funds, then try again.',
    },
    switchRejected:
      'Your wallet stayed on its current network, so nothing was paid. Try again and approve the switch.',
    simulateTitle: 'The payment would fail',
    walletTitle: 'The wallet did not send the payment',
    walletTail: 'Nothing was charged.',
    confirmTitle: 'The payment could not be confirmed',
    confirmTail: "Check your wallet's activity before you pay again.",
  },
  deploy: {
    rejected: {
      kind: 'rejected',
      title: 'Deploy cancelled in your wallet',
      message: 'Nothing was sent and no gas was spent. Try again when you are ready.',
    },
    balance: {
      kind: 'balance',
      title: 'Not enough funds for gas',
      message: 'This wallet cannot cover the deploy cost on this network. Add funds, then try again.',
    },
    switchRejected:
      'Your wallet stayed on its current network, so nothing was deployed. Try again and approve the switch.',
    simulateTitle: 'The deploy would fail',
    walletTitle: 'The wallet did not send the deploy',
    walletTail: 'No gas was spent.',
    confirmTitle: 'The deploy could not be confirmed',
    confirmTail: "Check your wallet's activity before you deploy again.",
  },
  manage: {
    rejected: {
      kind: 'rejected',
      title: 'Cancelled in your wallet',
      message: 'Nothing was sent and no gas was spent. Try again when you are ready.',
    },
    balance: {
      kind: 'balance',
      title: 'Not enough funds for gas',
      message: 'This wallet cannot cover the gas for this change on this network. Add funds, then try again.',
    },
    switchRejected:
      'Your wallet stayed on its current network, so nothing was sent. Try again and approve the switch.',
    simulateTitle: 'The change would fail',
    walletTitle: 'The wallet did not send the change',
    walletTail: 'No gas was spent.',
    confirmTitle: 'The change could not be confirmed',
    confirmTail: "Check your wallet's activity before you send it again.",
  },
}

function causes(error: unknown): unknown[] {
  const list: unknown[] = []
  let current: unknown = error
  for (let depth = 0; current && depth < 10; depth++) {
    list.push(current)
    current = (current as { cause?: unknown }).cause
  }
  return list
}

/** The error's `shortMessage` (viem) or `message`, read so a null or undefined rejection never throws inside a catch. */
function shortMessageOf(error: unknown): string {
  const shaped = error as { shortMessage?: unknown; message?: unknown } | null | undefined
  const message = shaped?.shortMessage ?? shaped?.message
  return typeof message === 'string' && message ? message : 'Unknown error.'
}

/** The custom error viem decoded from a failed simulation (`ContractFunctionRevertedError.data`), or null. */
function revertOf(chain: unknown[]): { errorName: string; args: readonly unknown[] } | null {
  for (const entry of chain) {
    const shaped = entry as { name?: unknown; data?: { errorName?: unknown; args?: unknown } }
    if (shaped.name !== 'ContractFunctionRevertedError' || typeof shaped.data?.errorName !== 'string')
      continue
    return { errorName: shaped.data.errorName, args: Array.isArray(shaped.data.args) ? shaped.data.args : [] }
  }
  return null
}

/**
 * The collection's custom errors in the creator's words (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.2), or
 * null for any other. Phases' owner check is OpenZeppelin's `OwnableUnauthorizedAccount`, read as `Unauthorized`.
 */
export function revertSentence(errorName: string, args: readonly unknown[]): string | null {
  const count = (value: unknown) => Number(value).toLocaleString('en-US')
  switch (errorName) {
    case 'BelowMinted':
      return `It cannot go below ${count(args[0])}, the tokens this phase has already minted.`
    case 'AllocationsExceedMaxSupply':
      return "The phases' allocations together would be more than the collection's supply. Lower another phase first."
    case 'MintGroupAlreadyExists':
      return `Phase ${String(args[0])} is already on the contract. Read the contract again, then add the phase.`
    case 'MintGroupDoesNotExist':
      return `Phase ${String(args[0])} is not on the contract. Read the contract again.`
    case 'AddressZero':
      return 'The address cannot be the zero address.'
    case 'Unauthorized':
    case 'OwnableUnauthorizedAccount':
      return `Only the contract's owner can make this change, and ${String(args[0])} is not the owner.`
    default:
      return null
  }
}

/** A wallet or chain error as the creator reads it, for the step it happened at. */
export function classifyError(error: unknown, at: TxErrorStep, subject: TxSubject = 'payment'): TxFailure {
  const words = WORDS[subject]
  const chain = causes(error)
  const rejected = chain.some(
    (entry) =>
      (entry as { name?: string }).name === 'UserRejectedRequestError' ||
      (entry as { code?: unknown }).code === 4001,
  )
  if (at === 'switching') {
    return rejected
      ? { kind: 'switch', title: 'Network switch cancelled', message: words.switchRejected }
      : {
          kind: 'switch',
          title: 'The network switch failed',
          message: `${shortMessageOf(error)} Switch your wallet by hand, then try again.`,
        }
  }
  if (rejected) return words.rejected
  const text = chain.map((entry) => (entry instanceof Error ? entry.message : String(entry))).join(' ')
  if (/insufficient funds/i.test(text)) return words.balance
  const short = shortMessageOf(error)
  if (at === 'simulating') {
    if (subject === 'manage') {
      const revert = revertOf(chain)
      const sentence =
        (revert && revertSentence(revert.errorName, revert.args)) ??
        `The check against the contract failed: ${short}`
      return { kind: 'reverted', title: words.simulateTitle, message: `${sentence} Nothing was sent.` }
    }
    return {
      kind: 'reverted',
      title: words.simulateTitle,
      message: `The check against the factory failed: ${short} Nothing was sent.`,
    }
  }
  if (at === 'wallet')
    return { kind: 'rejected', title: words.walletTitle, message: `${short} ${words.walletTail}` }
  return { kind: 'reverted', title: words.confirmTitle, message: `${short} ${words.confirmTail}` }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The manage machine**

Create `apps/web/src/components/manage/manage-machine.ts`:

```ts
import type { Address, Hex } from 'viem'
import {
  CONFIRM_INTERVAL_MS,
  CONFIRM_TIMEOUT_MS,
  type DeployEvent,
  type KeyStore,
} from '@/components/deploy/deploy-machine'
import { classifyError } from '@/components/deploy/tx-errors'
import type { TxFailure } from '@/components/deploy/tx-progress'
import {
  NOT_FROM_WALLET,
  NOT_THIS_CHANGE,
  NOT_THIS_CONTRACT,
  PHASE_NAME_TAKEN,
  PHASE_NOT_CONFIRMED,
  PHASE_REVERTED,
  PHASE_TIMED_OUT,
  THROWN,
  type ActionResult,
} from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { ManageCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import type { PhaseAdd } from '@/lib/manage/phases-types'

/**
 * Every on-chain change in Manage drop as one pure machine (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.2):
 * the deploy machine without prepare, reporting through its events and reducer. The wallet must be the
 * sign-in wallet and on the collection's chain (it is switched when it is not); the call is simulated on
 * the collection and the wallet signs exactly the simulated request (CLAUDE.md rule 2); the receipt lands;
 * then the server's confirmation, which reads the chain again, is polled every 3 seconds for up to 2
 * minutes. `TransactionDialog` wires the actions in.
 */
export type ManageInput = {
  /**
   * The change. Null on a tab still on sample data, whose sample actions check its review instead, and
   * for "Check again", which never simulates (`sentHash` is set).
   */
  call: ManageCall | null
  /** The connected account, if any. */
  account: string | undefined
  /** The sign-in wallet: the server accepts only a receipt sent from it. */
  wallet: Address
  /** The collection's chain. */
  chainId: number
  connectedChainId: number | undefined
  /** A remembered add: checked, never sent again. */
  sentHash?: Hex
  /** The id an add registers, named in the sentence when its name turns out to be taken. */
  addId?: number
}

export type ManageDeps<T> = {
  switchChain(chainId: number): Promise<void>
  /** Simulates the call on the collection; resolves with the function that sends the simulated request, unchanged. */
  simulate(call: ManageCall | null): Promise<() => Promise<Hex>>
  wait(hash: Hex): Promise<'success' | 'reverted'>
  confirm(hash: Hex): Promise<ActionResult<T>>
  /** Add phase only: keeps the sent hash until the server records the phase. */
  remember?(hash: Hex): void
  forget?(): void
  sleep(ms: number): Promise<void>
  now(): number
}

const NO_WALLET: TxFailure = {
  kind: 'refused',
  title: 'Connect your wallet',
  message: 'Connect your wallet to make this change. Nothing was sent.',
}
const REVERTED_ON_CHAIN: TxFailure = {
  kind: 'reverted',
  title: 'The change failed on chain',
  message: 'The transaction was included but reverted, so nothing changed. You paid gas only. Try again.',
}

/**
 * Asks the server to confirm a change every 3 seconds while it answers "not confirmed yet", for up to 2
 * minutes. A confirm that throws is a transient failure.
 */
export async function verifyManage<T>(
  hash: Hex,
  deps: Pick<ManageDeps<T>, 'confirm' | 'sleep' | 'now'>,
): Promise<{ result: T } | { error: string }> {
  const started = deps.now()
  for (;;) {
    let answer: ActionResult<T>
    try {
      answer = await deps.confirm(hash)
    } catch {
      return { error: THROWN }
    }
    if (answer.ok) return { result: answer.data }
    if (answer.error !== PHASE_NOT_CONFIRMED) return { error: answer.error }
    if (deps.now() - started + CONFIRM_INTERVAL_MS > CONFIRM_TIMEOUT_MS) return { error: PHASE_TIMED_OUT }
    await deps.sleep(CONFIRM_INTERVAL_MS)
  }
}

/**
 * Whether the server's answer ends this attempt for good, so a remembered add is forgotten: the receipt
 * reverted or proved something else, or the added phase's name is taken. Any other refusal (a session that
 * ended, a thrown action, a chain not ready) says nothing about the transaction: the hash stays and
 * "Check again" is offered.
 */
export function isTerminalManageAnswer(error: string): boolean {
  return (
    error === PHASE_REVERTED ||
    error === NOT_THIS_CONTRACT ||
    error === NOT_FROM_WALLET ||
    error === NOT_THIS_CHANGE ||
    error === PHASE_NAME_TAKEN
  )
}

/** A terminal answer in the creator's words: a taken name after an add says where the phase is. */
export function terminalMessage(error: string, addId: number | undefined): string {
  if (error === PHASE_NAME_TAKEN && addId !== undefined)
    return `The phase is on the contract as Group ${addId}, but its name is taken. Name it from its menu.`
  return error
}

/** Makes the change, or checks a remembered add, reporting each step through `dispatch`. */
export async function runManage<T>(
  input: ManageInput,
  deps: ManageDeps<T>,
  dispatch: (event: DeployEvent) => void,
): Promise<T | null> {
  let hash = input.sentHash
  if (!hash) {
    if (!input.account) {
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: NO_WALLET })
      return null
    }
    // The server accepts only a receipt from the sign-in wallet: another account must not reach the wallet.
    if (input.account.toLowerCase() !== input.wallet.toLowerCase()) {
      dispatch({
        type: 'failed',
        failure: {
          kind: 'refused',
          title: 'Use your sign-in wallet',
          message: `Cobweb checks changes sent from ${input.wallet}, your sign-in wallet. Switch to that account in your wallet, then try again.`,
        },
      })
      return null
    }
    if (input.connectedChainId !== input.chainId) {
      dispatch({ type: 'switching' })
      try {
        await deps.switchChain(input.chainId)
      } catch (error) {
        dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: classifyError(error, 'switching', 'manage') })
        return null
      }
    }
    dispatch({ type: 'simulating' })
    let send: () => Promise<Hex>
    try {
      send = await deps.simulate(input.call)
    } catch (error) {
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: classifyError(error, 'simulating', 'manage') })
      return null
    }
    dispatch({ type: 'wallet' })
    try {
      hash = await send()
    } catch (error) {
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: classifyError(error, 'wallet', 'manage') })
      return null
    }
    deps.remember?.(hash)
    dispatch({ type: 'confirming', txHash: hash })
    let status: 'success' | 'reverted'
    try {
      status = await deps.wait(hash)
    } catch (error) {
      // The receipt could not be read: the transaction may still land, so keep the hash and offer a check.
      dispatch({
        type: 'failed',
        failure: { ...classifyError(error, 'confirming', 'manage'), kind: 'unconfirmed' },
      })
      return null
    }
    if (status === 'reverted') {
      deps.forget?.()
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: REVERTED_ON_CHAIN })
      return null
    }
  }
  dispatch({ type: 'verifying', txHash: hash })
  const verified = await verifyManage(hash, deps)
  if ('result' in verified) {
    deps.forget?.()
    dispatch({ type: 'success', txHash: hash })
    return verified.result
  }
  if (!isTerminalManageAnswer(verified.error)) {
    // Timed out or a transient refusal: the change may still be confirmed, so the hash stays.
    dispatch({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', title: 'Change not confirmed yet', message: verified.error },
    })
  } else {
    deps.forget?.()
    dispatch({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: {
        kind: 'refused',
        title: 'Cobweb could not confirm this change',
        message: terminalMessage(verified.error, input.addId),
      },
    })
  }
  return null
}

/** What the browser keeps until the server records an added phase: the sent hash and the add's own fields. */
export type PendingAdd = { hash: Hex; form: PhaseAdd }

const keyFor = (collectionId: string) => `cobweb-phase:${collectionId}`
const HASH_RE = /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/

export function rememberAdd(store: KeyStore | null, collectionId: string, pending: PendingAdd): void {
  try {
    store?.setItem(keyFor(collectionId), JSON.stringify(pending))
  } catch {
    // Storage blocked: the add is still checked in this visit.
  }
}

/** An add this browser sent for the collection and the server has not recorded yet, or null. */
export function pendingAdd(store: KeyStore | null, collectionId: string): PendingAdd | null {
  try {
    const value = store?.getItem(keyFor(collectionId)) ?? null
    if (!value) return null
    const parsed = JSON.parse(value) as { hash?: unknown; form?: Partial<Record<keyof PhaseAdd, unknown>> }
    const form = parsed.form
    if (typeof parsed.hash !== 'string' || !HASH_RE.test(parsed.hash) || !form) return null
    if (
      typeof form.onchainId !== 'number' ||
      !Number.isInteger(form.onchainId) ||
      form.onchainId < 1 ||
      typeof form.name !== 'string' ||
      !form.name ||
      typeof form.description !== 'string' ||
      typeof form.visible !== 'boolean'
    )
      return null
    return {
      hash: parsed.hash as Hex,
      form: {
        onchainId: form.onchainId,
        name: form.name,
        description: form.description,
        visible: form.visible,
      },
    }
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

export function forgetAdd(store: KeyStore | null, collectionId: string): void {
  try {
    store?.removeItem(keyFor(collectionId))
  } catch {
    // Storage blocked: nothing was kept.
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: The fixture**

Create `apps/web/scripts/deploy-fixture.ts`:

```ts
// The local deploy fixture verify:deploy and verify:phases share (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section
// 10), so the two cannot drift: the local settings with non-local endpoints refused, the service client
// and the Anvil client, the PASS and FAIL lines, the SIWE sign-in, a seeded generative collection, the
// lock and deploy contexts the write helpers build, the payment, the render functions, the factory call,
// and `deployedCollection`, which locks, pays, renders, links, prepares, deploys and confirms through the
// cores. Every row it creates is tracked, and `removeFixtures` deletes them. Never prints a key.

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import {
  createTestClient,
  createWalletClient,
  http,
  parseAbi,
  publicActions,
  type Address,
  type Hex,
} from 'viem'
import type { PrivateKeyAccount } from 'viem/accounts'
import { anvil } from 'viem/chains'
import { createSiweMessage, generateSiweNonce } from 'viem/siwe'
import type { RenderQueue } from '@cobweb/shared/render-queue'
import type { Database } from '../src/lib/database.types.ts'
import { adminClient, clientOptions, localConfig, type LocalClient } from './render-deps.ts'

// Every local setting, with non-local endpoints refused (scripts/render-deps.ts). It also fills the
// environment the shared chain clients read (RPC_URL_31337, FACTORY_ADDRESS_31337).
export const settings = localConfig()
export const rpc: string = settings.rpc
export const factory: Address = settings.factory

// Imported after the environment is set, so the shared clients read the local chain settings.
export const { collectionSalt, encodeCollectionArgs, factoryAbi, predictCollectionAddress } =
  await import('@cobweb/shared')
export const { COLLECTION_CODE_HASH, COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE } = await import('@cobweb/shared/collection')
export const { confirmCore, lockCore } = await import('../src/lib/art/lock-core.ts')
export const { readLockCollection } = await import('../src/lib/art/lock-collection.ts')
export const { confirmDeployCore, prepareCore, readDeployment } =
  await import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts')
export const { collectionArgsOf } = await import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-args.ts')
export type LockContext = import('../src/lib/art/lock-core.ts').LockContext
export type DeployContext = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts').DeployContext
export type DeployOutcome<T> = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts').DeployOutcome<T>
export type DeployForm = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-types.ts').DeployForm
export type Prepared = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-types.ts').Prepared
export type DeployRecord = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-types.ts').DeployRecord

export const admin: LocalClient = adminClient(settings)
export const chain = createTestClient({ chain: anvil, mode: 'anvil', transport: http(rpc) }).extend(
  publicActions,
)
/** A queue that sends nothing: the render is driven by hand, not by a worker. */
const silentQueue: RenderQueue = { send: async () => {} }
export const CID = (letter: string) => `bafybei${letter.repeat(52)}`
export const HEX64 = (n: number) => n.toString(16).padStart(64, '0')
/** The factory's approval view, which the shipped ABI leaves out (the app never asks it). */
const approvalAbi = parseAbi(['function isCreationCodeApproved(bytes32 codeHash) view returns (bool)'])

let passed = 0
const failed: string[] = []

/** A check returns null on success, or a description of what happened instead. */
export async function check(name: string, fn: () => Promise<string | null>): Promise<void> {
  let problem: string | null
  try {
    problem = await fn()
  } catch (e) {
    problem = `threw ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`
  }
  if (problem === null) {
    passed++
    console.log(`PASS ${name}`)
  } else {
    failed.push(name)
    console.log(`FAIL ${name}: ${problem}`)
  }
}

/** Runs the checks, then the cleanup whatever happened, prints the totals and exits non-zero on any failure. */
export async function runChecks(main: () => Promise<void>, cleanup: () => Promise<void>): Promise<never> {
  try {
    await main()
  } catch (e) {
    failed.push('run')
    console.log(`FAIL run: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`)
  } finally {
    await cleanup()
  }
  console.log(`\n${passed} passed, ${failed.length} failed`)
  process.exit(failed.length ? 1 : 0)
}

const userIds: string[] = []
const collectionIds: string[] = []

/** Tracks a collection a script created outside `seededCollection`, so `removeFixtures` deletes it. */
export function trackCollection(cid: string): void {
  collectionIds.push(cid)
}

/** A signed-in creator: the wallet, its Supabase session and its user id. */
export type Creator = { account: PrivateKeyAccount; client: LocalClient; userId: string }

/** Signs in with SIWE, as the app does. */
export async function signIn(account: PrivateKeyAccount): Promise<Creator> {
  const client: LocalClient = createClient<Database>(settings.url, settings.publishableKey, clientOptions)
  const message = createSiweMessage({
    address: account.address,
    chainId: 1,
    domain: 'localhost:3000',
    nonce: generateSiweNonce(),
    uri: 'http://localhost:3000',
    version: '1',
    statement: 'Sign in to Cobweb.',
    issuedAt: new Date(),
  })
  const signature = await account.signMessage({ message })
  const { data, error } = await client.auth.signInWithWeb3({ chain: 'ethereum', message, signature })
  if (error || !data.user) throw new Error(`sign-in failed: ${error?.message ?? 'no user'}`)
  userIds.push(data.user.id)
  return { account, client, userId: data.user.id }
}

let fileCount = 0
/** A content hash no other upload of the run has. */
export const nextFileHash = () => HEX64(++fileCount)

/** A generative collection of three tokens over two layers of two traits, owned by the client's creator. */
export async function seededCollection(client: LocalClient, name: string): Promise<string> {
  const { data: col, error } = await client
    .from('collections')
    .insert({
      name: `Deploy check ${name} ${Date.now()} ${++fileCount}`,
      symbol: 'DPL',
      project_type: 'generative',
    })
    .select('id')
    .single()
  if (error || !col) throw new Error(`collection: ${error?.message ?? 'no row'}`)
  const cid = col.id
  collectionIds.push(cid)
  const art = await client
    .from('art_collections')
    .insert({ collection_id: cid, name: 'Owls', size: 3, position: 0 })
    .select('id')
    .single()
  if (art.error || !art.data) throw new Error(`art collection: ${art.error?.message ?? 'no row'}`)
  const artId = art.data.id
  const traits: string[][] = []
  for (const [position, layerName] of ['Background', 'Eyes'].entries()) {
    const layer = await client
      .from('layers')
      .insert({ collection_id: cid, art_collection_id: artId, name: layerName, position })
      .select('id')
      .single()
    if (layer.error || !layer.data) throw new Error(`layer: ${layer.error?.message ?? 'no row'}`)
    const ids: string[] = []
    for (const [index, traitName] of ['One', 'Two'].entries()) {
      const hash = nextFileHash()
      const upload = await client.rpc('record_upload', {
        p_collection_id: cid,
        p_kind: 'layer_image',
        p_storage_key: `${cid}/layers/${hash}.png`,
        p_content_hash: hash,
        p_mime: 'image/png',
        p_media_type: 'image',
        p_bytes: 1234,
        p_width: 1000,
        p_height: 1000,
        p_file_name: `${traitName}.png`,
      })
      if (upload.error) throw new Error(`upload: ${upload.error.message}`)
      const trait = await client
        .from('traits')
        .insert({
          collection_id: cid,
          layer_id: layer.data.id,
          name: traitName,
          weight: 50,
          position: index,
          upload_id: upload.data as string,
        })
        .select('id')
        .single()
      if (trait.error || !trait.data) throw new Error(`trait: ${trait.error?.message ?? 'no row'}`)
      ids.push(trait.data.id)
    }
    traits.push(ids)
  }
  const [background, eyes] = traits as [string[], string[]]
  const dna = [
    [background[0]!, eyes[0]!],
    [background[0]!, eyes[1]!],
    [background[1]!, eyes[0]!],
  ]
  const tokens = await client.from('tokens').insert(
    dna.map((traitIds, index) => ({
      collection_id: cid,
      token_id: index + 1,
      art_collection_id: artId,
      dna: traitIds,
    })),
  )
  if (tokens.error) throw new Error(`tokens: ${tokens.error.message}`)
  return cid
}

/** The context `lockWrite` builds, re-read from the row each time so the status is current. */
export async function lockContextFor(
  client: LocalClient,
  owner: string,
  wallet: string,
  cid: string,
): Promise<LockContext> {
  const collection = await readLockCollection(client, cid)
  if (!collection || collection.owner_id !== owner) throw new Error('the creator cannot read the collection')
  return {
    supabase: client,
    service: () => admin,
    owner,
    wallet: wallet.toLowerCase(),
    collection,
    queue: silentQueue,
  }
}

/** The context `deployWrite` and `phasesWrite` build: the same read, without the queue. */
export async function deployContextFor(
  client: LocalClient,
  owner: string,
  wallet: string,
  cid: string,
): Promise<DeployContext> {
  const { queue: _queue, ...context } = await lockContextFor(client, owner, wallet, cid)
  void _queue
  return context
}

/** A refused outcome as a short string: the sentence, the database code, or "accepted". */
export const refusal = <T>(outcome: DeployOutcome<T>) =>
  'refused' in outcome ? outcome.refused : 'error' in outcome ? `db ${outcome.error?.code}` : 'accepted'

/** Pays for a job from the account through the factory and waits for the receipt. */
export async function pay(account: PrivateKeyAccount, jobId: Hex, value: bigint): Promise<Hex> {
  const wallet = createWalletClient({ account, chain: anvil, transport: http(rpc) })
  const hash = await wallet.writeContract({
    address: factory,
    abi: factoryAbi,
    functionName: 'payForArt',
    args: [jobId],
    value,
  })
  const receipt = await chain.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash })
  if (receipt.status !== 'success') throw new Error(`payForArt reverted: ${hash}`)
  return hash
}

/** The render functions with the service role, from a paid (or placeholder) job to done, as the worker would. */
export async function finishRender(
  jobId: string,
  imagesCid: string,
  metadataCid: string,
  baseUri: string,
  placeholderImageCid: string | null = null,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const steps: [string, Record<string, unknown>][] = [
    ['art_claim_render', { p_job_id: jobId }],
    ['art_batch_start', { p_job_id: jobId, p_index: 0 }],
    ['art_batch_done', { p_job_id: jobId, p_index: 0, p_images_cid: imagesCid, p_images_size: 1000 }],
    [
      'art_render_finish',
      {
        p_job_id: jobId,
        p_metadata_cid: metadataCid,
        p_base_uri: baseUri,
        p_placeholder_image_cid: placeholderImageCid,
      },
    ],
  ]
  for (const [fn, args] of steps) {
    const { error } = await admin.rpc(fn as 'art_claim_render', args as never)
    if (error) return `${fn}: ${error.message}`
  }
  return null
}

/** Sends deployCollection with the shipped creation code, the given arguments and salt, and waits for the receipt. */
export async function deploy(account: PrivateKeyAccount, encodedArgs: Hex, salt: Hex): Promise<Hex> {
  const wallet = createWalletClient({ account, chain: anvil, transport: http(rpc) })
  const hash = await wallet.writeContract({
    address: factory,
    abi: factoryAbi,
    functionName: 'deployCollection',
    args: [COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE, encodedArgs, salt],
  })
  const receipt = await chain.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash })
  if (receipt.status !== 'success') throw new Error(`deployCollection reverted: ${hash}`)
  return hash
}

export type CollectionRow = {
  status: string
  chain_id: number | null
  contract_address: string | null
  slug: string | null
  slug_reserved_at: string | null
}

/** The collection's row as the service role reads it. */
export async function collectionRow(cid: string): Promise<CollectionRow> {
  const { data, error } = await admin
    .from('collections')
    .select('status, chain_id, contract_address, slug, slug_reserved_at')
    .eq('id', cid)
    .single()
  if (error) throw new Error(`collection row: ${error.message}`)
  return data
}

/** The mint page link each fixture collection reserves. */
export const slugFor = (cid: string) => `deploy-${cid.slice(0, 8)}`

/** Anvil answers as 31337 and the factory approves the shipped creation code hash, or why not. */
export async function chainReady(): Promise<string | null> {
  if ((await chain.getChainId()) !== 31337) return 'not chain 31337'
  const code = await chain.getCode({ address: factory })
  if (!code || code === '0x') return `no contract at ${factory}; run chain:local again`
  const approved = await chain.readContract({
    address: factory,
    abi: approvalAbi,
    functionName: 'isCreationCodeApproved',
    args: [COLLECTION_CODE_HASH],
  })
  return approved
    ? null
    : `the factory does not approve ${COLLECTION_CODE_HASH}; run forge build, the copy script and chain:local again`
}

/**
 * Locks a seeded collection, pays for it from the creator's wallet, renders it to done and reserves its
 * link, through the cores and functions the app uses. Throws on any refusal.
 */
export async function renderCollection(creator: Creator, cid: string): Promise<void> {
  const lockCtx = await lockContextFor(creator.client, creator.userId, creator.account.address, cid)
  const locked = await lockCore(lockCtx, { allowDuplicates: false, chainId: 31337 })
  if (!('data' in locked)) throw new Error(`lock: ${refusal(locked)}`)
  const job = locked.data
  const paidHash = await pay(creator.account, job.jobId, BigInt(job.quote.totalWei))
  const confirmed = await confirmCore(lockCtx, job.id, paidHash)
  if (!('data' in confirmed)) throw new Error(`pay: ${refusal(confirmed)}`)
  if (confirmed.data.state !== 'paid') throw new Error(`job ${confirmed.data.state}`)
  const rendering = await finishRender(job.id, CID('d'), CID('e'), `ipfs://${CID('e')}/`)
  if (rendering) throw new Error(rendering)
  const link = await creator.client.rpc('reserve_slug', { p_collection_id: cid, p_slug: slugFor(cid) })
  if (link.error) throw new Error(`link: ${link.error.message}`)
}

/** A collection deployed through the factory and recorded, with the context to run the cores on it. */
export type Deployed = {
  cid: string
  /** Read after the record, so the collection reads deployed. */
  ctx: DeployContext
  prepared: Prepared
  record: DeployRecord
  hash: Hex
}

/**
 * A seeded collection locked, paid, rendered, linked, prepared, deployed through the factory from the
 * creator's wallet and confirmed, through the same cores the app uses. Throws on any refusal.
 */
export async function deployedCollection(
  creator: Creator,
  form: DeployForm,
  name = 'deployed',
): Promise<Deployed> {
  const cid = await seededCollection(creator.client, name)
  await renderCollection(creator, cid)
  const before = await deployContextFor(creator.client, creator.userId, creator.account.address, cid)
  const prepared = await prepareCore(before, form)
  if (!('data' in prepared)) throw new Error(`prepare: ${refusal(prepared)}`)
  const hash = await deploy(creator.account, prepared.data.encodedArgs, prepared.data.salt)
  const confirmed = await confirmDeployCore(before, hash, form)
  if (!('data' in confirmed)) throw new Error(`confirm: ${refusal(confirmed)}`)
  const ctx = await deployContextFor(creator.client, creator.userId, creator.account.address, cid)
  return { cid, ctx, prepared: prepared.data, record: confirmed.data, hash }
}

/** Deletes every job, collection (its deployment and phases cascade) and user the run created, or says what is left. */
export async function removeFixtures(): Promise<string | null> {
  if (collectionIds.length) {
    const jobs = await admin.from('render_jobs').delete().in('collection_id', collectionIds)
    if (jobs.error) return `jobs: ${jobs.error.message}`
    const cols = await admin.from('collections').delete().in('id', collectionIds)
    if (cols.error) return `collections: ${cols.error.message}`
    const left = await admin.from('deployments').select('collection_id').in('collection_id', collectionIds)
    if (left.error) return `deployments: ${left.error.message}`
    if (left.data.length) return `${left.data.length} deployment(s) left`
    const groups = await admin.from('mint_groups').select('id').in('collection_id', collectionIds)
    if (groups.error) return `mint_groups: ${groups.error.message}`
    if (groups.data.length) return `${groups.data.length} mint group(s) left`
  }
  for (const id of userIds) {
    const { error } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(id)
    if (error) return error.message
  }
  return null
}
```
