- [ ] **Step 2: verify:deploy on the fixture**

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/scripts/verify-deploy.ts` with:

```ts
// Checks Deploy against a LOCAL Anvil chain and LOCAL Supabase (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md,
// section 10): a signed-in creator's generative collection is locked, paid and rendered to done through
// the same cores and functions the app uses; prepareCore answers the paid chain, the prediction (which
// the factory's own view agrees with) and the fees; the script wallet sends deployCollection with the
// prepared bytes; confirmDeployCore records the contract, the launch phases and the permanent link from
// the factory's event; the contract's views match the arguments; then the refusals: another chain, a
// draft with nothing rendered, an unrelated transaction, a contract deployed with other arguments, a
// second confirm (idempotent), a deployed collection, a hash recorded for another collection; and a
// deferred collection deploying on its placeholder base URI. Fifteen PASS lines with the cleanup.
//
//   supabase start
//   pnpm --filter @cobweb/web chain:local    (then copy its two lines into apps/web/.env.local)
//   pnpm --filter @cobweb/web verify:deploy
//
// The fixture (scripts/deploy-fixture.ts, shared with verify:phases) reads the settings from the
// environment, `supabase status -o env` and apps/web/.env.local, and refuses non-local endpoints. Never
// prints a key. Deletes every row it creates. Runs under
// `node --conditions=react-server --import ./scripts/resolve-ts.mjs`.

import { createWalletClient, http, parseEther, type Hex } from 'viem'
import { generatePrivateKey, privateKeyToAccount } from 'viem/accounts'
import { anvil } from 'viem/chains'
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import {
  CID,
  COLLECTION_CODE_HASH,
  COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE,
  chain,
  chainReady,
  check,
  collectionArgsOf,
  collectionRow,
  collectionSalt,
  confirmDeployCore,
  deploy,
  deployContextFor,
  encodeCollectionArgs,
  factory,
  factoryAbi,
  finishRender,
  nextFileHash,
  predictCollectionAddress,
  prepareCore,
  readDeployment,
  refusal,
  removeFixtures,
  renderCollection,
  rpc,
  runChecks,
  seededCollection,
  settings,
  signIn,
  slugFor,
  trackCollection,
  type DeployForm,
  type DeployRecord,
  type Prepared,
} from './deploy-fixture.ts'

const { ALREADY_DEPLOYED, DEPLOY_USED, NOT_RENDERED, NOT_THIS_DEPLOY, OTHER_CONTRACT, WRONG_CHAIN } =
  await import('../src/lib/art/result.ts')

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  console.log(`Verifying deploy on ${rpc} against ${settings.url}`)
  await check(
    '1. chain: Anvil answers as 31337 and the factory approves the shipped creation code hash',
    chainReady,
  )

  const creatorKey = privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey())
  const stranger = privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey())
  // A third address for the payout, so check 9 tells the payout from the owner (the deployer).
  const payee = privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey())
  await chain.setBalance({ address: creatorKey.address, value: parseEther('10') })
  await chain.setBalance({ address: stranger.address, value: parseEther('10') })
  const creator = await signIn(creatorKey)
  const { client, userId } = creator

  // The rendered collection.
  const rendered = await seededCollection(client, 'rendered')
  await check('2. seed: a generative collection is locked, paid, rendered to done and linked', async () => {
    await renderCollection(creator, rendered)
    const state = await collectionRow(rendered)
    return state.status === 'rendered' && state.slug === slugFor(rendered) ? null : JSON.stringify(state)
  })

  const form: DeployForm = {
    chainId: 31337,
    payout: payee.address,
    royaltyBps: 500,
    royaltyReceiver: stranger.address,
    publicMaxPerWallet: 2,
    groups: [
      { name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 2 },
      { name: 'Holders', priceWei: '1000000000000000', maxSupply: 1 },
    ],
  }
  let prepared: Prepared | null = null
  let ctx = await deployContextFor(client, userId, creatorKey.address, rendered)

  await check(
    '3. prepareCore: answers the paid chain, the prediction the factory agrees with, the base URI and the fees, and refuses another chain',
    async () => {
      const outcome = await prepareCore(ctx, form)
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return refusal(outcome)
      prepared = outcome.data
      const fromFactory = await chain.readContract({
        address: factory,
        abi: factoryAbi,
        functionName: 'predictCollectionAddress',
        args: [creatorKey.address, COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE, prepared.encodedArgs, prepared.salt],
      })
      const [flat, bps, airdrop] = await chain.readContract({
        address: factory,
        abi: factoryAbi,
        functionName: 'collectionFees',
      })
      const problems = [
        prepared.chainId === 31337 ? null : `chain ${prepared.chainId}`,
        prepared.factory === factory ? null : `factory ${prepared.factory}`,
        prepared.deployer === creatorKey.address ? null : `deployer ${prepared.deployer}`,
        prepared.salt === collectionSalt(rendered) ? null : `salt ${prepared.salt}`,
        prepared.codeHash === COLLECTION_CODE_HASH ? null : `code hash ${prepared.codeHash}`,
        prepared.predicted === fromFactory
          ? null
          : `predicted ${prepared.predicted}, the factory says ${fromFactory}`,
        prepared.baseUri === `ipfs://${CID('e')}/` ? null : `base URI ${prepared.baseUri}`,
        prepared.revealsTo === null ? null : `reveals to ${prepared.revealsTo}`,
        prepared.fees.mintFeeFlatWei === flat.toString() &&
        prepared.fees.mintFeeBps === Number(bps) &&
        prepared.fees.airdropFeePerTokenWei === airdrop.toString()
          ? null
          : `fees ${JSON.stringify(prepared.fees)}`,
        prepared.args.name !== '' && prepared.args.symbol === 'DPL' && prepared.args.maxSupply === '3'
          ? null
          : `args ${JSON.stringify(prepared.args)}`,
      ].filter(Boolean)
      if (problems.length) return problems.join('; ')
      const elsewhere = await prepareCore(ctx, { ...form, chainId: 1 })
      return refusal(elsewhere) === WRONG_CHAIN ? null : `another chain: ${refusal(elsewhere)}`
    },
  )

  await check('4. prepareCore: a draft collection with nothing rendered is refused', async () => {
    const draft = await seededCollection(client, 'draft')
    const supply = await client.from('collections').update({ max_supply: 3 }).eq('id', draft)
    if (supply.error) return `supply: ${supply.error.message}`
    const link = await client.rpc('reserve_slug', { p_collection_id: draft, p_slug: slugFor(draft) })
    if (link.error) return `link: ${link.error.message}`
    const outcome = await prepareCore(await deployContextFor(client, userId, creatorKey.address, draft), form)
    return refusal(outcome) === NOT_RENDERED ? null : refusal(outcome)
  })

  await check('5. confirmDeployCore: a transaction that deployed nothing is refused', async () => {
    const wallet = createWalletClient({ account: creatorKey, chain: anvil, transport: http(rpc) })
    const hash = await wallet.sendTransaction({ to: stranger.address, value: 1n })
    await chain.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash })
    const outcome = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, hash, form)
    return refusal(outcome) === NOT_THIS_DEPLOY ? null : refusal(outcome)
  })

  let otherHash: Hex | null = null
  await check(
    '6. confirmDeployCore: a contract deployed from the prepared bytes with other arguments is refused and never recorded',
    async () => {
      if (!prepared) return 'not prepared'
      const otherArgs = encodeCollectionArgs(collectionArgsOf({ ...prepared.args, royaltyBps: 600 }))
      otherHash = await deploy(creatorKey, otherArgs, prepared.salt)
      const outcome = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, otherHash, form)
      if (refusal(outcome) !== OTHER_CONTRACT) return refusal(outcome)
      const record = await readDeployment(client, rendered)
      if (record) return `recorded ${record.contractAddress}`
      return (await collectionRow(rendered)).status === 'rendered' ? null : 'the collection changed'
    },
  )

  let deployHash: Hex | null = null
  await check(
    '7. deploy: the script wallet sends deployCollection with the prepared bytes and the factory records the predicted address',
    async () => {
      if (!prepared) return 'not prepared'
      deployHash = await deploy(creatorKey, prepared.encodedArgs, prepared.salt)
      const isCollection = await chain.readContract({
        address: factory,
        abi: factoryAbi,
        functionName: 'isCollection',
        args: [prepared.predicted],
      })
      return isCollection ? null : `the factory does not know ${prepared.predicted}`
    },
  )

  let record: DeployRecord | null = null
  await check(
    '8. confirmDeployCore: records the contract, the launch phases and the permanent link; the collection reads deployed on its chain',
    async () => {
      if (!prepared || !deployHash) return 'not deployed'
      const outcome = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, deployHash, form)
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return refusal(outcome)
      record = outcome.data
      const state = await collectionRow(rendered)
      const problems = [
        record.chainId === 31337 ? null : `chain ${record.chainId}`,
        record.contractAddress === prepared.predicted ? null : `address ${record.contractAddress}`,
        record.txHash === deployHash.toLowerCase() ? null : `hash ${record.txHash}`,
        record.deployer === creatorKey.address ? null : `deployer ${record.deployer}`,
        record.baseUri === prepared.baseUri ? null : `base URI ${record.baseUri}`,
        record.payout === payee.address &&
        record.royaltyReceiver === stranger.address &&
        record.royaltyBps === 500
          ? null
          : 'payout or royalty',
        record.publicMaxPerWallet === 2 ? null : `cap ${record.publicMaxPerWallet}`,
        JSON.stringify(record.fees) === JSON.stringify(prepared.fees)
          ? null
          : `fees ${JSON.stringify(record.fees)}`,
        JSON.stringify(record.groups) ===
        JSON.stringify([
          { onchainId: 0, name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 2 },
          { onchainId: 1, name: 'Holders', priceWei: '1000000000000000', maxSupply: 1 },
        ])
          ? null
          : `groups ${JSON.stringify(record.groups)}`,
        state.status === 'deployed' && state.chain_id === 31337
          ? null
          : `collection ${JSON.stringify(state)}`,
        state.contract_address === prepared.predicted.toLowerCase()
          ? null
          : `contract ${state.contract_address}`,
        state.slug === slugFor(rendered) && state.slug_reserved_at === null
          ? null
          : `link ${state.slug} ${state.slug_reserved_at}`,
      ].filter(Boolean)
      return problems.length ? problems.join('; ') : null
    },
  )

  await check(
    '9. contract: name, symbol, maxSupply, baseURI, owner (the deployer), payout (a third address), publicMaxMintPerWallet, royaltyInfo and collectionState match the arguments',
    async () => {
      if (!prepared) return 'not prepared'
      const address = prepared.predicted
      const name = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'name' })
      const symbol = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'symbol' })
      const maxSupply = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'maxSupply' })
      const baseURI = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'baseURI' })
      const owner = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'owner' })
      const payout = await chain.readContract({
        address,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'ownerPayoutAddress',
      })
      const cap = await chain.readContract({
        address,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'publicMaxMintPerWallet',
      })
      const [receiver, royalty] = await chain.readContract({
        address,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'royaltyInfo',
        args: [1n, 10_000n],
      })
      const state = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'collectionState' })
      const problems = [
        name === prepared.args.name ? null : `name ${name}`,
        symbol === 'DPL' ? null : `symbol ${symbol}`,
        maxSupply === 3n ? null : `maxSupply ${maxSupply}`,
        baseURI === prepared.baseUri ? null : `baseURI ${baseURI}`,
        owner === creatorKey.address ? null : `owner ${owner}, not the deployer`,
        payout === payee.address ? null : `payout ${payout}, not the third address`,
        cap === 2n ? null : `cap ${cap}`,
        receiver === stranger.address && royalty === 500n ? null : `royalty ${receiver} ${royalty}`,
        state.groups.length === 2 &&
        state.groups[0]?.price === 0n &&
        state.groups[0]?.maxSupply === 2n &&
        state.groups[1]?.price === 1_000_000_000_000_000n &&
        state.groups[1]?.maxSupply === 1n &&
        state.groups.every((group) => !group.active && !group.activeFlag)
          ? null
          : `groups ${JSON.stringify(state.groups, (_k, v) => (typeof v === 'bigint' ? v.toString() : v))}`,
        state.maxSupply === 3n && state.totalMinted === 0n ? null : 'state supply',
      ].filter(Boolean)
      return problems.length ? problems.join('; ') : null
    },
  )

  await check(
    '10. confirmDeployCore: a second confirm with the same hash answers the record unchanged',
    async () => {
      if (!deployHash || !record) return 'not recorded'
      ctx = await deployContextFor(client, userId, creatorKey.address, rendered)
      const again = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, deployHash, form)
      if (!('data' in again)) return refusal(again)
      return JSON.stringify(again.data) === JSON.stringify(record)
        ? null
        : `changed: ${JSON.stringify(again.data)}`
    },
  )

  await check(
    '11. prepareCore and confirmDeployCore: a deployed collection is refused as already deployed',
    async () => {
      if (!otherHash) return 'no other hash'
      const prepare = await prepareCore(ctx, form)
      if (refusal(prepare) !== ALREADY_DEPLOYED) return `prepare: ${refusal(prepare)}`
      const confirm = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, otherHash, form)
      return refusal(confirm) === ALREADY_DEPLOYED ? null : `confirm: ${refusal(confirm)}`
    },
  )

  // The deferred collection.
  let deferred = ''
  await check(
    '12. seed: a deferred collection with its placeholder rendered, a supply and a live link',
    async () => {
      const { data: col, error } = await client
        .from('collections')
        .insert({ name: `Deploy check deferred ${Date.now()}`, symbol: 'DPL', project_type: 'generative' })
        .select('id')
        .single()
      if (error || !col) return `collection: ${error?.message ?? 'no row'}`
      deferred = col.id
      trackCollection(deferred)
      const set = await client
        .from('collections')
        .update({ art_deferred: true, max_supply: 5 })
        .eq('id', deferred)
      if (set.error) return `deferred: ${set.error.message}`
      const hash = nextFileHash()
      const upload = await client.rpc('record_upload', {
        p_collection_id: deferred,
        p_kind: 'placeholder',
        p_storage_key: `${deferred}/placeholder/${hash}.png`,
        p_content_hash: hash,
        p_mime: 'image/png',
        p_media_type: 'image',
        p_bytes: 1234,
        p_width: 1000,
        p_height: 1000,
        p_file_name: 'placeholder.png',
      })
      if (upload.error) return `upload: ${upload.error.message}`
      const lock = await client.rpc('art_lock_placeholder', {
        p_collection_id: deferred,
        p_placeholder_upload_id: upload.data as string,
      })
      if (lock.error) return `placeholder lock: ${lock.error.message}`
      const jobId = (lock.data as { id: string }).id
      const rendering = await finishRender(
        jobId,
        CID('p'),
        CID('q'),
        `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json`,
        CID('p'),
      )
      if (rendering) return rendering
      const link = await client.rpc('reserve_slug', { p_collection_id: deferred, p_slug: slugFor(deferred) })
      if (link.error) return `link: ${link.error.message}`
      const ready = await client.rpc('collection_placeholder_ready', { p_collection_id: deferred })
      if (ready.error) return `ready: ${ready.error.message}`
      return ready.data === true ? null : 'the placeholder is not ready'
    },
  )

  const deferredForm: DeployForm = { ...form, groups: [{ name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 5 }] }
  await check('13. confirmDeployCore: a transaction recorded for another collection is refused', async () => {
    if (!deployHash) return 'not deployed'
    const dctx = await deployContextFor(client, userId, creatorKey.address, deferred)
    const outcome = await confirmDeployCore(dctx, deployHash, deferredForm)
    if (refusal(outcome) !== DEPLOY_USED) return refusal(outcome)
    return (await collectionRow(deferred)).status === 'draft' ? null : 'the collection changed'
  })

  await check(
    '14. deploy: the deferred collection deploys on the chain the creator picked, on its placeholder base URI, with nothing to reveal to',
    async () => {
      const dctx = await deployContextFor(client, userId, creatorKey.address, deferred)
      const outcome = await prepareCore(dctx, deferredForm)
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return `prepare: ${refusal(outcome)}`
      const dprepared = outcome.data
      if (dprepared.chainId !== 31337) return `chain ${dprepared.chainId}`
      if (dprepared.baseUri !== `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json`) return `base URI ${dprepared.baseUri}`
      if (dprepared.revealsTo !== null) return `reveals to ${dprepared.revealsTo}`
      const expected = predictCollectionAddress({
        factory,
        deployer: creatorKey.address,
        salt: collectionSalt(deferred),
        creationCode: COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE,
        args: dprepared.encodedArgs,
      })
      if (dprepared.predicted !== expected) return `predicted ${dprepared.predicted}, expected ${expected}`
      const hash = await deploy(creatorKey, dprepared.encodedArgs, dprepared.salt)
      const confirmed = await confirmDeployCore(dctx, hash, deferredForm)
      if (!('data' in confirmed)) return `confirm: ${refusal(confirmed)}`
      const onChain = await chain.readContract({
        address: confirmed.data.contractAddress,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'baseURI',
      })
      const state = await collectionRow(deferred)
      return confirmed.data.contractAddress === dprepared.predicted &&
        onChain === `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json` &&
        state.status === 'deployed' &&
        state.chain_id === 31337 &&
        confirmed.data.groups.length === 1
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify(confirmed.data)} ${onChain} ${JSON.stringify(state)}`
    },
  )
}

async function cleanup(): Promise<void> {
  await check('15. cleanup: the run leaves no job, deployment, collection or user behind', removeFixtures)
}

await runChecks(main, cleanup)
```

Run: `pnpm --filter @cobweb/web verify:deploy`
Expected: the same fifteen PASS lines as before the change, then `15 passed, 0 failed`.

- [ ] **Step 3: verify:phases**

Create `apps/web/scripts/verify-phases.ts`:

```ts
// Checks Phases against a LOCAL Anvil chain and LOCAL Supabase (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section
// 10): a collection deployed through the factory with Public (free, 1) and Holders (0.001 ETH, 1) of three
// tokens reads from the chain; every change is built with phaseCalls, simulated as wagmi does, sent from the
// creator's key (the contract's owner) and confirmed through the core from its receipt: Add, Open, a past
// schedule, Clear, Close after a schedule, Price, the minted floor, the public limit, Close all; a group
// registered outside the flow is named; hide and show, the public sale refused, Move up, a taken name
// refused; then the confirm's refusals (another collection, another change, an add that did not happen, a
// stranger) and the read's failure path. Twenty PASS lines with the cleanup.
//
//   supabase start
//   pnpm --filter @cobweb/web chain:local    (then copy its two lines into apps/web/.env.local)
//   pnpm --filter @cobweb/web verify:phases
//
// The fixture (scripts/deploy-fixture.ts) reads the settings from the environment, `supabase status -o env`
// and apps/web/.env.local, and refuses non-local endpoints. Never prints a key. Deletes every row it
// creates. Runs under `node --conditions=react-server --import ./scripts/resolve-ts.mjs`.

import { createWalletClient, http, parseEther, type Address, type Hex } from 'viem'
import { generatePrivateKey, privateKeyToAccount, type PrivateKeyAccount } from 'viem/accounts'
import { anvil } from 'viem/chains'
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import {
  admin,
  chain,
  chainReady,
  check,
  deployedCollection,
  readDeployment,
  refusal,
  removeFixtures,
  rpc,
  runChecks,
  settings,
  signIn,
  type Deployed,
  type DeployForm,
} from './deploy-fixture.ts'

const { confirmPhaseTxCore, movePhaseCore, phasesViewCore, renamePhaseCore, setPhaseVisibilityCore } =
  await import('../src/lib/manage/phases-core.ts')
const { callOf, expectedEvent, phaseCalls, sentOf } = await import('../src/lib/manage/phase-calls.ts')
const { parseSentCall } = await import('../src/lib/manage/phase-schemas.ts')
const { phasesWriteError } = await import('../src/lib/art/db-error.ts')
const { classifyError } = await import('../src/components/deploy/tx-errors.ts')
const { NOT_DEPLOYED, NOT_FROM_WALLET, NOT_THIS_CHANGE, NOT_THIS_CONTRACT, PHASE_NAME_TAKEN, PUBLIC_SHOWN } =
  await import('../src/lib/art/result.ts')
type ManageCall = import('../src/lib/manage/phase-calls.ts').ManageCall
type PhasesContext = import('../src/lib/manage/phases-core.ts').PhasesContext
type PhasesView = import('../src/lib/manage/phases-types.ts').PhasesView

const HOLDERS_PRICE = '1000000000000000'
const BELOW_MINTED = 'It cannot go below 1, the tokens this phase has already minted. Nothing was sent.'

type PhaseRow = {
  onchain_id: number
  name: string
  description: string
  visible: boolean
  position: number | null
  price_wei: string
  max_supply: number
  start_at: string | null
}

/** The collection's rows in the creator's order, as the service role reads them. */
async function rowsOf(cid: string): Promise<PhaseRow[]> {
  const { data, error } = await admin
    .from('mint_groups')
    .select('onchain_id, name, description, visible, position, price_wei::text, max_supply, start_at')
    .eq('collection_id', cid)
    .order('position')
  if (error) throw new Error(`rows: ${error.message}`)
  return data as unknown as PhaseRow[]
}

type CopyRow = { public_max_per_wallet: number; state_block: number | null; state_read_at: string | null }

/** The deployment's copy of the public limit and the read it came from. */
async function copyOf(cid: string): Promise<CopyRow> {
  const { data, error } = await admin
    .from('deployments')
    .select('public_max_per_wallet, state_block, state_read_at')
    .eq('collection_id', cid)
    .single()
  if (error) throw new Error(`copy: ${error.message}`)
  return data
}

/** Simulates the call as wagmi does, sends exactly the simulated request from the account and waits. */
async function send(account: PrivateKeyAccount, address: Address, call: ManageCall): Promise<Hex> {
  const { request } = await chain.simulateContract({
    address,
    abi: collectionAbi,
    functionName: call.functionName,
    args: call.args,
    account,
  })
  const wallet = createWalletClient({ account, chain: anvil, transport: http(rpc) })
  const hash = await wallet.writeContract(request)
  const receipt = await chain.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash })
  if (receipt.status !== 'success') throw new Error(`${call.functionName} reverted: ${hash}`)
  return hash
}

/** The event the confirm action expects for a call, after the call's trip through SentCall and its shape. */
function expectedFor(call: ManageCall) {
  const sent = parseSentCall(sentOf(call))
  if (!sent.ok) throw new Error(`the sent call is refused: ${sent.error}`)
  return expectedEvent(callOf(sent.data))
}

/** `id:name` in the view's order. */
const order = (view: PhasesView) => view.groups.map((group) => `${group.id}:${group.name}`).join(' ')

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  console.log(`Verifying phases on ${rpc} against ${settings.url}`)
  await check(
    '1. chain: Anvil answers as 31337 and the factory approves the shipped creation code hash',
    chainReady,
  )

  const creatorKey = privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey())
  const strangerKey = privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey())
  await chain.setBalance({ address: creatorKey.address, value: parseEther('10') })
  await chain.setBalance({ address: strangerKey.address, value: parseEther('10') })
  const creator = await signIn(creatorKey)
  const stranger = await signIn(strangerKey)

  const form: DeployForm = {
    chainId: 31337,
    payout: creatorKey.address,
    royaltyBps: 500,
    royaltyReceiver: creatorKey.address,
    publicMaxPerWallet: 2,
    groups: [
      { name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 1 },
      { name: 'Holders', priceWei: HOLDERS_PRICE, maxSupply: 1 },
    ],
  }
  let deployed: Deployed | null = null
  /** The collection check 2 deploys; every later check runs on it. */
  const subject = (): Deployed => {
    if (!deployed) throw new Error('the collection of check 2 is not deployed')
    return deployed
  }
  /** Sends a change from the creator and confirms it through the core, as the confirm action does. */
  const change = async (call: ManageCall): Promise<{ view: PhasesView; hash: Hex }> => {
    const { ctx, record } = subject()
    const hash = await send(creatorKey, record.contractAddress, call)
    const outcome = await confirmPhaseTxCore(ctx, hash, { event: expectedFor(call) })
    if (!('data' in outcome)) throw new Error(`confirm ${call.functionName}: ${refusal(outcome)}`)
    return { view: outcome.data, hash }
  }
  const groupOf = (view: PhasesView, id: number) => view.groups.find((group) => group.id === id)

  await check(
    '2. read: a collection deployed with Public (free, 1) and Holders (0.001 ETH, 1) of three tokens reads both named and closed, with the copies and state_block',
    async () => {
      deployed = await deployedCollection(creator, form, 'phases')
      const outcome = await phasesViewCore(deployed.ctx)
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return refusal(outcome)
      const view = outcome.data
      const groups = view.groups.map((group) => [
        group.id,
        group.name,
        group.named,
        group.priceWei,
        group.flag,
        group.active,
      ])
      if (
        JSON.stringify(groups) !==
        JSON.stringify([
          [0, 'Public', true, '0', false, false],
          [1, 'Holders', true, HOLDERS_PRICE, false, false],
        ])
      )
        return `groups ${JSON.stringify(groups)}`
      if (
        !view.chainReachable ||
        view.publicMaxPerWallet !== 2 ||
        view.totalMinted !== 0 ||
        view.maxSupply !== 3
      )
        return `view ${JSON.stringify({ ...view, groups: undefined })}`
      const copy = await copyOf(deployed.cid)
      const rows = await rowsOf(deployed.cid)
      return copy.state_block !== null &&
        copy.public_max_per_wallet === 2 &&
        copy.state_read_at !== null &&
        Date.parse(copy.state_read_at) / 1000 === view.readAt &&
        rows
          .map((row) => `${row.onchain_id}:${row.price_wei}:${row.max_supply}:${row.position}`)
          .join(' ') === `0:0:1:0 1:${HOLDERS_PRICE}:1:1`
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify(copy)} ${JSON.stringify(rows)}`
    },
  )

  await check(
    '3. add: nextGroupId reads 2; registerMintGroup is sent; no row before the confirm; the confirm records Allowlist, described, hidden and last',
    async () => {
      const { cid, ctx, record } = subject()
      const ids = await chain.readContract({
        address: record.contractAddress,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'mintGroups',
      })
      const nextId = Number(ids.reduce((highest, id) => (id > highest ? id : highest), 0n)) + 1
      if (nextId !== 2) return `next id ${nextId}`
      const call = phaseCalls.register(nextId, '500000000000000', 1, 0)
      const hash = await send(creatorKey, record.contractAddress, call)
      if ((await rowsOf(cid)).some((row) => row.onchain_id === 2)) return 'a row exists before the confirm'
      const outcome = await confirmPhaseTxCore(ctx, hash, {
        event: { eventName: 'MintGroupRegistered', args: { mintGroup: 2n } },
        add: { onchainId: 2, name: 'Allowlist', description: 'Friends of the project.', visible: false },
      })
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return refusal(outcome)
      const added = outcome.data.groups.at(-1)
      const row = (await rowsOf(cid)).at(-1)
      return added?.id === 2 &&
        added.name === 'Allowlist' &&
        added.description === 'Friends of the project.' &&
        added.visible === false &&
        added.named === true &&
        added.priceWei === '500000000000000' &&
        row?.onchain_id === 2 &&
        row.position === 2
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify(added)} ${JSON.stringify(row)}`
    },
  )

  await check(
    '4. open: setMintGroupStatus(2, true); the view reads Allowlist live from the contract',
    async () => {
      const { view } = await change(phaseCalls.open(2))
      const group = groupOf(view, 2)
      return group?.flag === true && group.active === true ? null : JSON.stringify(group)
    },
  )

  await check(
    '5. schedule: a start already past on the closed Holders is allowed and reads live with its flag off; start_at is copied',
    async () => {
      const { cid } = subject()
      const block = await chain.getBlock()
      const start = Number(block.timestamp) - 60
      const { view } = await change(phaseCalls.schedule(1, start))
      const group = groupOf(view, 1)
      const row = (await rowsOf(cid)).find((entry) => entry.onchain_id === 1)
      return group?.flag === false &&
        group.active === true &&
        group.scheduledStart === start &&
        row?.start_at !== null &&
        row?.start_at !== undefined &&
        Date.parse(row.start_at) / 1000 === start
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify(group)} ${JSON.stringify(row)}`
    },
  )

  await check(
    '6. clear: scheduleMintGroupStart(1, 0) reads Holders closed with no start; start_at is null',
    async () => {
      const { cid } = subject()
      const { view } = await change(phaseCalls.clearSchedule(1))
      const group = groupOf(view, 1)
      const row = (await rowsOf(cid)).find((entry) => entry.onchain_id === 1)
      return group?.active === false && group.scheduledStart === 0 && row?.start_at === null
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify(group)} ${JSON.stringify(row)}`
    },
  )

  await check(
    '7. close: after a start a day ahead, setMintGroupStatus(1, false) leaves the flag off and clears the start',
    async () => {
      const block = await chain.getBlock()
      const scheduled = await change(phaseCalls.schedule(1, Number(block.timestamp) + 86_400))
      if (groupOf(scheduled.view, 1)?.scheduledStart === 0) return 'the start was not set'
      const { view } = await change(phaseCalls.close(1))
      const group = groupOf(view, 1)
      return group?.flag === false && group.scheduledStart === 0 && group.active === false
        ? null
        : JSON.stringify(group)
    },
  )

  let priceHash: Hex | null = null
  await check('8. price: changeSalePrice(0.002 ETH, 1) reads in the view and in price_wei', async () => {
    const { cid } = subject()
    const { view, hash } = await change(phaseCalls.price(1, '2000000000000000'))
    priceHash = hash
    const row = (await rowsOf(cid)).find((entry) => entry.onchain_id === 1)
    return groupOf(view, 1)?.priceWei === '2000000000000000' && row?.price_wei === '2000000000000000'
      ? null
      : `${JSON.stringify(groupOf(view, 1))} ${JSON.stringify(row)}`
  })

  let mintHash: Hex | null = null
  await check(
    '9. floor: with Public open and one token minted, setMintGroupMaxSupply(0, 0) is refused at the check with the sentence and nothing sent; Holders is lowered to 0',
    async () => {
      const { record } = subject()
      const address = record.contractAddress
      await change(phaseCalls.open(0))
      const [cost] = await chain.readContract({
        address,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'quoteBatchMint',
        args: [0n, 1n],
      })
      const minter = createWalletClient({ account: strangerKey, chain: anvil, transport: http(rpc) })
      mintHash = await minter.writeContract({
        address,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'batchMint',
        args: [1n, 0n],
        value: cost,
      })
      await chain.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: mintHash })
      const nonce = await chain.getTransactionCount({ address: creatorKey.address })
      let sentence = 'the simulation passed'
      try {
        const floor = phaseCalls.allocation(0, 0)
        await chain.simulateContract({
          address,
          abi: collectionAbi,
          functionName: floor.functionName,
          args: floor.args,
          account: creatorKey,
        })
      } catch (error) {
        sentence = classifyError(error, 'simulating', 'manage').message
      }
      if (sentence !== BELOW_MINTED) return sentence
      if ((await chain.getTransactionCount({ address: creatorKey.address })) !== nonce)
        return 'a transaction was sent'
      const { view } = await change(phaseCalls.allocation(1, 0))
      return groupOf(view, 1)?.maxSupply === 0 && groupOf(view, 0)?.minted === 1 && view.totalMinted === 1
        ? null
        : JSON.stringify(view.groups)
    },
  )

  await check(
    '10. public limit: setPublicMaxMintPerWallet(5) reads in the view and the copy; readDeployment keeps the launch values',
    async () => {
      const { cid } = subject()
      const { view } = await change(phaseCalls.publicCap(5))
      const copy = await copyOf(cid)
      const launch = await readDeployment(creator.client, cid)
      return view.publicMaxPerWallet === 5 &&
        copy.public_max_per_wallet === 5 &&
        launch?.publicMaxPerWallet === 2 &&
        launch.groups[1]?.priceWei === HOLDERS_PRICE &&
        launch.groups[1]?.maxSupply === 1
        ? null
        : `${view.publicMaxPerWallet} ${copy.public_max_per_wallet} ${JSON.stringify(launch)}`
    },
  )

  await check('11. close all: stopOrStartAllMintGroups(false) reads every phase closed', async () => {
    const { view } = await change(phaseCalls.closeAll())
    return view.groups.every((group) => !group.flag && !group.active && group.scheduledStart === 0)
      ? null
      : JSON.stringify(view.groups)
  })

  await check(
    '12. outside the flow: a group registered without Cobweb (id 9) reads as Group 9, unnamed, last; naming it records its row',
    async () => {
      const { cid, ctx, record } = subject()
      await send(creatorKey, record.contractAddress, phaseCalls.register(9, '0', 0, 0))
      const before = await phasesViewCore(ctx)
      if (!('data' in before)) return refusal(before)
      const unnamed = before.data.groups.at(-1)
      if (unnamed?.id !== 9 || unnamed.name !== 'Group 9' || unnamed.named !== false)
        return `before ${JSON.stringify(unnamed)}`
      const named = await renamePhaseCore(ctx, 9, { name: 'Late list', description: 'Registered elsewhere.' })
      if (!('data' in named)) return `rename: ${refusal(named)}`
      const after = await phasesViewCore(ctx)
      if (!('data' in after)) return refusal(after)
      const row = (await rowsOf(cid)).find((entry) => entry.onchain_id === 9)
      return groupOf(after.data, 9)?.name === 'Late list' &&
        groupOf(after.data, 9)?.named === true &&
        row?.position === 3
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify(groupOf(after.data, 9))} ${JSON.stringify(row)}`
    },
  )

  await check(
    '13. visibility: Allowlist shown, then hidden again; the public sale cannot be hidden',
    async () => {
      const { cid, ctx } = subject()
      const shown = await setPhaseVisibilityCore(ctx, 2, true)
      if (!('data' in shown)) return `show: ${refusal(shown)}`
      const visibleAfterShow = (await rowsOf(cid)).find((row) => row.onchain_id === 2)?.visible
      const hidden = await setPhaseVisibilityCore(ctx, 2, false)
      if (!('data' in hidden)) return `hide: ${refusal(hidden)}`
      const publicSale = await setPhaseVisibilityCore(ctx, 0, false)
      if (refusal(publicSale) !== PUBLIC_SHOWN) return `public sale: ${refusal(publicSale)}`
      const rows = await rowsOf(cid)
      return visibleAfterShow === true &&
        rows.find((row) => row.onchain_id === 2)?.visible === false &&
        rows.find((row) => row.onchain_id === 0)?.visible === true
        ? null
        : JSON.stringify(rows)
    },
  )

  await check(
    '14. order: Move up swaps Late list above Allowlist; the first phase moved up stays first',
    async () => {
      const { ctx } = subject()
      const moved = await movePhaseCore(ctx, 9, 'up')
      if (!('data' in moved)) return `move: ${refusal(moved)}`
      const top = await movePhaseCore(ctx, 0, 'up')
      if (!('data' in top)) return `top: ${refusal(top)}`
      const view = await phasesViewCore(ctx)
      if (!('data' in view)) return refusal(view)
      return order(view.data) === '0:Public 1:Holders 9:Late list 2:Allowlist' ? null : order(view.data)
    },
  )

  await check("15. names: another phase's name in other case is refused as taken", async () => {
    const { cid, ctx } = subject()
    const taken = await renamePhaseCore(ctx, 2, { name: 'HOLDERS', description: '' })
    if (!('error' in taken)) return `answered ${refusal(taken)}`
    const sentence = phasesWriteError(taken.error)
    if (sentence !== PHASE_NAME_TAKEN) return sentence
    return (await rowsOf(cid)).find((row) => row.onchain_id === 2)?.name === 'Allowlist'
      ? null
      : 'the name changed'
  })

  await check("16. confirm: a change sent to another collection's contract is refused", async () => {
    const { ctx } = subject()
    const other = await deployedCollection(
      creator,
      { ...form, groups: [{ name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 3 }] },
      'other',
    )
    const hash = await send(creatorKey, other.record.contractAddress, phaseCalls.open(0))
    const outcome = await confirmPhaseTxCore(ctx, hash, { event: expectedFor(phaseCalls.open(0)) })
    return refusal(outcome) === NOT_THIS_CONTRACT ? null : refusal(outcome)
  })

  await check(
    "17. confirm: the price change's hash is not an Open of group 0, nor an add of id 3, which writes no row",
    async () => {
      const { cid, ctx } = subject()
      if (!priceHash) return 'no price change'
      const asOpen = await confirmPhaseTxCore(ctx, priceHash, { event: expectedFor(phaseCalls.open(0)) })
      if (refusal(asOpen) !== NOT_THIS_CHANGE) return `as Open: ${refusal(asOpen)}`
      const asAdd = await confirmPhaseTxCore(ctx, priceHash, {
        event: { eventName: 'MintGroupRegistered', args: { mintGroup: 3n } },
        add: { onchainId: 3, name: 'Ghost', description: '', visible: true },
      })
      if (refusal(asAdd) !== NOT_THIS_CHANGE) return `as add: ${refusal(asAdd)}`
      return (await rowsOf(cid)).some((row) => row.onchain_id === 3) ? 'a row was written for id 3' : null
    },
  )

  await check(
    "18. a stranger's session reads no deployment and no rows; a stranger's transaction is not the creator's change; the stranger cannot move a phase",
    async () => {
      const { cid, ctx } = subject()
      if (!mintHash) return 'no mint from the stranger'
      const strangerCtx: PhasesContext = {
        supabase: stranger.client,
        service: () => admin,
        owner: stranger.userId,
        wallet: strangerKey.address.toLowerCase(),
        collection: ctx.collection,
      }
      const read = await phasesViewCore(strangerCtx)
      if (refusal(read) !== NOT_DEPLOYED) return `read: ${refusal(read)}`
      const rows = await stranger.client.from('mint_groups').select('id').eq('collection_id', cid)
      if (rows.error) return `rows: ${rows.error.message}`
      if (rows.data.length) return `the stranger reads ${rows.data.length} row(s)`
      const fromStranger = await confirmPhaseTxCore(ctx, mintHash, { event: expectedFor(phaseCalls.open(0)) })
      if (refusal(fromStranger) !== NOT_FROM_WALLET) return `stranger's transaction: ${refusal(fromStranger)}`
      const before = (await rowsOf(cid)).map((row) => `${row.onchain_id}:${row.position}`).join(' ')
      const moved = await movePhaseCore(strangerCtx, 2, 'up')
      if (refusal(moved) !== NOT_DEPLOYED) return `move: ${refusal(moved)}`
      const raw = await stranger.client.rpc('mint_group_move', {
        p_collection_id: cid,
        p_onchain_id: 2,
        p_up: true,
      })
      if (raw.error?.code !== '42501') return `raw move: ${raw.error?.code ?? 'accepted'}`
      const after = (await rowsOf(cid)).map((row) => `${row.onchain_id}:${row.position}`).join(' ')
      return before === after ? null : `${before} became ${after}`
    },
  )

  await check(
    '19. failure path: a read that does not answer gives the last copy, unreachable, with its block time as readAt',
    async () => {
      const { cid, ctx } = subject()
      const outcome = await phasesViewCore(ctx, async () => null)
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return refusal(outcome)
      const view = outcome.data
      const copy = await copyOf(cid)
      const readAt = copy.state_read_at === null ? null : Date.parse(copy.state_read_at) / 1000
      return !view.chainReachable &&
        view.readAt === readAt &&
        view.blockTimestamp === readAt &&
        view.totalMinted === 0 &&
        view.publicMaxPerWallet === 5 &&
        view.maxSupply === 3 &&
        order(view) === '0:Public 1:Holders 9:Late list 2:Allowlist' &&
        view.groups.every((group) => group.minted === 0 && !group.active)
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify({ ...view, groups: order(view) })} ${JSON.stringify(copy)}`
    },
  )
}

async function cleanup(): Promise<void> {
  await check(
    '20. cleanup: the run leaves no job, deployment, phase, collection or user behind',
    removeFixtures,
  )
}

await runChecks(main, cleanup)
```

describe('the sample actions and server', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers()
  })
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers()
  })

  it('checks for 650 ms, sends on Approve, refuses on Reject, and reverts after Approve, then revert', async () => {
    const sent = new Map<Hex, ManageCall>()
    const actions = sampleManageActions(() => view, sent)

    const simulating = actions.simulate(phaseCalls.open(2))
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(650)
    const send = await simulating
    const sending = send()
    actions.answer?.('approve')
    const hash = await sending
    expect(hash).toMatch(/^0x[0-9a-f]{64}$/)
    expect(sent.get(hash)).toEqual(phaseCalls.open(2))
    const waiting = actions.wait(hash)
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1600)
    expect(await waiting).toBe('success')

    const again = actions.simulate(phaseCalls.open(2))
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(650)
    const rejected = (await again)()
    actions.answer?.('reject')
    await expect(rejected).rejects.toMatchObject({ name: 'UserRejectedRequestError', code: 4001 })

    const third = actions.simulate(phaseCalls.open(2))
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(650)
    const reverting = (await third)()
    actions.answer?.('approve-revert')
    const revertHash = await reverting
    const revertWait = actions.wait(revertHash)
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1600)
    expect(await revertWait).toBe('reverted')
  })

  it('refuses at the check what the contract would refuse, and checks a sample tab by its review', async () => {
    const actions = sampleManageActions(() => view)
    const refused = actions.simulate(phaseCalls.allocation(1, 100))
    const expectation = expect(refused).rejects.toMatchObject({ name: 'ContractFunctionRevertedError' })
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(650)
    await expectation
    const review = actions.review?.({
      contract: 'Collection',
      functionName: 'airdropNFTs',
      args: [],
      check: () => 'ExceedsMaxSupply',
    })
    const reviewExpectation = expect(review).rejects.toMatchObject({
      shortMessage: 'The contract would reject this with ExceedsMaxSupply.',
    })
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(650)
    await reviewExpectation
  })

  it('answers the next free id, and records an add with the call the wallet sent', async () => {
    const sent = new Map<Hex, ManageCall>()
    const actions = sampleManageActions(() => view, sent)
    const server = samplePhasesServer(() => view, sent)
    expect(await actions.nextGroupId()).toBe(3)
    const hash: Hex = `0x${'cd'.repeat(32)}`
    sent.set(hash, phaseCalls.register(3, '1000', 20, 0))
    const recording = server.record(hash, { onchainId: 3, name: 'Holders', description: '', visible: true })
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(900)
    const result = await recording
    expect(result.ok && result.data.groups.at(-1)).toMatchObject({
      id: 3,
      name: 'Holders',
      priceWei: '1000',
      maxSupply: 20,
    })
  })

  it('answers the hash for a tab still on sample data', async () => {
    const hash: Hex = `0x${'ef'.repeat(32)}`
    const request = sampleRequest({ contract: 'Collection', functionName: 'withdrawMintFunds', args: [] })
    expect(request.call).toBeNull()
    expect(await request.confirm(hash)).toEqual({ ok: true, data: hash })
  })
})

- [ ] **Step 3: The browser's actions, the context and the bound server**

Create `apps/web/src/components/manage/manage-actions.ts`:

```ts
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import type { Address, Hex } from 'viem'
import type { Config } from 'wagmi'
import {
  readContract,
  simulateContract,
  switchChain,
  waitForTransactionReceipt,
  writeContract,
} from 'wagmi/actions'
import type { ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { ManageCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'

/**
 * What a change in Manage drop does in the browser, in one shape for both modes
 * (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.3): `realManageActions` runs wagmi on the collection,
 * `sampleManageActions` (manage-sim.ts) simulates. `TransactionDialog` builds the manage machine's deps
 * from these, the request's confirm and the timers.
 */

/** A contract call as the creator reviews it before signing. */
export type TxRequest = {
  /** The contract the call goes to. */
  contract: 'Collection' | 'Transfer validator'
  /** The function name exactly as in the ABI, e.g. `setMintGroupStatus`. */
  functionName: string
  /** Arguments in ABI order, already formatted for reading. */
  args: { name: string; value: string }[]
  /** Native amount sent with the call, in wei. */
  valueWei?: string
  /**
   * Tabs still on sample data only: the contract's own check mirrored in the browser, answering the
   * error's name or null. The sample actions run it at "Checking the change".
   */
  check?: () => string | null
}

export type ManageActions = {
  switchChain(chainId: number): Promise<void>
  /** Simulates the call on the collection; resolves with the function that sends the simulated request, unchanged. */
  simulate(call: ManageCall): Promise<() => Promise<Hex>>
  wait(hash: Hex): Promise<'success' | 'reverted'>
  /** The id Add phase registers: the highest of `mintGroups()` plus one (0 is the public sale). */
  nextGroupId(): Promise<number>
  /** Sample only: the simulated wallet's answer. */
  answer?(choice: 'approve' | 'reject' | 'approve-revert'): void
  /** Sample only: a tab still on sample data has no call, so its review's `check` stands in for the simulation. */
  review?(review: TxRequest): Promise<() => Promise<Hex>>
}

/** One change as a dialog sends it: the call, what the creator reviews, and how the server confirms it. */
export type ManageRequest<T> = {
  /** Null on a tab still on sample data. */
  call: ManageCall | null
  review: TxRequest
  confirm(hash: Hex): Promise<ActionResult<T>>
  /** Add phase only: keeps the sent hash until the server records the phase. */
  pending?: { remember(hash: Hex): void; forget(): void }
}

/** The real actions: wagmi switches, simulates on the collection, sends the simulated request and waits. */
export function realManageActions(
  config: Config,
  target: { chainId: number; contract: Address; wallet: Address },
): ManageActions {
  return {
    switchChain: async (chainId) => {
      await switchChain(config, { chainId })
    },
    simulate: async (call) => {
      // The chain is named here and carried on the simulated request, so viem checks the send against it.
      // The account is the sign-in wallet: wagmi refuses to send the request from a wallet that does not hold it.
      const simulated = await simulateContract(config, {
        address: target.contract,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: call.functionName,
        args: call.args,
        chainId: target.chainId,
        account: target.wallet,
      })
      // CLAUDE.md rule 2: the wallet gets exactly the request the simulation passed.
      return () => writeContract(config, simulated.request)
    },
    wait: async (hash) => (await waitForTransactionReceipt(config, { hash, chainId: target.chainId })).status,
    nextGroupId: async () => {
      const ids = await readContract(config, {
        address: target.contract,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'mintGroups',
        chainId: target.chainId,
      })
      return Number(ids.reduce((highest, id) => (id > highest ? id : highest), 0n)) + 1
    },
  }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/components/manage/phases-server.ts`:

```ts
import type { Hex } from 'viem'
import {
  confirmPhaseTx,
  getPhases,
  movePhase,
  recordPhase,
  renamePhase,
  setPhaseVisibility,
} from '@/app/actions/phases'
import type { ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { sentOf, type ManageCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import type { PhaseAdd, PhasesView } from '@/lib/manage/phases-types'

/**
 * Phases' server, bound to one collection (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.3): the six actions
 * in one object, which `samplePhasesServer` (manage-sim.ts) mirrors in sample mode.
 */
export type PhasesServer = {
  read(): Promise<ActionResult<PhasesView>>
  confirm(hash: Hex, call: ManageCall): Promise<ActionResult<PhasesView>>
  record(hash: Hex, add: PhaseAdd): Promise<ActionResult<PhasesView>>
  rename(onchainId: number, details: { name: string; description: string }): Promise<ActionResult>
  setVisibility(onchainId: number, visible: boolean): Promise<ActionResult>
  move(onchainId: number, direction: 'up' | 'down'): Promise<ActionResult>
}

export function phasesServer(collectionId: string): PhasesServer {
  return {
    read: () => getPhases(collectionId),
    confirm: (hash, call) => confirmPhaseTx(collectionId, hash, sentOf(call)),
    record: (hash, add) => recordPhase(collectionId, hash, add),
    rename: (onchainId, details) => renamePhase(collectionId, onchainId, details),
    setVisibility: (onchainId, visible) => setPhaseVisibility(collectionId, onchainId, visible),
    move: (onchainId, direction) => movePhase(collectionId, onchainId, direction),
  }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/components/manage/manage-context.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { createContext, useContext } from 'react'
import type { Address } from 'viem'
import { DEMO_CREATOR, DEMO_DEPLOYMENT } from '@/lib/demo'
import type { ManageActions } from './manage-actions'
import { sampleManageActions, samplePhasesView } from './manage-sim'

/**
 * The actions every `TransactionDialog` in Manage drop runs on (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section
 * 7.3), with the collection's chain and the sign-in wallet the machine checks against. Phases provides the
 * real or the sample ones; the tabs still on sample data (Whitelist, Airdrop, Settings, the Overview's
 * Reveal) run on one shared sample target until their sub-projects.
 */
export type ManageTarget = { actions: ManageActions; chainId: number; wallet: Address }

const ManageContext = createContext<ManageTarget | null>(null)

let sampleTarget: ManageTarget | null = null

/** The shared sample target, made on first use. */
function sampleFallback(): ManageTarget {
  sampleTarget ??= {
    actions: sampleManageActions(() => samplePhasesView({ max_supply: null })),
    chainId: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.chainId,
    wallet: DEMO_CREATOR,
  }
  return sampleTarget
}

export function ManageActionsProvider({
  value,
  children,
}: {
  value: ManageTarget
  children: React.ReactNode
}) {
  return <ManageContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ManageContext.Provider>
}

export function useManageActions(): ManageTarget {
  return useContext(ManageContext) ?? sampleFallback()
}
```

Then replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/components/manage/manage-sim.ts` with:

```ts
/**
 * Sample mode for Manage drop (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 8), and the sample values the tabs
 * still on sample data read. Nothing here talks to a wallet, an RPC or the database: `sampleManageActions`
 * and `samplePhasesServer` have the shape of `realManageActions` (manage-actions.ts) and `phasesServer`
 * (phases-server.ts), so Phases runs the same code in both modes. Every simulated change goes through
 * the manage machine and the same dialog.
 */
import { collectionAbi } from '@cobweb/shared/collection-abi'
import { ContractFunctionRevertedError, encodeErrorResult } from 'viem'
import {
  DEMO_COLLECTION,
  DEMO_DEPLOYMENT,
  DEMO_MINT_PAGE_SETTINGS,
  DEMO_NOW,
  DEMO_PUBLIC_MAX_PER_WALLET,
  DEMO_RENDER_JOBS,
  DEMO_WHITELISTS,
  sampleSupply,
  sampleTxHash,
  scaleSampleToSupply,
} from '@/lib/demo'
import type { Address, Hex, MintGroup } from '@/lib/demo'
import type { ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { phaseCalls, type ManageCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import type { PhaseAdd, PhasesView } from '@/lib/manage/phases-types'
import type { ManageActions, ManageRequest, TxRequest } from './manage-actions'
import type { PendingAdd } from './manage-machine'
import type { PhasesServer } from './phases-server'

const wait = (ms: number) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))

/** A fresh transaction hash for a simulated send. */
export function simulatedTxHash(): Hex {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(32)
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes)
  return `0x${Array.from(bytes, (byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')}`
}

/**
 * What the sample chain reports for a group after a change lands: the contract's `mintGroupActive` (flag
 * on, or a schedule reached with `block.timestamp >= start`) on the sample's clock. Sample mode only: real
 * screens take `active` from the server's read of `collectionState()`.
 */
export function chainReadGroup(group: Omit<MintGroup, 'active'>, blockTimestamp: number): MintGroup {
  const scheduledReached = group.scheduledStart !== 0 && blockTimestamp >= group.scheduledStart
  return { ...group, active: group.flag || scheduledReached }
}

/** Sample values the tabs still on sample data read beyond their props. Each names the real source. */
export const SAMPLE = {
  /** SEAM: the freshness server's `readAt` / block timestamp for the collection. */
  readAt: DEMO_NOW,
  /** SEAM: whitelist entries and the chunk table. */
  whitelists: DEMO_WHITELISTS,
  /** SEAM: the mint page settings row (avatar and the public switch on the overview). */
  mintPage: DEMO_MINT_PAGE_SETTINGS,
  /**
   * SEAM: the collection's `getTransferValidator()`. Limit Break's default validator, the
   * `DEFAULT_TRANSFER_VALIDATOR` constant of creator-token-standards, same address on every chain.
   */
  transferValidator: '0x721C008fdff27BF06E7E123956E2Fe03B63342e3' as Address,
  /** SEAM: `render_jobs.metadata_cid` of the finished render job, the reveal target. */
  metadataCid: DEMO_RENDER_JOBS.done.metadataCid,
} as const

/**
 * Saves off-chain settings (mint page details).
 * SEAM: a server action that checks the session and ownership, then writes the settings row.
 */
export async function saveOffChain(): Promise<void> {
  await wait(900)
}

/**
 * Today's sample phases scaled to the collection's supply, as a `PhasesView`. With `chainReachable` false,
 * the copy: what Cobweb last read, without status or minted counts (`?chain=unreachable`).
 */
export function samplePhasesView(
  collection: { max_supply: number | null },
  chainReachable = true,
): PhasesView {
  const maxSupply = sampleSupply(collection.max_supply, DEMO_COLLECTION.maxSupply)
  const sample = scaleSampleToSupply(maxSupply)
  const view: PhasesView = {
    chainId: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.chainId,
    contract: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.contractAddress,
    groups: sample.groups,
    publicMaxPerWallet: DEMO_PUBLIC_MAX_PER_WALLET,
    totalMinted: sample.totalMinted,
    maxSupply,
    blockTimestamp: DEMO_NOW,
    readAt: DEMO_NOW,
    chainReachable: true,
  }
  if (chainReachable) return view
  return {
    ...view,
    groups: view.groups.map((group) => ({ ...group, minted: 0, flag: false, active: false })),
    totalMinted: 0,
    chainReachable: false,
  }
}

/** A revert as viem decodes it, so `classifyError` words it as it would the contract's. */
function revert(functionName: string, data: Hex): ContractFunctionRevertedError {
  return new ContractFunctionRevertedError({ abi: collectionAbi, data, functionName })
}

/**
 * How the contract would refuse a call on the sample view, or null when it would pass: the mirror of the
 * collection's group checks (an unknown group, an id taken, below minted, the allocations over the supply).
 */
export function sampleRevert(call: ManageCall, view: PhasesView): ContractFunctionRevertedError | null {
  const find = (id: bigint) => view.groups.find((group) => BigInt(group.id) === id)
  const missing = (id: bigint) =>
    revert(
      call.functionName,
      encodeErrorResult({ abi: collectionAbi, errorName: 'MintGroupDoesNotExist', args: [id] }),
    )
  const others = (id: bigint) =>
    view.groups
      .filter((group) => BigInt(group.id) !== id)
      .reduce((sum, group) => sum + BigInt(group.maxSupply), 0n)
  const over = () =>
    revert(
      call.functionName,
      encodeErrorResult({ abi: collectionAbi, errorName: 'AllocationsExceedMaxSupply' }),
    )
  switch (call.functionName) {
    case 'registerMintGroup': {
      const [id, , maxSupply] = call.args
      if (find(id))
        return revert(
          call.functionName,
          encodeErrorResult({ abi: collectionAbi, errorName: 'MintGroupAlreadyExists', args: [id] }),
        )
      return others(id) + maxSupply > BigInt(view.maxSupply) ? over() : null
    }
    case 'setMintGroupMaxSupply': {
      const [id, maxSupply] = call.args
      const group = find(id)
      if (!group) return missing(id)
      if (maxSupply < BigInt(group.minted))
        return revert(
          call.functionName,
          encodeErrorResult({ abi: collectionAbi, errorName: 'BelowMinted', args: [BigInt(group.minted)] }),
        )
      return others(id) + maxSupply > BigInt(view.maxSupply) ? over() : null
    }
    case 'setMintGroupStatus':
    case 'scheduleMintGroupStart':
      return find(call.args[0]) ? null : missing(call.args[0])
    case 'changeSalePrice':
      return find(call.args[1]) ? null : missing(call.args[1])
    default:
      return null
  }
}

/** The sample view after a change lands, `active` recomputed on the sample's clock. */
export function sampleApply(call: ManageCall, view: PhasesView, add?: PhaseAdd): PhasesView {
  const patch = (id: bigint, change: Partial<MintGroup>) => (group: MintGroup) =>
    BigInt(group.id) === id ? { ...group, ...change } : group
  let groups = view.groups
  let publicMaxPerWallet = view.publicMaxPerWallet
  switch (call.functionName) {
    case 'registerMintGroup': {
      const [id, price, maxSupply, start] = call.args
      groups = [
        ...groups,
        {
          id: Number(id),
          name: add?.name ?? `Group ${id}`,
          kind: 'whitelist',
          priceWei: price.toString(),
          maxSupply: Number(maxSupply),
          minted: 0,
          flag: false,
          scheduledStart: Number(start),
          active: false,
          description: add?.description ?? '',
          visible: add?.visible ?? true,
          named: add !== undefined,
        },
      ]
      break
    }
    case 'setMintGroupStatus': {
      const [id, status] = call.args
      groups = groups.map(patch(id, status ? { flag: true } : { flag: false, scheduledStart: 0 }))
      break
    }
    case 'stopOrStartAllMintGroups': {
      const [status] = call.args
      groups = groups.map((group) =>
        status ? { ...group, flag: true } : { ...group, flag: false, scheduledStart: 0 },
      )
      break
    }
    case 'scheduleMintGroupStart': {
      const [id, start] = call.args
      groups = groups.map(patch(id, { scheduledStart: Number(start) }))
      break
    }
    case 'changeSalePrice': {
      const [price, id] = call.args
      groups = groups.map(patch(id, { priceWei: price.toString() }))
      break
    }
    case 'setMintGroupMaxSupply': {
      const [id, maxSupply] = call.args
      groups = groups.map(patch(id, { maxSupply: Number(maxSupply) }))
      break
    }
    case 'setPublicMaxMintPerWallet':
      publicMaxPerWallet = Number(call.args[0])
      break
  }
  return {
    ...view,
    publicMaxPerWallet,
    groups: groups.map((group) => chainReadGroup(group, view.blockTimestamp)),
  }
}

type Choice = 'approve' | 'reject' | 'approve-revert'

function userRejected(): Error {
  return Object.assign(new Error('User rejected the request.'), {
    name: 'UserRejectedRequestError',
    code: 4001,
  })
}

/**
 * The sample actions. `simulate` waits 650 ms and refuses as the contract would on the current view;
 * the send waits for the simulated wallet's `answer`; `wait` takes 1.6 seconds. `sent` remembers each
 * sent call by its hash, so the sample server applies exactly what was signed.
 */
export function sampleManageActions(
  current: () => PhasesView,
  sent: Map<Hex, ManageCall> = new Map(),
): ManageActions {
  let settle: ((choice: Choice) => void) | null = null
  const reverting = new Set<Hex>()
  /** The simulated wallet: a hash on Approve, a rejection on Reject. */
  const walletFor = (call: ManageCall | null) => () =>
    new Promise<Hex>((resolve, reject) => {
      settle = (choice) => {
        settle = null
        if (choice === 'reject') return reject(userRejected())
        const hash = simulatedTxHash()
        if (choice === 'approve-revert') reverting.add(hash)
        if (call) sent.set(hash, call)
        resolve(hash)
      }
    })
  return {
    switchChain: async () => {},
    simulate: async (call) => {
      await wait(650)
      const refused = sampleRevert(call, current())
      if (refused) throw refused
      return walletFor(call)
    },
    review: async (review: TxRequest) => {
      await wait(650)
      const reason = review.check?.() ?? null
      if (reason)
        throw Object.assign(new Error(reason), {
          shortMessage: `The contract would reject this with ${reason}.`,
        })
      return walletFor(null)
    },
    wait: async (hash) => {
      await wait(1600)
      return reverting.has(hash) ? 'reverted' : 'success'
    },
    nextGroupId: async () => Math.max(0, ...current().groups.map((group) => group.id)) + 1,
    answer: (choice) => settle?.(choice),
  }
}

/**
 * The sample server: every answer takes 900 ms. A confirm applies the call; a record applies the call the
 * simulated wallet sent with that hash (a plain registration for `?pending=add`) and names the phase.
 */
export function samplePhasesServer(
  current: () => PhasesView,
  sent: Map<Hex, ManageCall> = new Map(),
): PhasesServer {
  const answer = async <T>(data: () => T): Promise<ActionResult<T>> => {
    await wait(900)
    return { ok: true, data: data() }
  }
  return {
    read: () => answer(current),
    confirm: (_hash, call) => answer(() => sampleApply(call, current())),
    record: (hash, add) =>
      answer(() =>
        sampleApply(sent.get(hash) ?? phaseCalls.register(add.onchainId, '0', 0, 0), current(), add),
      ),
    rename: () => answer(() => undefined),
    setVisibility: () => answer(() => undefined),
    move: () => answer(() => undefined),
  }
}

/** A tab still on sample data: its review, simulated by the sample actions, and the hash as the answer. */
export function sampleRequest(review: TxRequest): ManageRequest<Hex> {
  return { call: null, review, confirm: async (hash) => ({ ok: true, data: hash }) }
}

/** The add `?pending=add` shows waiting to be recorded. */
export function samplePendingAdd(view: PhasesView): PendingAdd {
  return {
    hash: sampleTxHash(31),
    form: {
      onchainId: Math.max(0, ...view.groups.map((group) => group.id)) + 1,
      name: 'Holders',
      description: 'Holders of the first collection.',
      visible: true,
    },
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The dialog on the machine**

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/components/manage/transaction-dialog.tsx` with:

```tsx
'use client'

import { CheckIcon, WalletIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useReducer, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import type { Hex } from 'viem'
import { useAccount } from 'wagmi'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@cobweb/ui/components/dialog'
import { cn } from '@cobweb/ui/lib/utils'
import { ChainName } from '@/components/chains/chain-icon'
import { deployReducer, retryHash, type DeployEvent } from '@/components/deploy/deploy-machine'
import { classifyError } from '@/components/deploy/tx-errors'
import {
  isTxRunning,
  TX_IDLE,
  TxProgress,
  type StepView,
  type TxFailure,
} from '@/components/deploy/tx-progress'
import { explorerTxUrl, formatNative, type SupportedChain } from '@/lib/demo'
import type { ManageRequest, TxRequest } from './manage-actions'
import { useManageActions } from './manage-context'
import { runManage } from './manage-machine'

export type TransactionDialogProps<T> = {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  title: string
  description?: React.ReactNode
  /** Fields or a summary, shown until the transaction starts. */
  children?: React.ReactNode
  /** The change to send; null while the form above is incomplete or invalid. */
  request: ManageRequest<T> | null
  /** The button that starts the sequence, e.g. "Open phase". */
  actionLabel: string
  /** Red action button, for irreversible or risky calls. */
  destructive?: boolean
  chain: SupportedChain
  /** Shown in the toast and the done state, e.g. "Owl List is open". */
  successMessage: string
  /** The server's answer once the change is confirmed: the fresh view on Phases, the hash on a tab still on sample data. */
  onConfirmed?: (result: T) => void
  /** The failure's sentence, when the sequence ends without a confirmed change. */
  onFailed?: (message: string) => void
  className?: string
}

/**
 * The one dialog for every on-chain change in Manage drop (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.3).
 * It shows what will be sent, then runs the manage machine with the context's actions, the sign-in wallet
 * and the wallet's account and chain: switch when needed, check, confirm in the wallet, confirming, and
 * reading the contract, with the failure at whichever step failed and a way forward. The simulated wallet
 * shows only on sample actions.
 */
export function TransactionDialog<T>({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  title,
  description,
  children,
  request,
  actionLabel,
  destructive,
  chain,
  successMessage,
  onConfirmed,
  onFailed,
  className,
}: TransactionDialogProps<T>) {
  const { actions, chainId, wallet } = useManageActions()
  const account = useAccount()
  const sample = actions.answer !== undefined
  const [phase, dispatch] = useReducer(deployReducer, TX_IDLE)
  const [switching, setSwitching] = useState(false)
  const run = useRef(0)
  const resetTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>(undefined)
  const running = isTxRunning(phase)
  // A change sent but not confirmed in time is checked again, never sent again.
  const sentHash = retryHash(phase)

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      // Unmounted: drop any step still in flight.
      run.current++
    },
    [],
  )

  function reset() {
    run.current++
    dispatch({ type: 'set', phase: TX_IDLE })
  }

  function handleOpenChange(next: boolean) {
    clearTimeout(resetTimer.current)
    // A transaction in flight keeps going if the dialog closes; the toast reports the result.
    if (!next && !running) resetTimer.current = setTimeout(reset, 200)
    onOpenChange(next)
  }

  async function start(checkHash?: Hex) {
    if (!request) return
    const current = request
    const id = ++run.current
    const last: { failure: TxFailure | null } = { failure: null }
    const report = (event: DeployEvent) => {
      if (id !== run.current) return
      if (event.type === 'failed') last.failure = event.failure
      dispatch(event)
    }
    setSwitching(!sample && !checkHash && account.chainId !== chainId)
    const call = current.call
    let result: T | null = null
    try {
      result = await runManage<T>(
        {
          call,
          // Sample actions stand in for the wallet: the sign-in wallet on the collection's chain.
          account: sample ? wallet : account.address,
          wallet,
          chainId,
          connectedChainId: sample ? chainId : account.chainId,
          sentHash: checkHash,
          addId: call?.functionName === 'registerMintGroup' ? Number(call.args[0]) : undefined,
        },
        {
          switchChain: (target) => actions.switchChain(target),
          simulate: (next) => {
            if (next) return actions.simulate(next)
            if (actions.review) return actions.review(current.review)
            return Promise.reject(new Error('This change runs on sample data only.'))
          },
          wait: (hash) => actions.wait(hash),
          confirm: (hash) => current.confirm(hash),
          remember: current.pending ? (hash) => current.pending?.remember(hash) : undefined,
          forget: current.pending ? () => current.pending?.forget() : undefined,
          sleep: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
          now: () => Date.now(),
        },
        report,
      )
    } catch (error) {
      // The machine words every failure it expects; one it did not must not leave the dialog on a spinner.
      report({ type: 'failed', failure: classifyError(error, 'confirming', 'manage') })
    }
    if (id !== run.current) return
    if (result !== null) {
      onConfirmed?.(result)
      toast.success(successMessage)
      return
    }
    if (last.failure) onFailed?.(last.failure.message)
  }

  function submit(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (request && phase.step === 'idle') void start()
  }

  const steps: StepView[] = [
    ...(switching
      ? [
          {
            step: 'switching' as const,
            label: `Switch to ${chain.name}`,
            detail: 'Approve the network switch in your wallet.',
          },
        ]
      : []),
    {
      step: 'simulating',
      label: 'Checking the change',
      detail: 'A dry run against the contract, so the wallet only opens for a change that will go through.',
    },
    {
      step: 'wallet',
      label: 'Confirm in your wallet',
      detail: sample
        ? 'Answer for your wallet below.'
        : 'Your wallet shows a call to the collection contract and the gas for it.',
    },
    {
      step: 'confirming',
      label: `Confirming on ${chain.name}`,
      detail: 'Sent. Waiting for the transaction to be included in a block.',
    },
    {
      step: 'verifying',
      label: 'Reading the contract',
      detail:
        'Cobweb checks the receipt and reads the contract again, so the screen shows what the chain holds.',
    },
  ]
  const failedAt = phase.step === 'failed' ? phase.at : null

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={handleOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className={cn('sm:max-w-lg', className)}>
        <form onSubmit={submit} className="grid gap-5">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>{title}</DialogTitle>
            {description && <DialogDescription>{description}</DialogDescription>}
          </DialogHeader>

          {phase.step === 'idle' ? (
            <>
              {children}
              {request && <CallReview request={request.review} chain={chain} />}
            </>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-3">
              <TxProgress
                phase={phase}
                steps={steps}
                txUrl={chain.explorerUrl ? (hash) => explorerTxUrl(chain, hash) : undefined}
              />
              {sample && phase.step === 'wallet' && (
                <SimulatedWallet onRespond={(choice) => actions.answer?.(choice)} />
              )}
              {phase.step === 'success' && (
                <p className="bg-success-soft flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm">
                  <CheckIcon aria-hidden="true" className="text-success-foreground size-4 shrink-0" />
                  {successMessage}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <DialogFooter>
            {phase.step === 'idle' && (
              <>
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => handleOpenChange(false)}>
                  Cancel
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" variant={destructive ? 'destructive' : 'default'} disabled={!request}>
                  {actionLabel}
                </Button>
              </>
            )}
            {running && (
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => handleOpenChange(false)}>
                {phase.step === 'confirming' || phase.step === 'verifying'
                  ? 'Close and keep waiting'
                  : 'Close'}
              </Button>
            )}
            {phase.step === 'failed' && (
              <>
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={reset}>
                  {failedAt === 'simulating' ? 'Back to edit' : 'Back'}
                </Button>
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant={destructive ? 'destructive' : 'default'}
                  disabled={!request}
                  onClick={() => void start(sentHash)}
                >
                  {sentHash ? 'Check again' : 'Try again'}
                </Button>
              </>
            )}
            {phase.step === 'success' && (
              <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={() => handleOpenChange(false)}>
                Close
              </Button>
            )}
          </DialogFooter>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

/** The call as it will be sent: contract, function, arguments, value. */
export function CallReview({ request, chain }: { request: TxRequest; chain: SupportedChain }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-well shadow-inset rounded-lg border px-4 py-3">
      <p className="text-muted-foreground mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-1 text-xs font-medium">
        Sends to the {request.contract.toLowerCase()} on
        <ChainName chain={chain} className="text-foreground" iconClassName="size-3.5" />
      </p>
      <p className="numeric text-sm break-all">{request.functionName}</p>
      <dl className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-[0.8125rem]">
        {request.args.map((arg) => (
          <div key={arg.name} className="contents">
            <dt className="text-muted-foreground">{arg.name}</dt>
            <dd className="numeric min-w-0 text-right break-all">{arg.value}</dd>
          </div>
        ))}
        {request.valueWei && (
          <div className="contents">
            <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Value</dt>
            <dd className="numeric text-right">{formatNative(request.valueWei, chain.nativeSymbol)}</dd>
          </div>
        )}
      </dl>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Stands in for the wallet's own confirmation window on sample actions. */
function SimulatedWallet({
  onRespond,
}: {
  onRespond: (answer: 'approve' | 'reject' | 'approve-revert') => void
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-dashed px-4 py-3">
      <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
        <WalletIcon aria-hidden="true" className="text-muted-foreground size-4" />
        Simulated wallet
      </p>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1 text-[0.8125rem]">
        Your wallet would open here. Choose how it answers.
      </p>
      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="secondary" autoFocus onClick={() => onRespond('approve')}>
          Approve
        </Button>
        <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => onRespond('reject')}>
          Reject
        </Button>
        <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onRespond('approve-revert')}>
          Approve, then revert
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```
