- [ ] **Step 1: The row and the dialogs**

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/components/manage/phase-row.tsx` with:

```tsx
'use client'

import { EyeOffIcon, MoreHorizontalIcon, PencilIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { Badge } from '@cobweb/ui/components/badge'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@cobweb/ui/components/dropdown-menu'
import { Progress } from '@cobweb/ui/components/progress'
import { StatusBadge } from '@cobweb/ui/components/status-badge'
import { cn } from '@cobweb/ui/lib/utils'
import { NAME_FIRST } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { formatNative, type MintGroup, type SupportedChain } from '@/lib/demo'
import { count, percent, relativeTo, shortUtc } from './format'

export type PhaseAction =
  | 'open'
  | 'close'
  | 'schedule'
  | 'clear'
  | 'price'
  | 'supply'
  | 'cap'
  | 'details'
  | 'up'
  | 'down'
  | 'hide'
  | 'show'

/** Column template shared by the header and every row, once the list is 56rem wide. */
export const PHASE_COLUMNS =
  '@4xl:grid-cols-[minmax(8rem,1.2fr)_7rem_minmax(10rem,1fr)_5rem_minmax(9rem,0.9fr)_9rem]'

/** Why the on-chain controls are off while the contract cannot be read. */
export const UNREADABLE_REASON = 'The contract could not be read, so changes on chain wait until it can be.'

/**
 * One phase (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.4). Its state is the chain's `active` field and
 * nothing else: a scheduled start is shown as a time, and the badge says Scheduled until the chain reports
 * the phase live. A group the chain lists without a row reads "Group N", "Not named", until it is named.
 * While the contract cannot be read, status and minted counts are not shown and the on-chain controls are
 * off; naming, hiding and moving still work.
 */
export function PhaseRow({
  group,
  chain,
  now,
  publicCap,
  reachable,
  first,
  last,
  onAction,
}: {
  group: MintGroup
  chain: SupportedChain
  /** The chain's estimated time, for "Opens in" labels only. */
  now: number
  publicCap: number
  /** False while the view is the copy. */
  reachable: boolean
  /** Its place among the named phases, for Move up and Move down. */
  first: boolean
  last: boolean
  onAction: (action: PhaseAction) => void
}) {
  const named = group.named !== false
  const full = reachable && group.minted >= group.maxSupply
  const status = group.active ? 'live' : group.scheduledStart > 0 ? 'scheduled' : 'closed'
  const offChain = reachable ? undefined : UNREADABLE_REASON
  return (
    <li
      className={cn(
        'relative grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-4 px-5 py-5 @xl:grid-cols-4 @4xl:items-center @4xl:gap-y-0 @4xl:px-6',
        PHASE_COLUMNS,
        group.active && 'bg-primary-soft/40',
      )}
    >
      {group.active && <span aria-hidden="true" className="bg-primary absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-0.5" />}

      <div className="col-span-2 flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3 @xl:col-span-4 @4xl:col-span-1">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-heading flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="truncate">{group.name}</span>
            {!named && (
              <Badge variant="outline" className="h-5 shrink-0">
                Not named
              </Badge>
            )}
            {group.visible === false && (
              <Badge variant="secondary" className="h-5 shrink-0">
                <EyeOffIcon aria-hidden="true" />
                Hidden
              </Badge>
            )}
          </p>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-2 text-[0.8125rem]">
            {group.kind === 'public' ? 'Public' : 'Whitelist'}
            <span aria-hidden="true" className="bg-faint size-0.5 rounded-full" />
            <span className="numeric">Group {group.id}</span>
          </p>
          {group.description && (
            <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-[0.8125rem]" title={group.description}>
              {group.description}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2 @4xl:hidden">
          {reachable ? <StatusBadge status={status} /> : <StatusBadge status="draft">Not read</StatusBadge>}
        </div>
      </div>

      <Cell label="Price">
        <span className="numeric">{formatNative(group.priceWei, chain.nativeSymbol)}</span>
      </Cell>

      <Cell label="Minted of allocation">
        <div className="grid gap-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <p className="numeric text-sm">
              {reachable ? (
                <span className={cn(group.active && 'text-primary')}>{count(group.minted)}</span>
              ) : (
                <span className="text-muted-foreground font-sans">Not read</span>
              )}
              <span className="text-muted-foreground"> / {count(group.maxSupply)}</span>
            </p>
            {full && (
              <Badge variant="success" className="h-5">
                Full
              </Badge>
            )}
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="xs"
              className="text-muted-foreground -my-1 ml-auto"
              onClick={() => onAction('supply')}
              disabled={!reachable}
              title={offChain}
              aria-label={`Edit supply for ${group.name}`}
            >
              <PencilIcon aria-hidden="true" />
              Edit
            </Button>
          </div>
          {reachable && (
            <Progress
              size="sm"
              value={percent(group.minted, group.maxSupply)}
              aria-label={`${group.name}: ${count(group.minted)} of ${count(group.maxSupply)} minted`}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </Cell>

      <Cell label="Per wallet">
        {group.kind === 'public' ? (
          <span className="numeric">{publicCap}</span>
        ) : (
          <span className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">Quota</span>
        )}
      </Cell>

      <Cell label="Status" className="col-span-2 @xl:col-span-1">
        <div className="grid gap-1">
          {reachable ? (
            <StatusBadge status={status} className="hidden @4xl:inline-flex" />
          ) : (
            <StatusBadge status="draft" className="hidden @4xl:inline-flex">
              Not read
            </StatusBadge>
          )}
          {group.scheduledStart > 0 ? (
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">
              <span className="numeric text-foreground">{shortUtc(group.scheduledStart)}</span>
              <br />
              {group.scheduledStart > now ? 'Opens' : 'Start was'} {relativeTo(group.scheduledStart, now)}
            </p>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">
              {reachable && group.active ? 'Opened by hand' : 'No start set'}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </Cell>

      <div className="col-span-2 flex items-center justify-end gap-2 @xl:col-span-4 @4xl:col-span-1">
        {group.active ? (
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => onAction('close')}
            disabled={!reachable}
            title={offChain}
          >
            Close
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => onAction('open')}
            disabled={!reachable}
            title={offChain}
          >
            Open now
          </Button>
        )}
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label={`More actions for ${group.name}`}>
              <MoreHorizontalIcon />
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-56">
            <DropdownMenuItem onSelect={() => onAction('details')}>
              {named ? 'Edit details' : 'Name this phase'}
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!named || first} onSelect={() => onAction('up')}>
              Move up
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!named || last} onSelect={() => onAction('down')}>
              Move down
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            {group.kind !== 'public' && (
              <DropdownMenuItem
                disabled={!named}
                onSelect={() => onAction(group.visible === false ? 'show' : 'hide')}
              >
                {group.visible === false ? 'Show on the mint page' : 'Hide from the mint page'}
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            )}
            {!named && <p className="text-muted-foreground px-2 py-1.5 text-xs">{NAME_FIRST}</p>}
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!reachable || group.active} onSelect={() => onAction('schedule')}>
              {group.scheduledStart > 0 ? 'Change scheduled start' : 'Schedule start'}
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            {group.scheduledStart > 0 && !group.active && (
              <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!reachable} onSelect={() => onAction('clear')}>
                Clear schedule
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            )}
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!reachable} onSelect={() => onAction('price')}>
              Edit price
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!reachable} onSelect={() => onAction('supply')}>
              Edit max supply
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            {group.kind === 'public' && (
              <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!reachable} onSelect={() => onAction('cap')}>
                Edit per-wallet limit
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            )}
            {!reachable && <p className="text-muted-foreground px-2 py-1.5 text-xs">{UNREADABLE_REASON}</p>}
            {reachable && group.active && (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground px-2 py-1.5 text-xs">Close the phase to schedule it.</p>
            )}
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </div>
    </li>
  )
}

function Cell({
  label,
  children,
  className,
}: {
  label: string
  children: React.ReactNode
  className?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className={cn('min-w-0 text-sm', className)}>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground mb-1 text-xs font-medium @4xl:sr-only">{label}</p>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}
```

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/components/manage/phase-dialogs.tsx` with:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useId, useState } from 'react'
import { Field, FieldDescription, FieldError, FieldLabel } from '@cobweb/ui/components/field'
import { Input } from '@cobweb/ui/components/input'
import { formatNative, toWei, type MintGroup, type SupportedChain } from '@/lib/demo'
import { phaseCalls, reviewOf, type ManageCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import type { PhasesView } from '@/lib/manage/phases-types'
import { count, shortUtc } from './format'
import type { ManageRequest } from './manage-actions'
import { allocationCheck, priceError, publicCapError } from './phase-rules'
import type { PhasesServer } from './phases-server'
import { PriceInput } from './price-input'
import { TransactionDialog } from './transaction-dialog'

/** What every phase dialog needs from the screen. */
export type PhaseContext = {
  chain: SupportedChain
  groups: MintGroup[]
  /** The collection's max supply: allocations together may not exceed it. */
  maxSupply: number
  /** Tokens airdropped (and burned): collection supply no phase can sell. */
  airdropped: number
  /** The chain's time at the read; the dialogs' clocks start from it. Never liveness. */
  blockTimestamp: number
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  /** Phases' server: every confirm answers the fresh view. */
  server: PhasesServer
}

/**
 * A change as a Phases dialog sends it (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.4): the call built with
 * `phaseCalls`, the review from `reviewOf`, and the server's confirm, which answers the fresh view.
 */
export function phaseRequest(
  call: ManageCall | null,
  ctx: Pick<PhaseContext, 'chain' | 'server'>,
): ManageRequest<PhasesView> | null {
  if (!call) return null
  return {
    call,
    review: { contract: 'Collection', functionName: call.functionName, args: reviewOf(call, ctx.chain) },
    confirm: (hash) => ctx.server.confirm(hash, call),
  }
}

/** Open now or Close. Closing also clears the schedule, as the contract does. */
export function StatusDialog({
  group,
  nextStatus,
  onView,
  ...ctx
}: PhaseContext & { group: MintGroup; nextStatus: boolean; onView: (view: PhasesView) => void }) {
  const others = ctx.groups.filter((other) => other.id !== group.id && other.active)
  return (
    <TransactionDialog
      open={ctx.open}
      onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
      chain={ctx.chain}
      title={nextStatus ? `Open ${group.name}` : `Close ${group.name}`}
      description={
        nextStatus
          ? 'Minting starts as soon as the transaction confirms, and the phase stays open until you close it.'
          : 'Minting in this phase stops as soon as the transaction confirms.'
      }
      actionLabel={nextStatus ? 'Open phase' : 'Close phase'}
      destructive={!nextStatus}
      successMessage={nextStatus ? `${group.name} is open` : `${group.name} is closed`}
      request={phaseRequest(nextStatus ? phaseCalls.open(group.id) : phaseCalls.close(group.id), ctx)}
      onConfirmed={onView}
    >
      <ul className="text-muted-foreground grid list-disc gap-1.5 pl-5 text-sm">
        {!nextStatus && group.scheduledStart > 0 && (
          <li>
            Closing also clears the scheduled start (
            <span className="numeric">{shortUtc(group.scheduledStart)}</span>). To run it again, open it by
            hand or schedule a new start.
          </li>
        )}
        {!nextStatus && group.scheduledStart === 0 && <li>You can open it again at any time.</li>}
        {nextStatus && others.length > 0 && (
          <li>
            {others.map((other) => other.name).join(' and ')} {others.length === 1 ? 'is' : 'are'} also open.{' '}
            {others.length === 1 ? 'Both phases' : 'All of them'} will be live at once; close one if that is
            not what you want.
          </li>
        )}
        {nextStatus && group.scheduledStart > 0 && (
          <li>
            It is scheduled for <span className="numeric">{shortUtc(group.scheduledStart)}</span>. Opening now
            starts it early.
          </li>
        )}
        {nextStatus && group.minted >= group.maxSupply && (
          <li>
            Its allocation is full, so it will be open but nobody can mint until you raise the max supply.
          </li>
        )}
      </ul>
    </TransactionDialog>
  )
}

/** The emergency stop: closes every phase in one transaction, clearing every schedule. */
export function CloseAllDialog({ onView, ...ctx }: PhaseContext & { onView: (view: PhasesView) => void }) {
  const scheduled = ctx.groups.filter((group) => group.scheduledStart > 0)
  return (
    <TransactionDialog
      open={ctx.open}
      onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
      chain={ctx.chain}
      destructive
      title="Close every phase"
      description="Minting stops in every phase as soon as the transaction confirms. Use it to pause the whole drop."
      actionLabel="Close all phases"
      successMessage="Every phase is closed"
      request={phaseRequest(phaseCalls.closeAll(), ctx)}
      onConfirmed={onView}
    >
      {scheduled.length > 0 && (
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
          This also clears the scheduled start of {scheduled.map((group) => group.name).join(' and ')}.
          Schedule
          {scheduled.length === 1 ? ' it' : ' them'} again when you are ready.
        </p>
      )}
    </TransactionDialog>
  )
}

/** Removes a scheduled start without opening or closing the phase. */
export function ClearScheduleDialog({
  group,
  onView,
  ...ctx
}: PhaseContext & { group: MintGroup; onView: (view: PhasesView) => void }) {
  return (
    <TransactionDialog
      open={ctx.open}
      onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
      chain={ctx.chain}
      title="Clear the scheduled start"
      description={`${group.name} will no longer open by itself at ${shortUtc(group.scheduledStart)}. It stays closed until you open it or schedule it again.`}
      actionLabel="Clear schedule"
      successMessage={`${group.name} is no longer scheduled`}
      request={phaseRequest(phaseCalls.clearSchedule(group.id), ctx)}
      onConfirmed={onView}
    />
  )
}

/** Change the price per token, entered in the native token. */
export function PriceDialog({
  group,
  onView,
  ...ctx
}: PhaseContext & { group: MintGroup; onView: (view: PhasesView) => void }) {
  const id = useId()
  const [value, setValue] = useState(formatNative(group.priceWei, '', 18).trim().replace(/,/g, ''))
  const error = priceError(value, ctx.chain.nativeSymbol)
  const wei = error ? null : toWei(value.trim())
  const unchanged = wei === group.priceWei
  return (
    <TransactionDialog
      open={ctx.open}
      onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
      chain={ctx.chain}
      title={`Price for ${group.name}`}
      description="Applies to every mint after the transaction confirms. Platform fees are added on top for the minter."
      actionLabel="Change price"
      successMessage={`${group.name} price changed`}
      request={phaseRequest(wei && !unchanged ? phaseCalls.price(group.id, wei) : null, ctx)}
      onConfirmed={onView}
    >
      <PriceInput
        id={`${id}-price`}
        value={value}
        onChange={setValue}
        chain={ctx.chain}
        error={error}
        hint={<>Now {formatNative(group.priceWei, ctx.chain.nativeSymbol, 18)}. Enter 0 for a free mint.</>}
      />
    </TransactionDialog>
  )
}

/**
 * Change the allocation: never below what the phase has minted, never past the collection's supply.
 * Both are checked here before the simulation, which checks them again on the contract.
 */
export function SupplyDialog({
  group,
  onView,
  ...ctx
}: PhaseContext & { group: MintGroup; onView: (view: PhasesView) => void }) {
  const id = useId()
  const [value, setValue] = useState(String(group.maxSupply))
  const { error, warning, room, next } = allocationCheck({
    groups: ctx.groups,
    maxSupply: ctx.maxSupply,
    groupId: group.id,
    value,
    airdropped: ctx.airdropped,
  })
  return (
    <TransactionDialog
      open={ctx.open}
      onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
      chain={ctx.chain}
      title={`Max supply for ${group.name}`}
      description="How many tokens this phase may mint in total. Unsold tokens do not move to another phase by themselves; lower one phase and raise another."
      actionLabel="Change max supply"
      successMessage={`${group.name} max supply changed`}
      request={phaseRequest(
        error || next === group.maxSupply ? null : phaseCalls.allocation(group.id, next),
        ctx,
      )}
      onConfirmed={onView}
    >
      <Field data-invalid={!!error}>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-supply`}>Max supply</FieldLabel>
        <Input
          id={`${id}-supply`}
          type="number"
          inputMode="numeric"
          min={group.minted}
          max={room}
          value={value}
          onChange={(event) => setValue(event.target.value)}
          aria-invalid={!!error}
          aria-describedby={`${id}-supply-help`}
        />
        {error ? (
          <FieldError id={`${id}-supply-help`}>{error}</FieldError>
        ) : (
          <FieldDescription id={`${id}-supply-help`}>
            Between <span className="numeric">{count(group.minted)}</span> (already minted) and{' '}
            <span className="numeric">{count(room)}</span> (room left in the collection).
          </FieldDescription>
        )}
        {warning && <p className="text-warning-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">{warning}</p>}
      </Field>
    </TransactionDialog>
  )
}

/** The public phase's lifetime cap per wallet. */
export function PublicCapDialog({
  cap,
  onView,
  ...ctx
}: PhaseContext & { cap: number; onView: (view: PhasesView) => void }) {
  const id = useId()
  const [value, setValue] = useState(String(cap))
  const next = Number(value)
  const error = publicCapError(value, ctx.maxSupply)
  const publicAllocation = ctx.groups.find((group) => group.kind === 'public')?.maxSupply
  // Legacy refused a limit above the public allocation; the contract allows it, so it is a note.
  const above = !error && publicAllocation !== undefined && next > publicAllocation
  return (
    <TransactionDialog
      open={ctx.open}
      onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
      chain={ctx.chain}
      title="Public mints per wallet"
      description="The most one wallet can mint in the public phase, over the whole sale. Whitelist phases use each address's quota instead."
      actionLabel="Change limit"
      successMessage="Public limit changed"
      request={phaseRequest(error || next === cap ? null : phaseCalls.publicCap(next), ctx)}
      onConfirmed={onView}
    >
      <Field data-invalid={!!error}>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-cap`}>Per wallet</FieldLabel>
        <Input
          id={`${id}-cap`}
          type="number"
          inputMode="numeric"
          min={1}
          value={value}
          onChange={(event) => setValue(event.target.value)}
          aria-invalid={!!error}
          aria-describedby={`${id}-cap-help`}
        />
        {error ? (
          <FieldError id={`${id}-cap-help`}>{error}</FieldError>
        ) : (
          <FieldDescription id={`${id}-cap-help`}>
            Wallets that already minted more keep their tokens; they just cannot mint again.
          </FieldDescription>
        )}
        {above && (
          <p className="text-warning-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">
            More than the public phase&apos;s {count(publicAllocation ?? 0)} tokens, so one wallet could take
            the whole phase.
          </p>
        )}
      </Field>
    </TransactionDialog>
  )
}
```

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/components/manage/phase-details.tsx` with:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useId, useState } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@cobweb/ui/components/dialog'
import { Field, FieldDescription, FieldError, FieldLabel } from '@cobweb/ui/components/field'
import { Input } from '@cobweb/ui/components/input'
import { Spinner } from '@cobweb/ui/components/spinner'
import { Switch } from '@cobweb/ui/components/switch'
import { Textarea } from '@cobweb/ui/components/textarea'
import { THROWN, type ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { MintGroup } from '@/lib/demo'
import { PHASE_DESCRIPTION_MAX, PHASE_NAME_MAX, phaseNameError } from './phase-rules'
import type { PhasesServer } from './phases-server'

/** What Cobweb stores about a phase, apart from the chain: shown on the mint page only. */
export type PhaseDetails = { name: string; description: string; visible: boolean }

/**
 * Name, description and, for Add phase, the mint page switch, as legacy's mint group form had them. Edit
 * details leaves the switch out: a phase is hidden or shown from its menu (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md,
 * section 7.4).
 */
export function PhaseDetailsFields({
  value,
  onChange,
  nameError,
  showVisibility = true,
}: {
  value: PhaseDetails
  onChange: (value: PhaseDetails) => void
  nameError: string | null
  showVisibility?: boolean
}) {
  const id = useId()
  const tooLong = value.description.length > PHASE_DESCRIPTION_MAX
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-5">
      <Field data-invalid={!!nameError}>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-name`}>Name</FieldLabel>
        <Input
          id={`${id}-name`}
          value={value.name}
          onChange={(event) => onChange({ ...value, name: event.target.value })}
          maxLength={PHASE_NAME_MAX}
          autoComplete="off"
          aria-invalid={!!nameError}
        />
        {nameError ? (
          <FieldError>{nameError}</FieldError>
        ) : (
          <FieldDescription>Shown on the mint page. Stored by Cobweb, not on chain.</FieldDescription>
        )}
      </Field>
      <Field data-invalid={tooLong}>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-description`}>Description</FieldLabel>
        <Textarea
          id={`${id}-description`}
          value={value.description}
          onChange={(event) => onChange({ ...value, description: event.target.value })}
          rows={3}
          placeholder="Who can mint in this phase, and anything they should know."
          aria-invalid={tooLong}
        />
        <FieldDescription className={tooLong ? 'text-destructive-foreground' : undefined}>
          <span className="numeric">{value.description.length.toLocaleString('en-US')}</span> of{' '}
          {PHASE_DESCRIPTION_MAX} characters. Optional.
        </FieldDescription>
      </Field>
      {showVisibility && (
        <Field orientation="horizontal">
          <Switch
            id={`${id}-visible`}
            checked={value.visible}
            onCheckedChange={(visible) => onChange({ ...value, visible })}
          />
          <div className="grid gap-0.5">
            <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-visible`}>Show on the mint page</FieldLabel>
            <FieldDescription>
              {value.visible
                ? 'Visitors see this phase, its price and whether they are on its list.'
                : 'Hidden from visitors. It still mints while open, for wallets that know to use it.'}
            </FieldDescription>
          </div>
        </Field>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

/**
 * Edit a phase's name and description, or name a group registered outside Cobweb. No transaction: Cobweb
 * stores these, through `renamePhase`, which checks the session and ownership.
 */
export function PhaseDetailsDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  group,
  others,
  server,
  onSaved,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  group: MintGroup
  /** The other phases' names, which this one may not repeat. */
  others: string[]
  server: PhasesServer
  onSaved: (details: { name: string; description: string }) => void
}) {
  const named = group.named !== false
  // A group without a row reads "Group N" until named: the field starts empty.
  const [value, setValue] = useState<PhaseDetails>({
    name: named ? group.name : '',
    description: group.description ?? '',
    visible: group.visible !== false,
  })
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const nameError = phaseNameError(value.name, others)
  const invalid = !!nameError || value.description.length > PHASE_DESCRIPTION_MAX
  const unchanged =
    named && value.name.trim() === group.name && value.description.trim() === (group.description ?? '')

  async function save(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (invalid || unchanged) return
    setSaving(true)
    const details = { name: value.name.trim(), description: value.description.trim() }
    let result: ActionResult
    try {
      result = await server.rename(group.id, details)
    } catch {
      result = { ok: false, error: THROWN }
    }
    setSaving(false)
    if (!result.ok) {
      toast.error(result.error)
      return
    }
    onSaved(details)
    toast.success(`${details.name} saved`)
    onOpenChange(false)
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={(next) => !saving && onOpenChange(next)}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
        <form onSubmit={save} className="grid gap-5">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>{named ? `Edit ${group.name}` : `Name group ${group.id}`}</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              {named
                ? "How the phase appears on the mint page. Price, supply and start are changed on chain from the phase's menu."
                : 'This group was registered on the contract outside Cobweb. Name it to show it on the mint page and to add its whitelist.'}
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <PhaseDetailsFields
            value={value}
            onChange={setValue}
            nameError={nameError}
            showVisibility={false}
          />
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)} disabled={saving}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button type="submit" disabled={invalid || unchanged || saving}>
              {saving && <Spinner />}
              Save
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}
```

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/components/manage/phase-schedule.tsx` with:

```tsx
'use client'

import { AlertTriangleIcon, CalendarIcon, ExternalLinkIcon, InfoIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useId, useReducer, useState } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@cobweb/ui/components/alert'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import { Calendar } from '@cobweb/ui/components/calendar'
import { Field, FieldDescription, FieldError, FieldLabel } from '@cobweb/ui/components/field'
import { Input } from '@cobweb/ui/components/input'
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from '@cobweb/ui/components/popover'
import { Switch } from '@cobweb/ui/components/switch'
import { deployReducer, type DeployEvent } from '@/components/deploy/deploy-machine'
import { classifyError } from '@/components/deploy/tx-errors'
import { isTxRunning, TX_IDLE, TxProgress, type TxFailure } from '@/components/deploy/tx-progress'
import { useChainClock } from '@/components/mint/use-chain-clock'
import {
  explorerTxUrl,
  formatUtc,
  shortAddress,
  toWei,
  type MintGroup,
  type SupportedChain,
} from '@/lib/demo'
import { phaseCalls } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import type { PhaseAdd, PhasesView } from '@/lib/manage/phases-types'
import { count, relativeTo, useTimeZone } from './format'
import { useManageActions } from './manage-context'
import { runManage, type PendingAdd } from './manage-machine'
import { phaseRequest, type PhaseContext } from './phase-dialogs'
import { PhaseDetailsFields, type PhaseDetails } from './phase-details'
import { allocationCheck, phaseNameError, priceError } from './phase-rules'
import type { PhasesServer } from './phases-server'
import { PriceInput } from './price-input'
import { defaultStart, unixToZoned, zonedToUnix, type ZonedStart } from './schedule'
import { scheduleWarning } from './schedule-logic'
import { TransactionDialog } from './transaction-dialog'

/** `YYYY-MM-DD` to a Date for the calendar (a calendar day, not an instant). */
const toCalendarDay = (date: string) => {
  const [year, month, day] = date.split('-').map(Number)
  return year && month && day ? new Date(year, month - 1, day) : undefined
}
const fromCalendarDay = (date: Date) =>
  `${date.getFullYear()}-${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`

/**
 * Date and time in the creator's time zone, with what that means in UTC and the exact value
 * the transaction sends. Warns when the time has already passed on chain, or does not exist in the
 * zone (skipped by a daylight-saving change). `now` is the chain's estimated time (`useChainClock`):
 * it moves the labels, the calendar's first day and the warning, never liveness.
 */
function StartPicker({
  value,
  onChange,
  now,
  timeZone,
}: {
  value: ZonedStart
  onChange: (start: ZonedStart) => void
  now: number
  timeZone: string
}) {
  const id = useId()
  const [calendarOpen, setCalendarOpen] = useState(false)
  const unix = zonedToUnix(value, timeZone)
  const warning = scheduleWarning(unix, now)
  const day = toCalendarDay(value.date)
  const chainDay = toCalendarDay(unixToZoned(now, timeZone).date)
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4">
      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_9rem]">
        <Field>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-date`}>Date</FieldLabel>
          <Popover open={calendarOpen} onOpenChange={setCalendarOpen}>
            <PopoverTrigger asChild>
              <Button id={`${id}-date`} type="button" variant="outline" className="justify-start font-normal">
                <CalendarIcon aria-hidden="true" />
                {day
                  ? day.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
                      weekday: 'short',
                      month: 'short',
                      day: 'numeric',
                      year: 'numeric',
                    })
                  : 'Pick a date'}
              </Button>
            </PopoverTrigger>
            <PopoverContent className="w-auto p-0" align="start">
              <Calendar
                mode="single"
                selected={day}
                defaultMonth={day}
                disabled={chainDay ? { before: chainDay } : undefined}
                onSelect={(date) => {
                  if (date) onChange({ ...value, date: fromCalendarDay(date) })
                  setCalendarOpen(false)
                }}
              />
            </PopoverContent>
          </Popover>
        </Field>
        <Field>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-time`}>Time</FieldLabel>
          <Input
            id={`${id}-time`}
            type="time"
            value={value.time}
            onChange={(event) => onChange({ ...value, time: event.target.value })}
            className="numeric"
          />
        </Field>
      </div>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground -mt-2 text-[0.8125rem]">
        In your time zone, <span className="text-foreground">{timeZone}</span>.
      </p>

      {unix !== null ? (
        <dl className="bg-well shadow-inset grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-[0.8125rem]">
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">UTC</dt>
          <dd className="numeric text-right">{formatUtc(unix)}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">Unix time sent</dt>
          <dd className="numeric text-right">{unix}</dd>
          <dt className="text-muted-foreground">From the chain&apos;s time</dt>
          <dd className="numeric text-right">{relativeTo(unix, now)}</dd>
        </dl>
      ) : (
        value.date &&
        value.time && (
          <FieldError>
            {value.time} does not exist on that day in {timeZone}: the clocks skip it. Pick another time.
          </FieldError>
        )
      )}
      {warning && (
        <p role="alert" className="bg-warning-soft flex gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm">
          <AlertTriangleIcon aria-hidden="true" className="text-warning-foreground mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" />
          {warning}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

/** Schedule a start: the phase opens by itself at that second and stays open until closed. */
export function ScheduleDialog({
  group,
  onView,
  ...ctx
}: PhaseContext & { group: MintGroup; onView: (view: PhasesView) => void }) {
  const timeZone = useTimeZone()
  const now = useChainClock(ctx.blockTimestamp)
  const [start, setStart] = useState<ZonedStart>(() =>
    group.scheduledStart > 0
      ? unixToZoned(group.scheduledStart, timeZone)
      : defaultStart(ctx.blockTimestamp, timeZone),
  )
  const unix = zonedToUnix(start, timeZone)
  return (
    <TransactionDialog
      open={ctx.open}
      onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
      chain={ctx.chain}
      title={`Schedule ${group.name}`}
      description="The phase opens by itself at this time and stays open until you close it. There is no end time."
      actionLabel="Schedule start"
      successMessage={`${group.name} is scheduled`}
      request={phaseRequest(unix === null ? null : phaseCalls.schedule(group.id, unix), ctx)}
      onConfirmed={onView}
    >
      <StartPicker value={start} onChange={setStart} now={now} timeZone={timeZone} />
    </TransactionDialog>
  )
}

/**
 * Register a new whitelist phase with its price, allocation and an optional start, in one
 * `registerMintGroup` call (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.4). The id is the next free one
 * the contract reports. Name, description and visibility are recorded by Cobweb only after the receipt
 * proves the registration; until then the browser keeps the sent hash and the form (`pending`).
 */
export function AddPhaseDialog({
  onView,
  onRemember,
  onForget,
  ...ctx
}: PhaseContext & {
  onView: (view: PhasesView) => void
  onRemember: (pending: PendingAdd) => void
  onForget: () => void
}) {
  const id = useId()
  const timeZone = useTimeZone()
  const now = useChainClock(ctx.blockTimestamp)
  const { actions } = useManageActions()
  const [nextId, setNextId] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [idUnread, setIdUnread] = useState(false)
  const [details, setDetails] = useState<PhaseDetails>({ name: '', description: '', visible: true })
  const [price, setPrice] = useState('0')
  const [supply, setSupply] = useState(() =>
    String(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ctx.maxSupply - ctx.groups.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.maxSupply, 0)))),
  )
  const [scheduled, setScheduled] = useState(false)
  const [start, setStart] = useState<ZonedStart>(() => defaultStart(ctx.blockTimestamp, timeZone))

  // The next free id, from the contract's own list of groups (the highest plus one).
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true
    actions.nextGroupId().then(
      (next) => {
        if (live) setNextId(next)
      },
      () => {
        if (live) setIdUnread(true)
      },
    )
    return () => {
      live = false
    }
  }, [actions])

  const nameError = phaseNameError(
    details.name,
    ctx.groups.map((group) => group.name),
  )
  const priceProblem = priceError(price, ctx.chain.nativeSymbol)
  const allocation = allocationCheck({
    groups: ctx.groups,
    maxSupply: ctx.maxSupply,
    groupId: null,
    value: supply,
    airdropped: ctx.airdropped,
    min: 1,
  })
  const unix = scheduled ? zonedToUnix(start, timeZone) : 0
  const ready = !nameError && !priceProblem && !allocation.error && unix !== null && nextId !== null
  const add: PhaseAdd | null =
    nextId === null
      ? null
      : {
          onchainId: nextId,
          name: details.name.trim(),
          description: details.description.trim(),
          visible: details.visible,
        }
  const request =
    ready && add && unix !== null
      ? phaseRequest(phaseCalls.register(add.onchainId, toWei(price.trim()), allocation.next, unix), ctx)
      : null

  return (
    <TransactionDialog
      open={ctx.open}
      onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
      chain={ctx.chain}
      title="Add a phase"
      description="A new whitelist phase. It starts closed unless you schedule it; add its addresses on the Whitelist tab."
      actionLabel="Add phase"
      successMessage={`${details.name.trim() || 'Phase'} added`}
      className="sm:max-w-xl"
      request={
        request && add
          ? {
              ...request,
              confirm: (hash) => ctx.server.record(hash, add),
              pending: {
                remember: (hash) => onRemember({ hash, form: add }),
                forget: onForget,
              },
            }
          : null
      }
      onConfirmed={onView}
    >
      <div className="grid gap-5">
        <PhaseDetailsFields
          value={details}
          onChange={setDetails}
          nameError={details.name ? nameError : null}
        />
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <PriceInput
            id={`${id}-price`}
            value={price}
            onChange={setPrice}
            chain={ctx.chain}
            error={priceProblem}
          />
          <Field data-invalid={!!allocation.error}>
            <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-supply`}>Max supply</FieldLabel>
            <Input
              id={`${id}-supply`}
              type="number"
              min={1}
              max={allocation.room}
              value={supply}
              onChange={(event) => setSupply(event.target.value)}
              aria-invalid={!!allocation.error}
            />
            {allocation.error ? (
              <FieldError>{allocation.error}</FieldError>
            ) : (
              <FieldDescription className={allocation.warning ? 'text-warning-foreground' : undefined}>
                {allocation.warning ?? (
                  <>
                    <span className="numeric">{count(allocation.room)}</span> not allocated yet.
                  </>
                )}
              </FieldDescription>
            )}
          </Field>
        </div>
        <Field orientation="horizontal">
          <Switch id={`${id}-scheduled`} checked={scheduled} onCheckedChange={setScheduled} />
          <FieldLabel htmlFor={`${id}-scheduled`}>Schedule a start</FieldLabel>
        </Field>
        {scheduled && <StartPicker value={start} onChange={setStart} now={now} timeZone={timeZone} />}
        {idUnread ? (
          <p className="text-destructive-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">
            The contract could not be read for the next phase id. Close this and try again.
          </p>
        ) : (
          nextId === null && (
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">
              Reading the next phase id from the contract.
            </p>
          )
        )}
      </div>
    </TransactionDialog>
  )
}

/**
 * An add this browser sent that Cobweb has not recorded yet (section 7.4): its name, its transaction,
 * "Check again", which runs the manage machine from verifying with the remembered hash and form, and
 * "Discard", for a transaction that was replaced or will never land.
 */
export function PendingAddPanel({
  pending,
  chain,
  server,
  onView,
  onForget,
}: {
  pending: PendingAdd
  chain: SupportedChain
  server: PhasesServer
  onView: (view: PhasesView) => void
  onForget: () => void
}) {
  const { chainId, wallet } = useManageActions()
  const [phase, dispatch] = useReducer(deployReducer, TX_IDLE)
  const running = isTxRunning(phase)

  async function check() {
    const last: { failure: TxFailure | null } = { failure: null }
    const report = (event: DeployEvent) => {
      if (event.type === 'failed') last.failure = event.failure
      dispatch(event)
    }
    let view: PhasesView | null = null
    try {
      view = await runManage<PhasesView>(
        {
          call: null,
          account: wallet,
          wallet,
          chainId,
          connectedChainId: chainId,
          sentHash: pending.hash,
          addId: pending.form.onchainId,
        },
        {
          switchChain: async () => {},
          simulate: () => Promise.reject(new Error('A remembered add is checked, never sent again.')),
          wait: async () => 'success',
          confirm: (hash) => server.record(hash, pending.form),
          forget: onForget,
          sleep: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
          now: () => Date.now(),
        },
        report,
      )
    } catch (error) {
      report({ type: 'failed', failure: classifyError(error, 'confirming', 'manage') })
    }
    if (view) {
      onView(view)
      toast.success(`${pending.form.name} added`)
      return
    }
    // A terminal answer forgets the add, which closes this panel: its sentence goes to a toast.
    if (last.failure?.kind === 'refused') toast.error(last.failure.message)
  }

  return (
    <Alert>
      <InfoIcon aria-hidden="true" />
      <AlertTitle>A new phase is waiting to be recorded</AlertTitle>
      <AlertDescription>
        <p>
          This browser sent the registration of <span className="text-foreground">{pending.form.name}</span>{' '}
          as group <span className="numeric">{pending.form.onchainId}</span>. Cobweb records its name once the
          receipt shows it on the contract.
        </p>
        {chain.explorerUrl ? (
          <a
            href={explorerTxUrl(chain, pending.hash)}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[0.8125rem] underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
          >
            Transaction <span className="numeric">{shortAddress(pending.hash, 6)}</span>
            <ExternalLinkIcon aria-hidden="true" className="size-3.5" />
            <span className="sr-only">(opens the block explorer)</span>
          </a>
        ) : (
          <p className="numeric text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem] break-all">{pending.hash}</p>
        )}
        {phase.step !== 'idle' && (
          <TxProgress
            phase={phase}
            steps={[
              {
                step: 'verifying',
                label: 'Recording the phase',
                detail:
                  'Cobweb checks the receipt for the registration, records the name and reads the contract again.',
              },
            ]}
          />
        )}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => void check()} disabled={running}>
            Check again
          </Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={onForget} disabled={running}>
            Discard
          </Button>
        </div>
      </AlertDescription>
    </Alert>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The screen and the refusal panel**

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/components/manage/phases-screen.tsx` with:

```tsx
'use client'

import { InfoIcon, LayersIcon, PlusIcon, RefreshCwIcon, TriangleAlertIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import type { Address, Hex } from 'viem'
import { useConfig } from 'wagmi'
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@cobweb/ui/components/alert'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import {
  Empty,
  EmptyContent,
  EmptyDescription,
  EmptyHeader,
  EmptyMedia,
  EmptyTitle,
} from '@cobweb/ui/components/empty'
import { Panel } from '@cobweb/ui/components/panel'
import { Spinner } from '@cobweb/ui/components/spinner'
import { cn } from '@cobweb/ui/lib/utils'
import { browserStore } from '@/components/deploy/deploy-machine'
import { useChainClock } from '@/components/mint/use-chain-clock'
import { THROWN, type ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { chainView } from '@/lib/chain/chain-view'
import type { Tables } from '@/lib/database.types'
import { formatUtc, type MintGroup } from '@/lib/demo'
import type { ManageCall } from '@/lib/manage/phase-calls'
import type { PhasesView } from '@/lib/manage/phases-types'
import { moveGroup } from '@/lib/manage/phases-view'
import { relativeTo } from './format'
import { realManageActions } from './manage-actions'
import { ManageActionsProvider, type ManageTarget } from './manage-context'
import { forgetAdd, pendingAdd, rememberAdd, type PendingAdd } from './manage-machine'
import { sampleManageActions, samplePendingAdd, samplePhasesServer } from './manage-sim'
import {
  ClearScheduleDialog,
  CloseAllDialog,
  PriceDialog,
  PublicCapDialog,
  StatusDialog,
  SupplyDialog,
  type PhaseContext,
} from './phase-dialogs'
import { PhaseDetailsDialog } from './phase-details'
import { PHASE_COLUMNS, PhaseRow, UNREADABLE_REASON, type PhaseAction } from './phase-row'
import { AddPhaseDialog, PendingAddPanel, ScheduleDialog } from './phase-schedule'
import { phasesServer } from './phases-server'
import { SupplyMap } from './supply-map'

export type PhasesScreenProps = {
  collection: Tables<'collections'>
  /** The contract's groups joined with Cobweb's rows, read on the server (or the copy, or the sample). */
  view: PhasesView
  /** The sign-in wallet, which every change must be sent from. */
  wallet: Address
  sample: boolean
  /** Sample mode only (`?pending=add`): an add waiting to be recorded. */
  samplePending?: boolean
}

type DialogAction = Exclude<PhaseAction, 'up' | 'down' | 'hide' | 'show'> | 'add' | 'closeAll'
type OpenDialog = { action: DialogAction; groupId: number; key: number }

/** An off-chain edit's answer, or "Try again" when the action itself threw. */
async function settled(edit: Promise<ActionResult>): Promise<ActionResult> {
  try {
    return await edit
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: THROWN }
  }
}

/**
 * Manage drop, Phases (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.4), on the contract's state: the server
 * reads `collectionState()` and joins each group with its row; status comes only from `active`. Every
 * change on chain runs the manage machine from the sign-in wallet and answers the fresh view, which
 * replaces this one; names, visibility and order are saved by Cobweb. Sample mode runs the same code on the
 * sample actions and server.
 */
export function PhasesScreen({
  collection,
  view: initial,
  wallet,
  sample,
  samplePending = false,
}: PhasesScreenProps) {
  const config = useConfig()
  const [view, setView] = useState(initial)
  const viewRef = useRef(view)
  viewRef.current = view
  // The dialog stays mounted after it closes, so a transaction still confirming can finish.
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<OpenDialog | null>(null)
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<PendingAdd | null>(null)
  const [reading, setReading] = useState(false)
  // Sample mode: what the simulated wallet sent, by hash, so the sample server applies exactly that call.
  const [sampleSent] = useState(() => new Map<Hex, ManageCall>())
  const chain = chainView(view.chainId)
  const now = useChainClock(view.blockTimestamp)
  const reachable = view.chainReachable

  const server = useMemo(
    () => (sample ? samplePhasesServer(() => viewRef.current, sampleSent) : phasesServer(collection.id)),
    [sample, sampleSent, collection.id],
  )
  const target = useMemo<ManageTarget>(
    () => ({
      actions: sample
        ? sampleManageActions(() => viewRef.current, sampleSent)
        : realManageActions(config, { chainId: view.chainId, contract: view.contract, wallet }),
      chainId: view.chainId,
      wallet,
    }),
    [sample, sampleSent, config, view.chainId, view.contract, wallet],
  )

  // An add sent before a reload waits to be recorded: it is checked, never sent again.
  useEffect(() => {
    setPending(
      sample
        ? samplePending
          ? samplePendingAdd(viewRef.current)
          : null
        : pendingAdd(browserStore(), collection.id),
    )
  }, [sample, samplePending, collection.id])

  const live = view.groups.filter((group) => group.active)
  const named = view.groups.filter((group) => group.named !== false)
  const airdropped = Math.max(0, view.totalMinted - view.groups.reduce((sum, group) => sum + group.minted, 0))
  const addOpen = dialog?.action === 'add' && open

  function rememberPending(entry: PendingAdd) {
    if (!sample) rememberAdd(browserStore(), collection.id, entry)
    setPending(entry)
  }

  function forgetPending() {
    if (!sample) forgetAdd(browserStore(), collection.id)
    setPending(null)
  }

  function act(action: PhaseAction | 'add' | 'closeAll', groupId = -1) {
    if (action === 'up' || action === 'down') return void move(groupId, action)
    if (action === 'hide' || action === 'show') return void setVisible(groupId, action === 'show')
    setDialog({ action, groupId, key: Date.now() })
    setOpen(true)
  }

  async function readAgain() {
    setReading(true)
    let result: ActionResult<PhasesView>
    try {
      result = await server.read()
    } catch {
      result = { ok: false, error: THROWN }
    }
    setReading(false)
    if (result.ok) setView(result.data)
    else toast.error(result.error)
  }

  /** Moves a phase one place among the named ones; saved by Cobweb, no transaction. */
  async function move(groupId: number, direction: 'up' | 'down') {
    setView((current) => ({ ...current, groups: moveGroup(current.groups, groupId, direction) }))
    const result = await settled(server.move(groupId, direction))
    if (result.ok) {
      toast.success('Order saved. The mint page lists the phases in this order.')
      return
    }
    setView((current) => ({
      ...current,
      groups: moveGroup(current.groups, groupId, direction === 'up' ? 'down' : 'up'),
    }))
    toast.error(result.error)
  }

  async function setVisible(groupId: number, visible: boolean) {
    const result = await settled(server.setVisibility(groupId, visible))
    if (!result.ok) return void toast.error(result.error)
    setView((current) => ({
      ...current,
      groups: current.groups.map((group) => (group.id === groupId ? { ...group, visible } : group)),
    }))
    toast.success(visible ? 'Shown on the mint page' : 'Hidden from the mint page')
  }

  /** Edit details saved: a named phase changes in place; a newly named one takes a position, so read again. */
  function saved(target: MintGroup, details: { name: string; description: string }) {
    if (target.named === false) return void readAgain()
    setView((current) => ({
      ...current,
      groups: current.groups.map((group) => (group.id === target.id ? { ...group, ...details } : group)),
    }))
  }

  const ctx: PhaseContext = {
    chain,
    groups: view.groups,
    maxSupply: view.maxSupply,
    airdropped,
    blockTimestamp: view.blockTimestamp,
    open,
    onOpenChange: setOpen,
    server,
  }
  const dialogTarget = dialog ? view.groups.find((group) => group.id === dialog.groupId) : undefined

  return (
    <ManageActionsProvider value={target}>
      <div className="grid gap-8">
        {reachable ? (
          <div className="text-muted-foreground -mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-sm">
            <span>Read from the chain {relativeTo(view.readAt, now)}</span>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => void readAgain()} disabled={reading}>
              {reading ? <Spinner /> : <RefreshCwIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
              Read again
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Alert variant="destructive">
            <TriangleAlertIcon aria-hidden="true" />
            <AlertTitle>The contract could not be read</AlertTitle>
            <AlertDescription>
              <p>
                These are the prices, allocations and starts Cobweb last read, on {formatUtc(view.readAt)}.
                Status and minted counts are not shown, and changes on chain wait until it can be read.
              </p>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => void readAgain()} disabled={reading}>
                {reading ? <Spinner /> : <RefreshCwIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
                Read again
              </Button>
            </AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}

        <Panel
          title="Supply"
          description="How the collection's supply is split between phases. Select a phase to change its allocation."
          bodyClassName="p-5 lg:p-6"
        >
          <SupplyMap
            groups={view.groups}
            maxSupply={view.maxSupply}
            totalMinted={view.totalMinted}
            onEdit={reachable ? (groupId) => act('supply', groupId) : undefined}
          />
        </Panel>

        <section aria-labelledby="phases-heading" className="grid gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h2 id="phases-heading" className="text-heading">
                Phases
              </h2>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground max-w-2xl text-sm">
                {live.length > 1
                  ? `${live.length} phases are live at once. Each stays open until you close it.`
                  : 'Phases do not end by themselves. Each stays open until you close it, and several can be open at the same time.'}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {live.length > 0 && (
                <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => act('closeAll')} disabled={!reachable}>
                  Close all
                </Button>
              )}
              {!pending && (
                <Button
                  onClick={() => act('add')}
                  disabled={!reachable}
                  title={reachable ? undefined : UNREADABLE_REASON}
                >
                  <PlusIcon aria-hidden="true" />
                  Add phase
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {pending && !addOpen && (
            <PendingAddPanel
              pending={pending}
              chain={chain}
              server={server}
              onView={setView}
              onForget={forgetPending}
            />
          )}

          {view.groups.length === 0 ? (
            <Empty className="border">
              <EmptyHeader>
                <EmptyMedia variant="icon">
                  <LayersIcon />
                </EmptyMedia>
                <EmptyTitle>No phases yet</EmptyTitle>
                <EmptyDescription>
                  Add a phase to set a price and an allocation, then open or schedule it.
                </EmptyDescription>
              </EmptyHeader>
              <EmptyContent>
                <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => act('add')} disabled={!reachable || !!pending}>
                  Add phase
                </Button>
              </EmptyContent>
            </Empty>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-card shadow-raised @container overflow-hidden rounded-xl border">
              <div
                aria-hidden="true"
                className={cn(
                  'text-muted-foreground hidden gap-x-4 border-b px-6 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium @4xl:grid',
                  PHASE_COLUMNS,
                )}
              >
                <span>Phase</span>
                <span>Price</span>
                <span>Minted of allocation</span>
                <span>Per wallet</span>
                <span>Status</span>
                <span />
              </div>
              <ul className="divide-y" aria-label="Phases">
                {view.groups.map((group) => (
                  <PhaseRow
                    key={group.id}
                    group={group}
                    chain={chain}
                    now={now}
                    publicCap={view.publicMaxPerWallet}
                    reachable={reachable}
                    first={named[0]?.id === group.id}
                    last={named[named.length - 1]?.id === group.id}
                    onAction={(action) => act(action, group.id)}
                  />
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}

          <p className="text-muted-foreground flex gap-2 text-[0.8125rem]">
            <InfoIcon aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 size-3.5 shrink-0" />
            Live means the contract says the phase is open right now. A scheduled phase turns live on its own
            at its start time, as soon as the chain reports it.
          </p>
        </section>

        {dialog && (
          <PhaseDialog
            key={dialog.key}
            dialog={dialog}
            target={dialogTarget}
            ctx={ctx}
            publicCap={view.publicMaxPerWallet}
            onView={setView}
            onSaved={saved}
            onRemember={rememberPending}
            onForget={forgetPending}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </ManageActionsProvider>
  )
}

function PhaseDialog({
  dialog,
  target,
  ctx,
  publicCap,
  onView,
  onSaved,
  onRemember,
  onForget,
}: {
  dialog: OpenDialog
  target: MintGroup | undefined
  ctx: PhaseContext
  publicCap: number
  onView: (view: PhasesView) => void
  onSaved: (target: MintGroup, details: { name: string; description: string }) => void
  onRemember: (pending: PendingAdd) => void
  onForget: () => void
}) {
  if (dialog.action === 'add')
    return <AddPhaseDialog {...ctx} onView={onView} onRemember={onRemember} onForget={onForget} />
  if (dialog.action === 'closeAll') return <CloseAllDialog {...ctx} onView={onView} />
  if (!target) return null
  switch (dialog.action) {
    case 'open':
    case 'close':
      return <StatusDialog {...ctx} group={target} nextStatus={dialog.action === 'open'} onView={onView} />
    case 'schedule':
      return <ScheduleDialog {...ctx} group={target} onView={onView} />
    case 'clear':
      return <ClearScheduleDialog {...ctx} group={target} onView={onView} />
    case 'price':
      return <PriceDialog {...ctx} group={target} onView={onView} />
    case 'supply':
      return <SupplyDialog {...ctx} group={target} onView={onView} />
    case 'cap':
      return <PublicCapDialog {...ctx} cap={publicCap} onView={onView} />
    case 'details':
      return (
        <PhaseDetailsDialog
          open={ctx.open}
          onOpenChange={ctx.onOpenChange}
          group={target}
          others={ctx.groups
            .filter((group) => group.id !== target.id && group.named !== false)
            .map((group) => group.name)}
          server={ctx.server}
          onSaved={(details) => onSaved(target, details)}
        />
      )
  }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/components/manage/manage-unavailable.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { RefreshCwIcon, TriangleAlertIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { useTransition } from 'react'
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@cobweb/ui/components/alert'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import { Spinner } from '@cobweb/ui/components/spinner'

/**
 * A Manage page whose server read was refused (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.4): the
 * sentence, and "Try again", which renders the page again on the server.
 */
export function ManageUnavailable({ title, message }: { title: string; message: string }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition()
  return (
    <Alert variant="destructive">
      <TriangleAlertIcon aria-hidden="true" />
      <AlertTitle>{title}</AlertTitle>
      <AlertDescription>
        <p>{message}</p>
        <Button
          variant="secondary"
          size="sm"
          disabled={pending}
          onClick={() => startTransition(() => router.refresh())}
        >
          {pending ? <Spinner /> : <RefreshCwIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
          Try again
        </Button>
      </AlertDescription>
    </Alert>
  )
}
```

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/app/collections/[id]/manage/phases/page.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { getAddress } from 'viem'
import { getPhases } from '@/app/actions/phases'
import { ManageUnavailable } from '@/components/manage/manage-unavailable'
import { samplePhasesView } from '@/components/manage/manage-sim'
import { PhasesScreen } from '@/components/manage/phases-screen'
import { StagePage } from '@/components/shell/stage-page'
import { requireOwnedCollection, requireUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import type { PhasesView } from '@/lib/manage/phases-types'
import {
  LockedStagePage,
  searchParam,
  stageContext,
  type CollectionParams,
  type SearchParams,
} from '../../_lib/stage'

const TITLE = 'Phases'
const DESCRIPTION =
  'The public sale and whitelist phases: price, supply and when each opens. A phase stays open until you close it.'

/**
 * Manage drop, Phases (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.4): the contract's groups, read on the
 * server and joined with Cobweb's rows. Sample mode only (`NEXT_PUBLIC_DEMO_DATA=1`): the sample phases on
 * any collection, `?chain=unreachable` shows the copy with its alert, `?pending=add` an add waiting to be
 * recorded.
 */
export default async function Page({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: CollectionParams
  searchParams: SearchParams
}) {
  const { id } = await params
  const collection = await requireOwnedCollection(id)
  const user = await requireUser()
  const ctx = stageContext(collection, 'manage', 'manage')
  if (ctx.access === 'locked') return <LockedStagePage collection={collection} stage="manage" />

  let view: PhasesView
  if (ctx.demo) {
    view = samplePhasesView(collection, (await searchParam(searchParams, 'chain')) !== 'unreachable')
  } else {
    const result = await getPhases(collection.id)
    if (!result.ok)
      return (
        <StagePage title={TITLE} description={DESCRIPTION} width="wide">
          <ManageUnavailable title="The phases could not be read" message={result.error} />
        </StagePage>
      )
    view = result.data
  }

  return (
    <StagePage title={TITLE} description={DESCRIPTION} width="wide">
      <PhasesScreen
        collection={collection}
        view={view}
        wallet={getAddress(user.walletAddress)}
        sample={ctx.demo}
        samplePending={ctx.demo && (await searchParam(searchParams, 'pending')) === 'add'}
      />
    </StagePage>
  )
}
```

Replace the whole content of `apps/web/src/app/collections/[id]/manage/page.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { getDeployment } from '@/app/actions/deploy'
import { getPhases } from '@/app/actions/phases'
import { ManageUnavailable } from '@/components/manage/manage-unavailable'
import { OverviewScreen } from '@/components/manage/overview-screen'
import { StagePage } from '@/components/shell/stage-page'
import { NOT_DEPLOYED } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { requireOwnedCollection } from '@/lib/auth'
import { chainView } from '@/lib/chain/chain-view'
import {
  DEMO_ACTIVITY,
  DEMO_COLLECTION,
  DEMO_DEFERRED_RENDER_JOBS,
  DEMO_DEPLOYMENT,
  DEMO_NOW,
  getChain,
  sampleSupply,
  scaleSampleToSupply,
} from '@/lib/demo'
import {
  LockedStagePage,
  searchParam,
  stageContext,
  type CollectionParams,
  type SearchParams,
} from '../_lib/stage'

const TITLE = 'Overview'
const DESCRIPTION =
  'How the drop is doing: supply minted, the contract, the mint page, live phases and proceeds.'

/**
 * Manage drop, overview (docs/specs/2026-10-15-phases.md, section 7.5). The header's chain, contract and
 * deploy time come from the deployment record; minted and the live phases from the server's read of the
 * chain. The pointer and Reveal, holders, the mint page bar's published state, proceeds and recent mints
 * keep their sample values until their sub-projects. In sample mode `?art=deferred` shows the deferred-art
 * scenario, whose real set is not rendered yet.
 */
export default async function Page({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: CollectionParams
  searchParams: SearchParams
}) {
  const { id } = await params
  const collection = await requireOwnedCollection(id)
  const ctx = stageContext(collection, 'manage', 'manage', { art: await searchParam(searchParams, 'art') })
  if (ctx.access === 'locked') return <LockedStagePage collection={collection} stage="manage" />
  // Seam: the real set is rendered when the collection's finished render job is not the placeholder's (no placeholder image CID).
  const realArtRendered = !ctx.artDeferred || DEMO_DEFERRED_RENDER_JOBS.done.placeholderImageCid === null

  if (ctx.demo) {
    const maxSupply = sampleSupply(collection.max_supply, DEMO_COLLECTION.maxSupply)
    const sample = scaleSampleToSupply(maxSupply)
    return (
      <StagePage title={TITLE} description={DESCRIPTION} width="wide">
        <OverviewScreen
          // Manage drop is the deployed collection's home; sample mode opens it before deploy, so the
          // mint page link reads as a deployed one there too (no reservation wording).
          collection={{ ...collection, status: 'deployed' }}
          deployment={DEMO_DEPLOYMENT}
          chain={getChain(DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.chainId)!}
          groups={sample.groups}
          activity={DEMO_ACTIVITY}
          totalMinted={sample.totalMinted}
          maxSupply={maxSupply}
          readAt={DEMO_NOW}
          chainReachable
          slug={collection.slug}
          realArtRendered={realArtRendered}
        />
      </StagePage>
    )
  }

  const [record, phases] = await Promise.all([getDeployment(collection.id), getPhases(collection.id)])
  if (!record.ok || !record.data || !phases.ok) {
    const message = !record.ok ? record.error : !phases.ok ? phases.error : NOT_DEPLOYED
    return (
      <StagePage title={TITLE} description={DESCRIPTION} width="wide">
        <ManageUnavailable title="The drop could not be read" message={message} />
      </StagePage>
    )
  }
  const view = phases.data
  return (
    <StagePage title={TITLE} description={DESCRIPTION} width="wide">
      <OverviewScreen
        collection={collection}
        // Seam: the pointer (base URI, revealed), fees, royalty and payout stay sample until the settings sub-project.
        deployment={{
          ...DEMO_DEPLOYMENT,
          chainId: record.data.chainId,
          contractAddress: record.data.contractAddress,
          txHash: record.data.txHash,
          deployer: record.data.deployer,
          deployedAt: record.data.deployedAt,
        }}
        chain={chainView(view.chainId)}
        groups={view.groups}
        activity={DEMO_ACTIVITY}
        totalMinted={view.chainReachable ? view.totalMinted : null}
        maxSupply={view.maxSupply}
        readAt={view.readAt}
        chainReachable={view.chainReachable}
        slug={collection.slug}
        realArtRendered={realArtRendered}
      />
    </StagePage>
  )
}
```

With the dev server (`pnpm --filter @cobweb/web dev`) on local Supabase and Anvil and the dev wallet signed in, walk these and note what each screen showed:

1. A collection deployed on the current Anvil (if Anvil restarted since it was deployed, deploy a fresh one: lock and pay in Lock and upload, `pnpm --filter @cobweb/web render:local <collectionId>`, then Deploy). Phases reads "Read from the chain under a minute ago" with "Read again"; its launch phases read Closed, minted 0 of their allocation, with the public limit in Per wallet.
2. Open now on Public: the dialog runs "Checking the change", "Confirm in your wallet" (sign in the dev wallet), "Confirming on Anvil" and "Reading the contract", then the toast "Public is open" and the row reads Live from the server's answer. Close it again: Closed.
3. Schedule a phase a minute ahead: the dialog shows the time in your zone, UTC, the Unix value and "From the chain's time"; the row reads Scheduled, "Opens in 1 minute". After the minute, "Read again": the row reads Live (from the contract's `active`, with the flag off). Clear schedule, then Close.
4. Edit price and Edit per-wallet limit: the review lists `newMintPrice` as the native amount and its wei (price first, then `mintGroup`) and `newPublicMaxMintPerWallet`; the row shows the new values after "Reading the contract". Edit max supply above the room: the dialog shows the room error and Change max supply stays off.
5. Add phase: the dialog reads the next id from the contract and the review shows it as `mintGroup`; fill a name, a description and turn "Show on the mint page" off; after signing, the new phase appears last, named, described and Hidden.
6. Add another phase and reload the page while it shows "Confirming" or "Reading the contract": the panel "A new phase is waiting to be recorded" shows its name, group id and transaction, with "Check again" and "Discard"; "Check again" records it and the panel goes.
7. From a row's menu: Edit details (rename, change the description), "Hide from the mint page" and "Show on the mint page" (not offered for Public), Move up and Move down. Reload: every edit is still there.
8. Close all (shown while a phase is live): every phase reads Closed.
9. The Overview: the header shows Anvil, the contract address, "deployed <relative>", "Minted N / M" from the chain; Live now lists the live phases (or "Nothing live").
10. Stop Anvil (end `chain:local`) and reload Phases: the alert "The contract could not be read. These are the prices, allocations and starts Cobweb last read, on <UTC>. ..." with "Read again"; status and minted read "Not read"; Open now, the on-chain menu items, Add phase and Close all are off with the reason; renaming, hiding and moving still save. The Overview reads Minted "Not read" and Live now "The contract could not be read, so live phases are not shown.". Start `chain:local` again afterwards (the earlier contracts are gone from the fresh chain; their pages keep showing the copy).
11. Sample mode (`NEXT_PUBLIC_DEMO_DATA=1` in `apps/web/.env.local`, then restart the dev server): Phases shows the sample (Public scheduled, Owl List live, Team hidden); a dialog runs "Checking the change" then the simulated wallet (Approve, Reject, Approve then revert each give their outcome), and an allocation below a phase's minted count reads "It cannot go below 186, the tokens this phase has already minted. Nothing was sent."; `?chain=unreachable` shows the copy with the alert; `?pending=add` shows the waiting panel; the Overview, Whitelist, Airdrop and Settings dialogs still run on the simulated wallet. Remove `NEXT_PUBLIC_DEMO_DATA` afterwards.

```markdown
Phases (second sub-project of four) landed on <date>; see [specs/2026-10-15-phases.md](specs/2026-10-15-phases.md). What exists: `@cobweb/shared/collection-abi` (the collection's ABI alone, which Manage drop's browser code imports); the phases migration (`mint_groups.description`, `visible` and `position`, names of 1 to 40 characters unique in the collection ignoring case, the public sale always shown, the owner's grant narrowed to those four columns, `mint_group_record`, `mint_groups_refresh` with the read's block so a slower read never overwrites a newer one, `mint_group_move`, and `deployments.public_max_per_wallet` as the copy of the contract's limit with `state_block` and `state_read_at`); the server's read of `collectionState()` on each Manage page load and after each confirmed change, joined with the rows (`getPhases`), and the last copy with an alert when the chain does not answer; `confirmPhaseTx` and `recordPhase`, which check the receipt (to the collection, from the sign-in wallet, carrying the expected event) and read the chain again, the added phase's row written only then; `renamePhase`, `setPhaseVisibility` and `movePhase`; the manage machine (switch, check, wallet, confirming, reading the contract) that every on-chain change in Manage drop runs through, with the collection's custom errors in the creator's words; Phases and the Overview's minted total and live phases on real data, Add phase with its pending store and "Check again", sample mode on the same code; the deployed view's launch phases from `deployments.args`; `pnpm --filter @cobweb/web verify:phases` (20 checks against local Supabase and Anvil) on a deploy fixture shared with `verify:deploy`. Next: the whitelist and the holder snapshot (the third sub-project), then airdrop and settings (the fourth). Known: until the freshness server (Phase 6) the screens change only on a read, so a start that passes while the page is open reads Scheduled until the next read; a replaced or cancelled transaction is never matched (a replaced add is discarded before naming its "Group N"); an id or a limit set outside Cobweb above 2,147,483,647 is not copied; the Whitelist, Airdrop and Settings dialogs run on the sample actions until their sub-projects.
```
